Coupling this isochoric result with a simplicial truncation analysis in the Hausdorff topology, we establish a quadratic quantitative stability bound against local polyhedral perturbations. This discrete framework eludes the degenerations of traditional smooth continuous analysis, providing an explicit algebraic resolution for the strict local minimality of this geometric configuration and its topological isolation in the subspace of polytopes.

</details>


### [105] [Extended Kalman Filter-Based State Estimation for a Nine-Compartment Nonlinear Epidemic Model -- Convergence Analysis and In-Silico Benchmark Calibrated on the COVID-19 Third Wave in Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16305)
*Lokman Rachid Melhani,Antonino Sferlazza,Dominique Persano Adorno,Filippo D'Ippolito,Antonino Lo Burgio,Alberto Firenze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对九室非线性COVID-19流行病模型（含双共循环毒株、超级传播者亚群、具有免疫力衰退的疫苗接种、住院和死亡），从三个常规报告观测值（住院人数H、死亡人数F、疫苗接种存量V）重构所有九个状态。理论贡献包括：基于Lie导数的可观测性分析推导出闭式行列式；设计EKF并证明局部指数均方有界性；通过残差标定噪声协方差并进行NEES和创新白度检验。参数失配实验显示直接测量通道在±30%模型误差下仍保持准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为复杂流行病模型提供状态估计的理论基础，特别是当系统存在多毒株、超级传播者、疫苗接种及免疫力衰退等复杂因素时，仅依靠有限观测数据难以直接获取完整状态信息，需要可观测性分析与状态估计算法来支撑后续的模型预测控制。

Method: 1) Lie导数可观测性分析：通过六步推导得到闭式可观测性矩阵行列式，揭示对称参数下的秩亏缺及对称破缺参数的作用；2) EKF设计：基于Euler离散化动力学方程，使用闭式9×9雅可比矩阵和Joseph协方差更新；3) 误差有界性证明：利用Reif-Gunther-Yaz-Unbehauen假设，针对双线性漂移-线性输出系统证明局部指数均方有界性；4) 噪声协方差设计：基于标定残差，通过NEES和白度检验评估。

Result: 可观测性行列式表达式为|det(O9)| = delta_w * gamma_a^2 * kappa * rho2 * w1^2 * (delta_i - delta_p)^2 * |r1 - r2|，在对称参数点(delta_i = delta_p, r1 = r2)秩亏缺，三阶Lie导数恢复满秩9。EKF在合成数据上RMSE为0.07%-2.72%。参数失配±30%下直接测量通道保持准确，间接观测通道性能优雅退化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了复杂流行病模型状态估计的理论框架，为基于状态反馈的模型预测控制奠定了基础；证明了双线性漂移-线性输出系统误差有界性的扩展定理；参数失配实验验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper addresses real-time state estimation for a nine-compartment nonlinear COVID-19 epidemic model with two co-circulating strains, a super-spreader subpopulation, vaccination with waning immunity, hospitalization, and mortality. Time-varying transmission and vaccination rates are known inputs from a companion calibration, leaving the reconstruction of all nine states from three routinely reported observables: hospitalizations H, fatalities F, and vaccinated stock V. The contributions are theoretical rather than in the filter recursion. First, a Lie-derivative observability analysis yields, via a six-step derivation, the closed-form determinant |det(O9)| = delta_w * gamma_a^2 * kappa * rho2 * w1^2 * (delta_i - delta_p)^2 * |r1 - r2|, showing the level-2 codistribution is rank-deficient at the calibrated symmetric parameters (delta_i = delta_p, r1 = r2); the third Lie derivative restores full rank 9, with r2 the symmetry-breaking parameter. Second, an EKF is designed on the Euler-discretized dynamics with a closed-form 9x9 Jacobian and Joseph covariance update. Third, local exponential mean-square boundedness of the error is proved as a full theorem via the Reif-Gunther-Yaz-Unbehauen hypotheses, exploiting the bilinear drift and linear output to obtain a global-radius quadratic remainder bound that extends to bilinear-drift, linear-output systems. Fourth, the noise covariances are designed from calibration residuals and assessed by NEES and innovation-whiteness tests. All experiments use synthetic measurements from the calibrated model, so reported RMSE values (0.07%-2.72%) are methodology benchmarks, not predictive accuracy. A parameter-mismatch study shows measured and directly-coupled channels stay accurate under model error up to +/-30% while indirectly observed states degrade gracefully. The framework provides the state-feedback basis for future Model Predictive Control.

</details>


### [106] [Collective Optimization on Riemannian Manifolds with Bounded Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14884)
*Hui Huang,Dohyun Kim,Hansol Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形上内蕴共识的优化框架，利用流形自身的对数映射和指数映射构建粒子系统，证明了全局适定性和McKean-Vlasov动力学的收敛性，并在球面、双曲空间和特殊正交群上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共识的优化方法大多依赖欧几里得嵌入的外蕴方式处理流形上的优化问题，缺乏直接在黎曼流形上建立内蕴框架的理论与方法。

Method: 利用黎曼流形上的内在测地距离诱导的对数映射和指数映射，直接基于黎曼结构建立共识优化粒子系统及其对应的McKean-Vlasov平均场动力学。

Result: 证明了粒子系统的全局适定性以及平均场方程在适当条件下全局收敛到目标函数的全局极小值点；在球面、双曲空间和特殊正交群上的数值实验表明该内蕴CBO动力学对非凸流形优化问题有效。

Conclusion: 该内蕴框架克服了外蕴方法的局限性，为曲率有界的黎曼流形上的非凸优化问题提供了一种具有理论保证的全局优化方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an intrinsic consensus-based optimization framework on Riemannian manifolds with bounded sectional curvature. In contrast to extrinsic approaches based on an ambient Euclidean embedding, our model is formulated directly in terms of the Riemannian structure, using logarithmic and exponential maps induced by the intrinsic geodesic distance. We prove the global well-posedness of the proposed particle system and its associated McKean--Vlasov dynamics. We also establish the global convergence of the mean-field equation toward a global minimizer of the objective function under suitable conditions. Numerical experiments on the sphere, hyperbolic space, and the special orthogonal group demonstrate the effectiveness of the intrinsic CBO dynamics for nonconvex optimization problems on manifolds.

</details>


### [107] [CVXPY 1.9: Recent Advances in Optimization Modeling Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14891)
*William Zhang,Parth Nobel,Aryaman Jeendgar,Riley Murray,Philipp Schiele,Steven Diamond*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CVXPY 1.1至1.9版本的主要进展，包括统一圆锥二次规划标准形式、参数化程序加速、N维表达式支持、显式稀疏变量、多变量属性、量子信息理论相关锥/原子以及遵循非线性规划（DNLP）的引入，并概述了这些特性的设计、算法和建模影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续改进CVXPY优化求解器，提升表达能力和求解效率，扩展应用领域。

Method: 统一CQP标准形式进行规范化；采用stacked-slices后端加速参数化程序；支持N维表达式和显式稀疏变量；引入量子信息论相关锥/原子；提出DNLP扩展。

Result: 实现了从1.1到1.9版本的多个重大功能升级，增强了CVXPY的表达能力、求解效率和适用范围。

Conclusion: CVXPY通过一系列版本迭代，显著提升了作为凸优化领域特定语言的实用性和前沿性。

Abstract: CVXPY is a Python-embedded domain-specific language for convex optimization that lets users express problems in mathematical notation while the system verifies convexity and reduces valid programs to solver-ready form. This paper reports on the major advances from versions 1.1 through 1.9. These include a unified conic quadratic program (CQP) standard form for canonicalization; a stacked-slices backend that accelerates parameterized programs; first-class support for N-dimensional expressions; explicit sparsity for variables; support for multiple variable attributes; cones/atoms relevant to quantum information theory; and the introduction of disciplined nonlinear programming (DNLP). We outline the design, algorithms, and modeling consequences of these features.

</details>


### [108] [Controller and Control Architecture Co-Design via Mixed-Integer System-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14966)
*Chenchen Zhou,Jose Matias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向动态输出反馈系统的控制器与控制架构协同设计方法。通过有限时域输出反馈系统级综合（OF-SLS），将传感器、执行器、链路的离散选择与延迟以二进制变量编码，构造混合整数凸规划问题。在车辆队列基准测试中，该方法在99/8748个架构满足二次不变性（QI）的条件下，以相同架构成本将非QI架构的性能损失降至最佳QI架构的1/3.8，优于正则化与规范化信息流基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态输出反馈系统的控制器设计与控制架构（传感器、执行器、链路及延迟的选择）通常是耦合的，但现有方法要么固定架构，要么使用正则化，或者将控制器信息模式限制在二次不变性（QI）类中，无法实现真正的协同优化。本文旨在解决这一混合整数非凸优化问题，实现架构与控制器的最优联合设计。

Method: 将有限时域输出反馈系统级综合（OF-SLS）响应作为优化变量，用二进制变量编码传感器、执行器、链路和延迟的选择，通过指示约束在选定延迟之前将不可用的FIR响应块置零。对于实现局部化的OF-SLS架构，在预设的有限延迟菜单上构建精确的混合整数凸规划（MICP），全局求解出给定延迟菜单、FIR时域、可行架构集和标量化权重下的最优架构-响应对。

Result: 在车辆队列基准测试中，8748个架构中仅有99个与QI兼容。在相同架构成本下，所选非QI OF-SLS架构的性能损失仅为最佳QI架构的1/3.8，且优于正则化方法和规范化信息流基线。

Conclusion: OF-SLS方法能够有效突破QI架构的限制，实现控制器与控制架构的全局协同优化，在保持凸性的同时显著提升性能，适用于延迟约束下的网络化控制系统设计。

Abstract: We study controller and control-architecture co-design for dynamic output-feedback systems. The architecture selects active sensors and actuators, sensor-to-actuator links, and link delays, with costs for hardware activation and communication latency. Direct optimization over controller transfer matrices and discrete links is mixed-integer nonconvex; common alternatives fix the architecture, use regularization, or restrict the controller information pattern to a quadratically invariant (QI) class. We instead optimize finite-horizon output-feedback system-level synthesis (OF-SLS) responses. Binary variables select sensors, actuators, links, and delays, and indicator constraints zero unavailable FIR response blocks before the selected delays. For implementation-local OF-SLS architectures, this gives an exact mixed-integer convex program over a prescribed finite delay menu. A global solve certifies the best architecture-response pair for the chosen delay menu, FIR horizon, admissible architecture set, and scalarization weight. The same encoding gives a QI controller-support reference problem. In a vehicle-platoon benchmark, 99 of 8748 architectures are QI-compatible. At equal architecture cost, the selected non-QI OF-SLS architecture reduces performance loss by a factor of 3.8 relative to the best QI architecture and outperforms regularization-based and canonical information-flow baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Real-time nonlinear model predictive control framework for event-triggered switching in industrial batch polymerization process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14976)
*Chenchen Zhou,Zuzhen Ji,Jose Matias*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地将四种实时NMPC技术（平滑模态混合、高级步进热启动、变量缩放和迭代预算上限）结合，解决了间歇聚合反应器的实时控制问题，在工业气液聚合基准测试中相比PID和传统NMPC实现了更好的约束满足和更短的批次时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇聚合反应器控制面临三大挑战：缺乏稳态工作点导致标准线性化方法失效、动力学本质非线性、多相操作引发状态触发切换。现有方法依赖临时切换启发式策略，缺乏系统性的实时可行性保证。

Method: 系统性地组合四种成熟的实时NMPC技术：1) 平滑模态混合（smooth mode blending）；2) 高级步进热启动（advanced-step warm starts）；3) 变量缩放（variable scaling）；4) 迭代预算上限（capped iteration budget）。同时提供平滑增益选择和切换面定位的实践指导，并阐明平滑处理引入的近似误差。

Result: 在工业气液聚合基准测试中，含估计器回路的闭环仿真表明：相比PID和传统NMPC基线，该方法在有限计算资源下实现了更好的约束满足和更短的批次时长。消融实验量化了各技术组件的单独贡献。

Conclusion: 通过适当的调参，平滑后的切换逻辑与原切换逻辑在求解器容差水平上数值上不可区分，表明该方法无需临时切换启发式即可实现实时可行性。

Abstract: Controlling batch polymerization is challenging because the absence of a steady operating point prevents standard linearization; the dynamics are intrinsically nonlinear; and multi-phase operation induces state-triggered switching. This study systematically combines four established real-time NMPC ingredients, smooth mode blending, advanced-step warm starts, variable scaling, and a capped iteration budget, to attain real-time feasibility without ad hoc switching heuristics. We provide practice-oriented guidance for selecting smoothing gains and locating switching surfaces, and we make explicit the approximations introduced by smoothing such that, with appropriate tuning, the smoothed and original switching logic are numerically indistinguishable at solver-tolerance levels. All results are obtained in closed-loop simulation using an industrial gas-liquid polymerization benchmark with estimator-in-the-loop, compared against PID and conventional NMPC baselines. Results show improved constraint satisfaction and shorter batch duration under bounded computation, while an ablation study quantifies the specific contributions of each component individually

</details>


### [110] [A Transformer-Based Mixture-of-Experts Framework for False Data Injection Attack Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15005)
*Ruslan Abdulin,Mohammad Rasoul Narimani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的编码器-only Transformer混合专家框架，用于智能电网中虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）的联合检测与定位。该框架结合拉普拉斯位置编码、扩散偏置自注意力以及基于Chebyshev和ARMA图卷积专家的密集路由混合专家模块，在IEEE 118和300总线系统上实现了93.91%的检测F1分数和84.97%的定位F1分数，优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假数据注入攻击（FDIAs）通过操纵测量数据误导电力系统状态估计，威胁智能电网安全运行。现有数据驱动方法多依赖单一图滤波机制，难以适应多样化的网络攻击模式。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的编码器-only Transformer混合专家框架，包括：(1) 拉普拉斯位置编码和扩散偏置自注意力，用于捕获全局空间依赖；(2) 密集路由混合专家模块，包含Chebyshev和自回归移动平均（ARMA）图卷积专家，根据当前电网状态自适应应用互补的图滤波器。

Result: 在IEEE 118和300总线系统上基于NYISO负荷数据进行评估，检测F1分数达93.91%，定位F1分数达84.97%，优于现有基准方法，且保持较低的误报率。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过拓扑感知的Transformer架构和自适应混合专家机制，有效实现了FDIA的联合检测与定位，对多种攻击模式具有较好的适应性。

Abstract: False data injection attacks (FDIAs) threaten smart-grid operation by manipulating measurement data and misleading power system state estimation. Although recent data-driven methods have shown promising performance, many existing approaches rely on a single graph-filtering mechanism and therefore struggle to adapt to diverse cyberattack patterns. This paper proposes a topology-aware encoder-only transformer mixture-of-experts framework for joint FDIA detection and localization. The proposed model integrates Laplacian positional encoding and diffusion-biased self-attention to capture global spatial dependencies, while a densely routed mixture-of-experts module composed of Chebyshev and autoregressive moving-average graph convolutional experts adaptively applies complementary graph filters based on the current grid state. The framework is evaluated on the IEEE 118- and 300-bus systems using the New York Independent System Operator load profile. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.91% detection F1 score and 84.97% localization F1 score, outperforming existing benchmark methods while maintaining low false-alarm rates.

</details>


### [111] [Revisiting The PBH Test: Fast Uncontrollability Certificates via Krylov Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15181)
*Ahmad F. Taha,Mohamad H. Kazma,Abdallah A. Albustami*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过有限时域可达性重新审视PBH检验，将状态转移建模为最小能量原优化问题，证明不可达状态空间机动存在对偶不可行性证书，该证书无需构建可控性矩阵即可计算，并证明证书是可控性广义特征向量的线性组合，最后提出基于Krylov子空间的方法提取PBH不可控模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PBH检验虽理论优雅，但需要全局特征分解，在大规模动态系统中计算代价高昂。本文旨在提供一种无需显式构造可控性矩阵或进行全局特征分解即可高效认证不可控性的方法。

Method: 将状态转移问题表述为最小能量原优化问题，利用对偶性导出不可行性证书；无需可控性矩阵，通过优化视角直接计算证书；利用Krylov子空间方法提取部分PBH不可控模式。

Result: 证明不可控性证书是广义不可控特征向量的线性组合，可在不进行全局特征分解的情况下获得谱解释；基于Krylov子空间的算法在数千节点的大规模动态网络上表现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提供了一种高效、可扩展的不可控性认证框架，弥合了经典PBH检验与大规模系统实际计算需求之间的差距。

Abstract: This letter revisits the classical PBH test through the lens of finite-horizon reachability. By casting state transfer as a minimum energy, primal optimization problem, we show that unreachable state-space maneuvers admit dual infeasibility certificates. These certificates are computable without forming the controllability matrix meaning that uncontrollability can be efficiently certified. We prove that any such certificate is a linear combination of uncontrollable generalized eigenvectors, thereby providing a spectral interpretation without a global eigendecomposition. We also devise algorithms based on Krylov sub-space methods that extract some of the uncontrollable PBH modes from a certificate and demonstrate favorable scaling on large dynamic networks with thousands of nodes.

</details>


### [112] [A Feasible-Velocity Framework for Local Controllability of Nonlinear Systems with Zero-Excluding Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15195)
*Amal Bouazza,Mohamed Boutayed,Mustapha Oudani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性控制仿射系统在严格排除零输入的状态相关箱型约束下的局部能控性问题，提出了容许平衡集概念来分类参考状态，并建立了能控性的正/负判别准则，通过平面缆索驱动机器人案例验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小时间局部能控性理论及正/锥约束控制框架无法处理严格排除零输入的状态相关箱型约束（如缆索驱动机器人中的正驱动约束），亟需建立新的理论框架来分析此类系统的局部能控性。

Method: 引入容许平衡集（admissible balancing set）对参考状态进行分类；当存在内点容许平衡输入时，通过局部均匀输入平移恢复对称控制系统；当不存在时，利用可行速度集与原点严格分离的性质，构造分离余向量定义局部屏障泛函，证明其沿任意容许轨迹以均匀正速率增长。

Result: 建立了局部能控性的充分条件（存在内点容许平衡输入时适用经典判据）和必要条件（屏障泛函提供的定量障碍）；屏障不排除有限时间可达性，引出了容许偏离（admissible excursions）概念；在欠驱动平面缆索驱动机器人上数值验证了屏障的存在性。

Conclusion: 为严格正驱动约束下的非线性系统提供了新的局部能控性分析框架，区分了小时间局部能控性与有限时间可达性，对缆索机器人等系统的运动规划与控制具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: This paper studies local controllability of nonlinear control-affine systems subject to state-dependent box constraints that strictly exclude the zero input. Such constraints arise naturally in cable-driven robots and other systems with strictly positive actuation, but fall outside classical small-time local controllability theory and existing frameworks for positive or cone-constrained controls. We introduce the admissible balancing set, an input-space object that classifies reference states without requiring the control distribution to have full rank. When an admissible balancing input lies in the interior of the input set, a locally uniform input shift recovers a symmetric-control system, allowing classical accessibility and small-time local controllability criteria to be applied. When no admissible balancing input exists, the feasible-velocity set is strictly separated from the origin. We show that the resulting separating covector defines a local barrier functional that increases at a uniform positive rate along every admissible trajectory, thereby providing a quantitative obstruction to small-time local controllability. This obstruction does not exclude finite-time reachability through trajectories leaving the barrier neighborhood, which motivates the notion of admissible excursions. The framework is illustrated on an underactuated planar cable-driven parallel robot, for which the barrier is certified numerically over a prescribed state neighborhood.

</details>


### [113] [Dual-Network PINNs for Optimal Control: A Reproducible Benchmark on the Mass-Spring-Damper System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15271)
*Abdeladhim Tahimi,Rinaldo Vieira da Silva Junior*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个透明且可复现的基准研究，使用直接双网络物理信息神经网络(PINN)公式求解质量-弹簧-阻尼器系统的最优控制问题，并与庞特里亚金最小原理和直接转录两种经典方法进行比较。PINN方法通过两个前馈神经网络（状态网络和控制网络）将最优控制问题重构为约束优化问题，在基准测试中实现了四位有效数字的最优成本精度，状态和控制误差落在两种经典参考方法的分布范围内，但训练速度比经典打靶法慢约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低PINN应用于最优控制问题的入门门槛，提供清晰、透明且可复现的方法论参考，使没有伴随方法或两点边值问题背景的从业者能够理解和使用PINN进行最优控制。

Method: 采用双网络结构：一个状态网络（通过复合三次函数与掩码的ansatz精确施加边界条件）和一个无约束控制网络。损失函数由配点处的物理残差和代价泛函的梯形近似组成，通过单个标量超参数加权。将最优控制问题重新表述为约束优化问题，使用两个独立的前馈神经网络求解。

Result: PINN方法再现了四位有效数字的最优成本，精确满足终端状态约束，点态状态和控制误差落在两种经典参考方法的分布范围内。但训练速度比经典打靶法慢约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于方法论的清晰性而非新颖性，提供的公式和Google Colab实现旨在降低PINN在最优控制领域的应用门槛，并诚实地报告了计算效率上的局限性。

Abstract: This work presents a transparent and reproducible benchmark study of a direct dual-network Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) formulation for the optimal control of a mass-spring-damper system. The classical linear-quadratic optimal control problem is solved by two independent classical methods -- Pontryagin's Minimum Principle with single shooting, and direct transcription through trapezoidal collocation -- and recast as a constrained optimization problem solved by two feedforward neural networks: a state network whose boundary conditions are enforced exactly through a composite cubic-and-mask ansatz, and an unconstrained control network. The composite loss combines the physics residual at the collocation points with a trapezoidal approximation of the cost functional, weighted by a single scalar hyperparameter. On the benchmark considered, the PINN reproduces the classical optimal cost to four significant digits, satisfies the terminal state constraints exactly by construction, and produces pointwise state and control errors that fall within the spread of the two classical references. Training is approximately two orders of magnitude slower than classical shooting on this benchmark, which is honestly reported. The contribution is methodological clarity rather than methodological novelty: the formulation and the accompanying Google Colab implementation are intended to lower the barrier to entry for practitioners exploring PINN-based optimal control without prior exposure to adjoint methods or two-point boundary value problems.

</details>


### [114] [Inexact Warped Resolvent iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15426)
*Raul T. Marcavillaca,Fernando Roldan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非精确扭曲预解算子的结构单调包含问题求解算法，在相对误差准则下实现，可解释为动态生成割集上的松弛投影方法，统一了多种经典分裂算法框架，并验证了在鞍点问题和CT重建中的计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单调包含问题的求解方法（如Tseng分裂、Chambolle-Pock等）依赖于精确预解算子计算，而实际应用中精确计算代价高昂。本文旨在设计一种统一框架，允许在相对误差准则下使用非精确扭曲预解算子，同时保持算法的收敛性保证。

Method: 1) 提出基于非精确扭曲预解算子的算法，采用相对误差准则；2) 算法几何上等价于动态生成割集上的松弛投影方法；3) 为获得强收敛，引入Haugazeau型投影步骤到半空间交集；4) 在度量次正则性假设下推导线性收敛率；5) 将算法统一到Tseng's FBF、FBHF、Chambolle-Pock、Condat-Vũ等经典框架。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了迭代的弱收敛性；通过Haugazeau型方案实现了强收敛；在度量次正则性条件下推导了线性收敛率；在合成鞍点问题和CT重建上的数值实验证明了计算优势。

Conclusion: 提出的非精确扭曲预解算子算法不仅统一了多种经典单调算子分裂和原始-对偶优化方案，而且在实际问题（如CT重建）中展现出显著的计算效率提升，为非精确计算在单调包含问题中的应用提供了理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: In this paper we aim to solve structured monotone inclusions using inexact warped resolvents evaluated under a relative-error criterion. The resulting algorithms admit a geometric interpretation as relaxed projection methods onto dynamically generated cuts, extending classical projection--proximal and hybrid extragradient proximal frameworks to nonlinear warped resolvent. Under mild assumptions, we establish weak convergence of the iterates. We further derive strong convergence results by incorporating projection steps onto intersections of halfspaces via Haugazeau-type scheme, as well as linear convergence under a metric subregularity assumption. The proposed algorithm provides a unified framework for incorporating inexact resolvent computations into several classical schemes arising in monotone operator theory and primal-dual optimization, such as Tseng's forward-backward-forward splitting, forward-backward-half-forward, Chambolle--Pock, and Condat--Vũ. Finally, we present applications in saddle-point and structured convex minimization problems. Numerical experiments on synthetic saddle-point instances and computed tomography reconstruction demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [115] [Coercivity and Local Convergence of Physical Learning in Linear Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15443)
*Joshua A. McGinnis,Xinbo Li,Yoichiro Mori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次对三种物理学习方法（EP、CL和新提出的AL）在线性电路中的局部收敛性进行了分析，证明了在小扰动极限下EP和AL执行自然损失函数的梯度下降，而CL存在三次修正项。通过建立基于网络拓扑的秩条件（coercivity条件），证明了指数级收敛性，并利用Sard定理说明退化情况是非普遍的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习方法利用系统自身的物理特性处理全局信息传递，但缺乏对这些方法收敛性的严格理论分析。本文旨在为线性电路中的三种物理学习方法提供首次局部收敛性分析，揭示其收敛条件与失败模式。

Method: 针对线性电路，在离散和连续时间的小扰动极限下，对EP、CL和新提出的AL三种方法进行理论分析。通过构建基于网络结构的coercivity条件（矩阵秩条件），证明训练损失指数衰减和参数收敛到解流形。利用Sard定理证明退化情况的非普遍性。

Result: EP和AL执行自然损失函数的梯度下降，CL则有三次修正项。在coercivity条件下，训练损失指数衰减，参数收敛到解流形。通过kite电路示例展示了对称性导致的coercivity常数退化，但Sard定理证明这种退化几乎对所有期望输出都不存在。

Conclusion: 本文为物理学习方法的收敛性提供了理论基础，明确了收敛所需的结构条件，并证明了退化情况是非普遍的，从而增强了这些方法的理论可信度。

Abstract: Physical learning methods train physical networks to perform computational tasks using only local update rules, exploiting the physics of the system to handle the global transfer of information. We provide the first local convergence analysis of three such methods -- Equilibrium Propagation (EP), Coupled Learning (CL), and a new method we call Adjoint Coupled Learning (AL) -- for linear circuits, in the limit of small-nudging for both discrete and continuous time. EP and AL perform gradient descent on a natural loss function, while CL follows modified dynamics with an additional cubic correction. Assuming the existence of a solution, we identify a coercivity condition, expressed as a rank condition on a matrix built from the network's incidence structure, under which the training loss decays exponentially and the parameters converge to the solution manifold. We show that coercivity can fail by exhibiting a kite circuit in which a symmetry causes the coercivity constant to degenerate on the solution manifold, but prove using Sard's theorem that such degeneracies are non-generic: coercivity holds at every point of the solution manifold for almost every choice of desired output.

</details>


### [116] [A Conservation Law for Equilibrium Propagation and Coupled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15444)
*Joshua A. McGinnis,Adam G. Kline,Yoichiro Mori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了物理学习方法（耦合学习和均衡传播）在连续时间、小扰动极限下，可训练参数中存在类质量守恒律，并探讨了该守恒律对线性电路训练动态的影响及实践意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示物理学习方法中隐含的守恒律，从而理解训练动态的约束机制，提升对收敛可靠性的理论认识。

Method: 通过理论推导，证明耦合学习（CL）和均衡传播（EP）在连续时间、小扰动极限下参数空间的类质量守恒性，并在广泛物理设置中验证该定律的普适性，最后在线性电路中分析其对训练动态的约束效果。

Result: 发现了可训练参数中的类质量守恒量，证明该守恒律在多种物理设定下成立，并在线性电路中确保了训练收敛的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该守恒律对物理学习系统的训练动态有重要约束，具有实际应用上的启示意义。

Abstract: In this paper we show that the physical learning methods known as coupled learning (CL) and equilibrium propagation (EP) conserve a mass-like quantity in the trainable parameters in the continuous-time, small-nudging limit. We prove that this conservation holds in a broad range of physically relevant settings. We then show that the conservation law constrains the training dynamics in a way that makes convergence reliable in important settings for linear circuits. We conclude by discussing some practical implications of this conservation law.

</details>


### [117] [Modern Primal-Dual Frameworks for Prior-Free Online Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15501)
*Rad Niazadeh,Rajan Udwani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提供了两个现代原始-对偶框架的教程：基于LP的凸规划框架（通过KKT条件生成对偶证书）和无LP的通用证书系统（用于随机环境下竞争比评估），涵盖在线顶点加权二分匹配、带随机奖励的在线匹配、可重复使用资源、双边品类优化、全页面配置优化、AdWords和带取消成本等在线资源分配模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于线性规划的原始-对偶方法是分析在线资源分配问题的基本工具，但标准LP松弛在处理随机结果时可能较弱或难以处理，需要发展新的分析框架来应对现代运营研究中的复杂模型。

Method: 第一部分提出基于LP的凸规划框架，通过在每个到达时刻求解正则化凸规划来平衡贪婪与对冲，并利用KKT条件提供对偶证书；第二部分提出无LP的通用证书系统，用于评估随机不确定性下的竞争比。

Result: 该教程覆盖了在线顶点加权二分匹配、带自由处置的边加权在线匹配、随机奖励在线匹配、可重复使用资源、双边品类优化、配置分配（全页面优化）、AdWords和带取消成本等多种模型，提供了通用的证明模板。

Conclusion: 这些现代原始-对偶框架为读者提供了多功能的证明模板，既能分析现有算法，也能为新兴应用开发新解决方案。

Abstract: Linear-programming (LP)-based primal-dual methods are fundamental for designing and analyzing algorithms in adversarial (prior-free) online resource allocation. This chapter provides a tutorial on two modern primal-dual frameworks, emphasizing recent developments and contemporary models in operations research. Part~I develops an LP-based convex-programming framework where solving a regularized convex program at each arrival captures the tradeoff between greediness and hedging, yielding a dual certificate via Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. Because standard LP relaxations can be weak or intractable for stochastic outcomes, Part~II introduces a complementary LP-free framework that provides a universal certificate system for evaluating competitive ratios under such uncertainty. Covering a wide array of models -- including online vertex-weighted bipartite matching, edge-weighted online matching with free disposal, online matching with stochastic rewards, reusable resources, two-sided assortment optimization, configuration allocation (whole-page optimization), AdWords, and costly cancellations -- the tutorial equips readers with versatile proof templates to analyze existing algorithms and develop new solutions for emerging applications.

</details>


### [118] [Service-Induced Congestion in Memory-Constrained LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15555)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jing Dong,Gan Luo,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了大语言模型服务中受内存约束的离散时间动力学模型，分析了在连续批处理下请求接纳、KV缓存增长与驱逐机制。研究发现：同质负载下，无驱逐均衡不稳定，系统收敛至具有50%吞吐损失的极限环；异质负载下，共素化解码长度可稳定无驱逐均衡，非共素长度则引发失稳。提出了服务诱导拥塞的结构性失稳机制及调度设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中每个请求的KV缓存会随生成token持续增长，在高并发下聚合内存使用随时间内生增长，导致容量超限时系统驱逐活动请求、丢弃缓存状态并重启，造成计算浪费和吞吐降低。现有研究缺乏对这一内存约束下动力学行为的理论刻画。

Method: 建立离散时间动力系统模型，涵盖请求接纳、内存增长和连续批处理下的驱逐机制。通过不动点分析和极限环分析研究系统稳定性，利用Lebesgue测度论和数论（互质/非互质解码长度）分析收敛行为。

Result: 同质负载下无驱逐均衡不稳定，系统收敛至唯一最坏情况极限环，吞吐损失高达50%（除测度为零的精确捕获集）。异质负载下推导了两类公共输入稳定性判据，发现互质解码长度可稳定均衡，非互质长度产生同步模式导致失稳。

Conclusion: 识别出服务诱导拥塞（service-induced congestion）作为结构性失稳机制，提出通过负载异质性和解码长度的互质化设计来稳定内存受限服务的调度原则，以维持高吞吐。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM) serving, each request accumulates persistent graphics processing unit (GPU) memory during service as its key-value cache grows with every generated token. Under high concurrency, aggregate memory usage therefore increases endogenously over time: the service process itself creates future capacity pressure. When memory capacity is exceeded, systems evict active requests, discarding cached state and restarting them later, which wastes computation and reduces throughput. We develop a discrete-time dynamical model of memory-constrained LLM inference that captures admission, memory growth, and eviction under continuous batching. In the saturated-input regime, the system admits both eviction-free fixed points and limit cycles with evictions. For homogeneous workloads, we show that the eviction-free equilibrium is unstable and that, except for a Lebesgue-measure-zero exact-capture set, the system converges to a unique worst-case limit cycle that is asymptotically stable outside this exceptional set, with throughput losses as large as 50%. For heterogeneous workloads, we prove a stability criterion in the two-class common-input setting and explain how the survival-polynomial mechanism generalizes to multiple classes and heterogeneous-input lengths. Under an input-dominated scaling regime, coprime decoding lengths stabilize the eviction-free equilibrium, while non-coprime lengths create synchronized modes that drive instability. These results characterize when workload heterogeneity desynchronizes completions and helps stabilize memory-constrained serving. More broadly, we identify service-induced congestion as a structural instability mechanism and derive scheduling design principles for sustaining high throughput.

</details>


### [119] [Schattor: Schatten-family methods for deep learning optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15702)
*Bohao Ma,Junyu Zhang,Chuan He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Schatten范数的自适应一阶优化方法族Schattor，统一了SGD和矩阵自适应优化器Muon，并在随机矩阵优化问题上证明了无维度平稳性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习优化面临异构参数结构、噪声梯度和高度非凸景观的挑战，SGD存在局限性，而自适应优化器虽成功但缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 基于Schatten范数构造自适应一阶方法族Schattor，通过新颖的矩阵鞅矩界建立无维度平稳性保证，并开发多块扩展以自适应平衡块间优化进度。

Result: 在随机矩阵优化问题上证明了Schattor族方法的无维度平稳性保证，并推广至更一般的多块设置。

Conclusion: Schattor统一了SGD与Muon，提供了一个兼具理论保证与实际效能的矩阵优化框架。

Abstract: Modern deep learning optimization features heterogeneous parameter structures, noisy gradients, and highly nonconvex landscapes, posing significant challenges for both algorithm design and theoretical analysis. Motivated by the limitations of SGD and the success of adaptive optimizers, we propose {\it Schattor}, a family of adaptive first-order methods based on Schatten norms. Schattor unifies SGD and the recently proposed matrix-variate adaptive optimizer Muon within a single Schatten-norm-based framework. We establish dimension-free stationarity guarantees for methods in the Schattor family for stochastic matrix optimization problems via a novel matrix martingale moment bound. We also develop multi-block extensions that adaptively balance block-wise optimization progress and prove dimension-free stationarity guarantees in this more general setting.

</details>


### [120] [Multi-Stage Stochastic Optimization and Reinforcement Learning Approaches for Dynamic Inspection of Infrastructure Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15840)
*Juan-Alberto Estrada-Garcia,Siqian Shen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基础设施系统中多车辆路径调度与动态检测问题，提出了基于随机混合整数规划（SDDiP）和强化学习两种方法，揭示了最优性与可扩展性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础设施系统中的组件面临异构随机故障，且故障时间受先前检测行为和故障传播动力学的内生不确定性影响，需要同时优化路径、调度和决策相关的可靠性动态。

Method: 建立多阶段随机混合整数规划模型；开发了随机对偶动态整数规划（SDDiP）算法，结合对偶近似、整数状态约简和基于采样的前向模拟；并提出了强化学习框架，通过学习任务聚类结构和路径策略来应对大规模问题。

Result: 数值实验表明SDDiP能产生高质量解但可扩展性受限，而基于学习的方法取得了强可扩展性和竞争性能，呈现最优性与可扩展性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了多阶段随机路径问题中内生不确定性的建模，桥接了随机规划与学习方法，为实际中何时选用优化方法还是学习方法提供了指导，支持关键基础设施系统的风险感知检测资源分配。

Abstract: We study a dynamic inspection problem for infrastructure systems in which multiple vehicles are routed and scheduled to monitor components subject to heterogeneous and stochastic failures. A key challenge is endogenous uncertainty in which failure-time realizations are filtered through prior inspection actions and failure-propagation dynamics. This yields a multi-stage decision problem that integrates routing, scheduling, and decision-dependent reliability dynamics over time. We formulate the problem as a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes routing and scheduling decisions under endogenous uncertainty. We develop a stochastic dual dynamic integer programming (SDDiP) algorithm that integrates dual approximation, integer state reduction, and sampling-based forward simulation to approximate cost-to-go functions. In parallel, we propose a reinforcement learning framework that learns job clustering structures and routing policies through interaction with simulated system dynamics and failure processes. Numerical experiments on infrastructure networks with diverse topologies and failure patterns show that SDDiP yields high-quality solutions but faces scalability limitations, while the learning-based approach achieves strong scalability with competitive performance, highlighting a trade-off between optimality and tractability. This work advances the modeling of endogenous uncertainty in multi-stage stochastic routing problems and bridges stochastic programming with learning-based approaches. The results provide guidance on when to deploy optimization-based versus learning-based methods, enabling more effective and scalable inspection planning in practice. More broadly, the framework supports risk-aware allocation of inspection resources and improved reliability of critical infrastructure systems.

</details>


### [121] [Output-Feedback Boundary Control of Reaction--Diffusion PDEs on Arbitrary Lipschitz Domains: A Target-Domain Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16345)
*Rafael Vazquez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种域扩展框架，用于任意有界Lipschitz域（包括非凸和多连通几何）上反应-扩散方程的输出反馈边界镇定。通过将原问题嵌入一个已知镇定设计的靶域（如球或矩形），利用虚拟PDE填充间隙并耦合，实现了物理状态的指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反步法边界镇定设计通常局限于简单几何形状（如矩形、球），难以处理具有任意不规则形状（非凸、多连通）且边界上存在未驱动部分的反应-扩散方程。

Method: 提出域扩展框架：将原域嵌入一个已知镇定设计的靶域（球或矩形），在间隙中引入虚拟PDE副本并通过界面条件耦合，使整个拼接系统演化等同于靶域上的已知闭环系统。物理状态的指数稳定性通过限制到原域得到。输出反馈通过提升靶域观测器实现，观测器由虚拟域中继的共位界面测量驱动。

Result: 证明了物理状态在限制下的指数稳定性；给出了常系数反应-扩散方程在球和矩形域上的封闭形式设计数据；提出了n-球上新的显式Neumann驱动反步法；数值实验验证了星形、马蹄形、多连通域及共享壁腔体上的镇定效果。

Conclusion: 该框架将反步法的应用范围从简单几何扩展到了任意有界Lipschitz域，包括非凸和多连通几何，且离线设计数据可继承自靶域设计，在线计算复杂度与全阶PDE观测器相当。

Abstract: We present a domain-extension framework for output-feedback boundary stabilization of reaction-diffusion equations on arbitrary bounded Lipschitz domains, including non-convex and multiply connected geometries. The plant is posed on an irregular domain whose boundary has actuated and uncontrolled portions. Just as backstepping transforms the plant dynamics into a stable target system, the method embeds the plant in a target domain, such as a ball or a rectangle, where a stabilizing design is already known. Every boundary portion through which the extension proceeds must carry actuation and the complementary collocated measurement. Uncontrolled portions are allowed when they are shared with the target boundary and have the same boundary-condition type. The gap between the two domains is filled with a virtual copy of the plant dynamics, coupled to the plant through interface conditions, and the concatenated state evolves exactly as the known closed loop on the target domain. Well-posedness and exponential stability of the physical state follow by restriction. The offline design data are inherited from the target design and are closed-form for constant-coefficient plants on balls and rectangles. Online simulation of the virtual PDE has the same computational character as a full-order PDE observer, a standard component of output-feedback designs. A new explicit Neumann-actuated backstepping law on n-balls enlarges the available target designs. Output feedback is obtained by lifting the target-domain observer, driven by a collocated interface measurement relayed through the virtual domain. Numerical experiments on star-shaped, horseshoe, and multiply connected domains, with a partitioned plant/controller implementation and a shared-wall cavity, test the designs.

</details>


### [122] [Robust Koopman MPC with Sets Updates for Time Delayed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16485)
*Xinglong Zhang,Xinxin Yao,Xin Xu,Keyou You,Dewen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒自适应Koopman模型预测控制方法，通过在线更新不确定集来解决非线性时滞系统的闭环鲁棒性问题，并在数值实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒Koopman MPC方法依赖预计算的不确定集，但不确定集边界是时变的且依赖于状态与控制，导致保守性过高；同时，尚无Koopman MPC方法处理非线性时滞系统的闭环鲁棒性挑战。

Method: 首先对未知非线性时滞系统进行数据驱动建模，在特征空间中导出提升的时滞Koopman模型；然后分析可控性和可观性等基本属性，设计基于鲁棒管道的MPC算法；进一步提出在线更新不确定集的鲁棒自适应Koopman MPC算法以降低保守性。

Result: 证明了在外生扰动下的闭环鲁棒性以及在标称场景下的渐近收敛性，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 在线更新不确定集的自适应Koopman MPC能有效降低保守性，并为非线性时滞系统提供了可靠的闭环鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: Koopman operators have shown significant potential in designing linear model predictive control (MPC) schemes for nonlinear systems on a lifted observable space. Recent advances have tackled the robust Koopman MPC design issue in the presence of modeling errors, relying on the prior estimation of the modeling uncertainty set. However, deriving a robust positively invariant set using a precalculated uncertainty set can be conservative because the uncertainty set bound is time-varying and dependent on the state and control. Additionally, no existing Koopman MPC design has addressed the closed-loop robustness challenge for nonlinear time delayed systems. Thereby, this article presents a robust adaptive Koopman MPC approach with online updates of uncertainty sets for a class of nonlinear time delayed systems. The unknown nonlinear time delayed system is first modeled in a data-driven manner to derive a lifted time delayed Koopman model in the feature space. By analyzing fundamental properties such as controllability and observability, a robust tube-based MPC algorithm is designed for the time delayed Koopman model. The robust adaptive Koopman MPC algorithm with online updates of the uncertainty sets is then presented to reduce conservatism. Closed-loop robustness under exogenous disturbances and asymptotic convergence in the nominal scenario are proven. Finally, numerical examples verify the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [123] [Testing the max-flow min-cut property and the replication conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16543)
*Ahmad Abdi,Tamás Schwarcz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和SAT求解器验证，证明了对于度数不超过δ的立方体簇以及不超过10个元素的团簇，复制猜想成立。同时提出了一个更紧的权向量上界，将验证MFMC性质所需的检查范围从指数级降低到多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制猜想（Replication Conjecture）如果成立，将对整数规划、组合优化和交换代数等领域产生深远影响。本文旨在验证该猜想对于特定类型的团簇（立方体簇）是否成立。

Method: 1. 证明最小反例的维度不超过δ，从而将搜索空间限定为有限集。2. 提出新的理论结果：验证n个元素上的团簇的MFMC性质只需检查有限个权向量w∈{0,1,...,t}^n，其中t≤max{⌈n/2⌉, ⌊n-√(4n+1)+1⌋}，该上界显著优于之前指数级的界。3. 利用最先进的SAT求解器对度数不超过9的立方体簇和不超过10个元素的团簇进行穷举验证。

Result: 成功验证了复制猜想对于度数不超过9的立方体簇以及不超过10个元素的团簇成立。同时得到了一个更优的权向量上界，将验证MFMC性质的复杂度从指数级降低到多项式级。

Conclusion: 复制猜想在研究的特定范围内成立，所提出的权向量上界有效降低了验证复杂度，为进一步验证更一般情形提供了理论依据和计算基础。

Abstract: The replication conjecture [Conforti and Cornuéjols, 1993] states that every clutter with the packing property has the MFMC property. If true, this conjecture would have far-reaching consequences from integer programming and combinatorial optimization to commutative algebra. In this paper, we set out to verify the conjecture for the cuboid of a set-system in which the Hamming graph induced on the infeasible points has degree at most $δ$.
  The family of cuboids of degree at most $δ$ contains a rich source of clutters with the packing property, including all clutters over a ground set of size at most $δ$. We prove that any minimal counterexample must have dimension at most $δ$, thus making the target search space finite. We then use a state-of-the-art SAT solver to verify the replication conjecture for cuboids of degree at most $9$, and for clutters over at most $10$ elements.
  Our computational verification relies crucially on another theoretical result, that to verify the MFMC property of a clutter over $n$ elements, it suffices to check finitely many weight vectors, namely $w\in \left\{0,1,\ldots,t\right\}^n$ where $t\leq\max\{\lceil n/2\rceil, \lfloor n-\sqrt{4n+1}+1\rfloor\}$. The upper bound of $t$ improves the previous best upper bound by algebraists, which could be exponential in $n$.

</details>


### [124] [Restarted Reflected Halpern Acceleration for Augmented Primal-Dual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16552)
*Benqi Liu,Ju Cao,Wotao Yin,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带光滑项和可近端非光滑项的线性约束复合凸优化问题，提出了统一增广原始-对偶框架，包含多种度量选择（如PDHG型和增广Chambolle-Pock型）。通过退化近端点形式和预条件前向-后向形式，分析了反射Halpern加速在原始-对偶变量中的收敛性，证明了影子迭代收敛到KKT点，给出了非遍历O(1/k)界，并展示了有限识别、局部锐度准则及重启锚点线性收敛等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性约束复合凸优化方法在处理增广项和线性化时缺乏统一框架，且加速方案（如Halpern迭代）的收敛分析在原始-对偶变量中不够直接。本文旨在提供一个统一视角，将PDHG型和增广Chambolle-Pock型方法纳入同一框架，并系统分析其加速收敛性、有限识别和局部收敛行为。

Method: 提出统一增广原始-对偶框架，包含全增广Chambolle-Pock型族（保留增广二次项）和线性化方案。精确格式退化为近端点形式，线性化格式为预条件前向-后向形式。利用反射Halpern加速直接分析原始-对偶变量，通过影子迭代建立KKT收敛性和O(1/k)非遍历界。引入有限识别分析，利用仿射面模型推导精确残差恒等式和局部锐度准则。最后基于固定点锐度证明重启锚点的线性收敛。

Result: 1) 证明影子迭代收敛到KKT点，并给出非遍历O(1/k)收敛界（含标量最坏情况例子）；2) 有限识别发生在影子序列而非锚定Halpern状态中；3) 识别后仿射面模型给出精确残差恒等式和局部锐度准则；4) 在访问重启集上的固定点锐度条件下，证明重启锚点的线性收敛，以及基于局部误差界的局部/尾部收敛；5) 线性规划和凸二次规划实验验证了增广和线性化效果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一增广原始-对偶框架有效连接了PDHG和增广Chambolle-Pock方法，反射Halpern加速在原始-对偶变量中可直接分析。理论结果（O(1/k)界、有限识别、线性收敛）为算法设计提供了坚实保障，实验验证了实际有效性。

Abstract: We study linearly constrained composite convex optimization with a smooth term and a proximable nonsmooth term. We develop a unified augmented primal-dual framework with primal-dual hybrid gradient-type and augmented Chambolle-Pock-type metric choices, including a fully augmented Chambolle-Pock-type family that retains the augmented quadratic term. The exact scheme admits a degenerate proximal-point form; the linearized scheme admits a preconditioned forward-backward form. These representations allow reflected Halpern acceleration to be analyzed directly in primal-dual variables. For the shadow iterates, we prove convergence to Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points and nonergodic O(1/k) bounds for the KKT residual and objective gap, with a scalar worst-case example. We show that finite identification belongs to the shadow sequence rather than to the anchored Halpern state. After identification, an affine-face model yields an exact reduced residual identity and a local-sharpness criterion. Finally, we prove linear convergence of restart anchors under fixed-point sharpness on the visited restart set, with local or tail convergence when sharpness follows from local error bounds. Experiments on linear and convex quadratic programs illustrate augmentation and linearization.

</details>


### [125] [Distributionally robust decision-making under ambiguity: case study of water environmental management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16565)
*Hidekazu Yoshioka,Motoh Tsujimura,Yumi Yoshioka,Ayumi Hashiguchi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合移动平均过程的分布鲁棒随机优化方法，用于解决环境工程中存在的次指数记忆和模型模糊性问题，并通过凸优化与正则化技术实现了高效计算，案例验证了方法在水资源管理中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境决策面临两大挑战：一是环境过程（如径流）呈现次指数长程记忆特性；二是数据不足导致模型识别存在模糊性。现有方法难以同时处理这两类不确定性，亟需一种统一的分布鲁棒优化框架。

Method: 基于混合移动平均（MMA）过程建模环境动态，利用其仿射性质简化优化问题；构建带非光滑条件风险价值（CVaR）的凸目标函数，通过正则化获得光滑严格凸近似；采用散度惩罚刻画模型模糊性，保持问题凸性；最终形成可计算的分布鲁棒随机优化模型。

Result: 通过两个实际案例（参数已识别）验证方法有效性，基于统计仿真评估优化后动态过程的性能表现，表明所提框架能有效处理长程记忆与模型模糊性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了融合随机过程、凸优化与分布鲁棒决策的多学科框架，为环境工程中的不确定性决策提供了理论基础与计算可行的工具，尤其适用于洪水缓解和水力发电等水资源管理场景。

Abstract: Decision-making under uncertainty is ubiquitous in environmental project planning. Environmental processes such as a streamflow discharge often present a subexponential memory, where the autocorrelation persists for a long time. In addition, optimization problems driven by environmental processes encounter the issue of model ambiguity because of a lack of sufficient data for model identification. To facilitate decision-making for the management of aquatic environments (e.g., flood mitigation, water abstraction for hydropower generation), we formulate a unified distributionally robust stochastic optimization problem based on a mixed moving average (MMA) process. The MMA process is a superposition of infinite-dimensional affine stochastic processes that is seemingly complex, but the affine property helps with the formulation and computation of the optimization. Our problem is based on a convex objective with a nonsmooth conditional value-at-risk measure. We present a convergent regularization to obtain its smooth and strictly convex counterpart. The model ambiguity is represented as a distortion of the probability density of the target dynamics, and it is penalized by a divergence with which the optimization problem remains convex and becomes computable. As a case study, we apply the optimization problem to two cases with identified parameter values. The performance of the optimized dynamics is evaluated through a statistical simulation. This paper serves as a multidisciplinary work covering both the theory and application of distributionally robust optimization.

</details>


### [126] [Chebyshev-Exact Acceleration under Hessian Variation, I: Sine-Jacobi Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16671)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky,Martin Takáč*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域一步梯度实现，在Chebyshev极小化极大终端残差条件下分析了时间相关Hessian扰动下的一阶变分增益。结果表明Chebyshev终端多项式不决定一阶Hessian漂移增益，且正弦权重的Jacobi方法具有更优的增益常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限时域下梯度方法的终端残差最优性，以及Hessian扰动对一阶变分增益的影响，探究不同实现方法（Chebyshev vs Jacobi）在终端精确性条件下的增益差异。

Method: 采用Chebyshev极小化极大终端残差框架，通过时间有序谱核分析一阶变分；比较前缀精确Chebyshev递推与终端精确Jacobi方法（正弦权重）的ℓ₂增益，并通过实验验证有限时域效应。

Result: 前缀精确Chebyshev递推的增益A_N^pref = (4/√3) N^{3/2}/(L-μ) ε_N^*(1+o(1))；终端精确Jacobi方法（正弦权重）的增益A_N(J_N^sin) = 2√c_sin N^{3/2}/(L-μ) ε_N^*(1+o(1))，其中2√c_sin≈2.137936 < 4/√3，表明Chebyshev终端多项式不决定一阶Hessian漂移增益。

Conclusion: Chebyshev终端多项式不能唯一确定一阶Hessian漂移增益；正弦权重的Jacobi方法具有更优的增益常数；有限时域效应体现在更低的随机曲率开销、更大的 admissible-block 边界、更准确的时变二次预测以及更低的端点耦合光滑强凸GLM重启成本。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon one-gradient realizations with the Chebyshev minimax terminal residual on $[μ,L]$. Under time-dependent Hessian perturbations, the terminal first variation is governed by a time-ordered spectral kernel $K_s(λ,ν)$; its sharp $\ell_2$ gain is $A_N$. For the prefix-exact Chebyshev recurrence, $$
  A_N^{\rm pref}
  =\frac{ε_N^\star}{L-μ}
  \left(4N^2+16\sum_{m=1}^{N-1}m^2\right)^{1/2}
  =\frac4{\sqrt3}\frac{N^{3/2}}{L-μ}ε_N^\star(1+o(1)), $$ and this is sharp in the causal two-term class with Chebyshev exactness at every prefix. For terminal-only exactness, Jacobi coordinates give $P_N=2^{1-N}T_N$: the spectrum is fixed at the midpoint Chebyshev nodes, while the spectral weights parametrize the realizations. The sine weights give a final-exact Jacobi method with the same terminal residual and $$
  A_N(J_N^{\sin})
  =2\sqrt{c_{\sin}}\frac{N^{3/2}}{L-μ}ε_N^\star(1+o(1)),
  \; 2\sqrt{c_{\sin}}\approx2.137936<4/\sqrt3. $$ Thus the Chebyshev terminal polynomial does not determine the first-order Hessian-drift gain. The experiments show the finite-horizon effect: lower stochastic curvature overhead, larger admissible-block frontiers, accurate time-varying quadratic predictions, and lower restart cost on an endpoint-coupled smooth strongly convex GLM.

</details>


### [127] [Novel insights into Pareto fronts in multiobjective topology optimization and a comparative study of scalarization strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16792)
*Tom De Weer,Ole Sigmund,Gabriele Eichfelder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过理论比较和数值实验，系统研究了多目标拓扑优化中的标量化方法，发现Pareto前沿由局部前沿片段组成，可能导致非凸和不连通的前沿，并证明Pascoletti-Serafini标量化比加权和法与ε-约束法能获得更均匀的Pareto前沿近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化本质上是多目标权衡问题，但多目标优化与拓扑优化两个领域长期共存却交叉有限，现有研究多集中在单目标公式的改进上，缺乏对多目标拓扑优化中Pareto前沿特性和标量化方法的系统理解。

Method: 首先理论比较了加权和法、ε-约束法和Pascoletti-Serafini三种标量化方法；然后通过大量双目标算例（包括体积、柔度、最大应力、动态柔度等目标组合）进行数值对比分析。

Result: 发现拓扑优化中Pareto前沿由局部前沿片段组成，导致前沿可能非凸且不连通；Pascoletti-Serafini标量化能获得更均匀的前沿近似，而加权和法易出现聚类现象。

Conclusion: 本文为拓扑优化与多目标优化之间架起了桥梁，揭示了Pareto前沿的结构特性，并为不同标量化方法在多目标拓扑优化中的适用性提供了指导。

Abstract: Topology optimization produces optimized structures by minimizing an objective function under a set of constraints. While considerable research has focused on improving and extending such single-objective formulations, design is usually a trade-off between conflicting criteria. This brings topology optimization into the realm of multiobjective optimization. Unfortunately, cross-fertilization has been limited despite the long coexistence of these two fields. This work aims to bridge this gap through three contributions. The first is a theoretical comparison of two common scalarization techniques, the weighted-sum and $\varepsilon$-constraint method, to the more advanced Pascoletti-Serafini scalarization. The second contribution is a novel insight: the Pareto frontier in topology optimization contexts consists of pieces belonging to local fronts. This can cause disconnected and nonconvex fronts, in contrast to what is often seen in the literature. Finally, the third contribution is a numerical comparison of the scalarization methods, highlighting more uniform approximations obtained with the Pascoletti-Serafini scalarization and clustering observed for the weighted-sum scalarization. The paper contains a large number of bi-objective examples, including minimization of volume, compliance, maximum stress and dynamic compliance.

</details>


### [128] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Mathematical Programs with Second-Order Cone Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16820)
*Yan-Chao Liang,Chen-Yuan Zhu,Sheng-Jie Zhang,Gui-Hua Lin,Xide Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二阶锥互补约束的数学规划问题（SOCMPCCs），提出了一种定制化的增广拉格朗日方法（ALM）算法框架，证明了其在有界条件下收敛到可行点，并在非退化条件下收敛到K-驻点，数值实验验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOCMPCCs因标准约束规格（如Robinson CQ）在所有可行点处失效，导致经典非线性规划理论与算法难以直接应用，亟需开发针对性的求解方法。

Method: 借鉴增广拉格朗日方法在MPCCs中的成功，将其扩展至SOCMPCCs，设计了定制化的ALM算法框架，并分析了其在有界罚参数或增广拉格朗日函数下的可行性收敛性，以及在SOCMPCC-非退化条件下的K-驻点收敛性。

Result: 在有界ALM罚参数或有界增广拉格朗日函数下，算法序列收敛到SOCMPCC的可行点；在可行性和SOCMPCC-非退化条件下，进一步收敛到K-驻点。数值实验（含高维问题）验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提定制化ALM算法能有效解决SOCMPCCs带来的理论与计算挑战，具备良好的收敛性保障和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper investigates mathematical programs with second-order cone complementarity constraints (SOCMPCCs), which extend classical mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) by incorporating second-order cone structures. SOCMPCCs present significant theoretical and computational challenges, primarily due to the failure of standard constraint qualifications (such as Robinson's constraint qualification) at all feasible points. This difficulty hinders the direct application of classical nonlinear programming theories and algorithms. Motivated by the success of the augmented Lagrangian method (ALM) in solving MPCCs, we explore its extension to SOCMPCCs. The ALM, known for its matrix-free implementation and strong local convergence properties, is well suited for handling the intricate interplay between complementarity and second-order cone constraints. In this paper, we propose a tailored ALM algorithm framework for SOCMPCCs and establish its feasibility and convergence properties. We show that, under bounded ALM penalty parameters or bounded augmented Lagrangian functions, the generated sequence converges to feasible points of the SOCMPCC. Furthermore, under feasibility and additional SOCMPCC-nondegeneracy condition, we prove convergence to K-stationary points, which constitute a fundamental optimality condition for SOCMPCCs. Numerical experiments, including both illustrative examples and high-dimensional problems, are conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed algorithm in addressing the challenges inherent in SOCMPCCs.

</details>


### [129] [Functional Gradient Descent with Adaptive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16926)
*Daniel Csillag,Rodrigo Schuller,Pedro Dall'Antonia,Leonidas Guibas,Luiz Velho,Tiago Novello*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种理论上可证的自适应函数梯度下降算法，通过动态调整函数梯度的表示形式，在光滑损失和PL条件下分别收敛到驻点和全局最小值，且在回归、PDE求解和计算机视觉任务中优于固定近似FGD和神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化方法（如神经网络参数优化）导致高度非凸损失，难以训练和理论分析。函数梯度下降（FGD）虽在函数空间有强收敛性和简洁理论，但实际中函数梯度是无限维的，无法完全计算或存储，现有实现依赖固定近似引入近似误差。

Method: 提出一种自适应表示的自适应FGD算法，在优化过程中动态调整函数梯度的表示形式，并将这种近似显式地纳入理论分析中。

Result: 在光滑损失下收敛到驻点，在光滑+PL条件下收敛到全局最小值，这是首个在一般设置下具有此类保障的可实现FGD方法。在回归、PDE数值求解和现代计算机视觉任务中，该方法在效率和精度上一致优于固定近似FGD和神经网络基线。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在一般环境下为可实现的FGD方法建立了收敛到驻点和全局最小值的理论保证，并通过实验验证了其在多种任务中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Functional optimization problems are typically solved by optimizing the parameters of a fixed representation, such as a neural network, resulting in highly nonconvex losses that complicate both training and theoretical analysis. An interesting alternative is functional gradient descent (FGD), that is, gradient descent directly in function space, which benefits from strong convergence results and admits a clean theory. However, FGD is difficult to implement in practice because functional gradients are infinite-dimensional, and thus cannot be fully computed nor stored in memory. Existing implementations therefore rely on fixed approximations, which introduce approximation error. We propose a new, theoretically-grounded FGD algorithm that adapts the representation of the functional gradients over the course of optimization. By explicitly incorporating this approximation into the analysis, we establish convergence to a stationary point (for smooth losses) and to a global minimizer (under smoothness + a Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type condition) regardless of our approximations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first implementable FGD method with such guarantees in a general setting. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on regression, numerical solution of PDEs, and modern computer vision. Across settings, our method consistently outperforms both FGD with fixed approximations and neural network baselines in efficiency and accuracy.

</details>


### [130] [Exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks: the effect of residual connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17013)
*Vivek S Borkar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用乘法遍历理论分析了深度神经网络中梯度爆炸与消失现象，并解释了残差连接对李雅普诺夫谱的影响，利用Furstenberg-Kifer定理作出了精确的数学表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络训练中梯度爆炸与消失是核心难题，现有解释多停留在直观层面，缺乏严格的数学分析。作者试图通过乘法遍历理论（特别是李雅普诺夫指数）为此现象提供严谨的理论基础。

Method: 运用乘法遍历理论中的李雅普诺夫指数分析梯度传播动力学；利用Furstenberg和Kifer提出的李雅普诺夫特征刻画方法，建立残差连接对李雅普诺夫谱影响的精确数学表述。

Result: 给出了梯度爆炸与消失现象在李雅普诺夫指数框架下的严格数学刻画；明确了残差连接如何影响李雅普诺夫谱，从而缓解梯度问题。

Conclusion: 乘法遍历理论为理解梯度问题提供了强大工具；残差连接通过改变李雅普诺夫谱来稳定梯度传播，该结果从数学上验证了残差网络设计的有效性。

Abstract: The well known phenomenon of exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks is analyzed using multiplicative ergodic theory. The effect of adding a residual connection is explained in this context. Specifically, a characterization of Liapunov exponents due to Furstenberg and Kifer is exploited in order to make a precise statement about the Liapunov spectrum and the effect of residual connections on it.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [A classification of derived-discrete graded algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15274)
*Riku Fushimi,Bohan Xing*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Vossieck关于导出离散代数的分类结果到局部有限非正分次代数（即连通局部有限微分分次代数，平凡微分）的情形，证明了此类分次代数是导出离散的当且仅当它分次Morita等价于Dynkin型的片断遗传代数，或是满足分次钟条件的分次Gentle单圈代数。此外，利用曲面模型证明了Kalck-Yang猜想：分次钟条件在导出等价下不变，并建立了Dynkin型$D$的路代数有界导出范畴上半正交分解的一个限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Vossieck关于有限维代数的导出离散性分类从有限维情形推广到更一般的局部有限非正分次代数（即连通局部有限dg代数，平凡微分），并研究该情形下的导出离散性刻画，同时解决Kalck-Yang关于分次钟条件在导出等价下不变性的猜想。

Method: 利用Broomhead、Pauksztello和Ploog建立的离散三角范畴理论，结合曲面模型方法，分析分次代数的导出离散性。通过分次Morita等价、片断遗传代数、分次Gentle代数等工具进行分类刻画，并验证分次钟条件在导出等价下的不变性。

Result: 1. 分类结果：局部有限非正分次代数是导出离散的当且仅当它分次Morita等价于Dynkin型的片断遗传代数，或是分次Gentle单圈代数且不满足分次钟条件。2. 证明了Kalck-Yang猜想：分次钟条件在导出等价下不变。3. 建立了Dynkin型$D$的路代数有界导出范畴上半正交分解的一个限制结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Vossieck的导出离散代数分类推广到分次情形，并证实了分次钟条件在导出等价下的不变性，深化了对导出离散代数的理解，为相关领域提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A finite-dimensional algebra is derived-discrete, in the sense of Vossieck, precisely when it is a piecewise hereditary algebra of Dynkin type or a gentle one-cycle algebra not satisfying the clock condition. Building on the discrete triangulated categories of Broomhead, Pauksztello, and Ploog, we study derived-discreteness for locally finite non-positively graded algebras, regarded as connective locally finite dg algebras with trivial differential. Our main result extends Vossieck's classification to this setting: such a graded algebra is derived-discrete if and only if it is graded Morita equivalent to a piecewise hereditary algebra of Dynkin type, or it is a graded gentle one-cycle algebra not satisfying the graded clock condition. Along the way, using surface models we prove the conjecture of Kalck and Yang that the graded clock condition is invariant under derived equivalence. We also establish a restriction on semi-orthogonal decompositions of the bounded derived category of a path algebra of Dynkin type $D$.

</details>


### [132] [Shalika Models for Finite General Linear and Unitary Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15299)
*Fang Shi,Zhicheng Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文完全分类了有限一般线性群 GL_{2n}(F_q) 和有限酉群 U_{2n}(F_q) 中具有 Shalika 模型的不可约表示，利用 Lusztig 的 Jordan 分解建立了 Shalika 重数公式，并探讨了与辛模型、线性模型及相对 Langlands 对偶的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对有限一般线性群和酉群中具有 Shalika 模型的不可约表示进行完整的显式分类，并建立重数公式与相关模型和 Langlands 对偶的联系。

Method: 利用 Lusztig 的不可约表示 Jordan 分解，将对偶群中半单元素中心化子的 Weyl 群与 Shalika 重数关联，进而给出分类与重数公式。

Result: 给出了 GL_{2n}(F_q) 和 U_{2n}(F_q) 上具有 Shalika 模型的不可约表示的完全分类，并推导出 Shalika 重数的精确公式。

Conclusion: 建立了 Shalika 模型与辛模型、线性模型及相对 Langlands 对偶之间的深刻联系，为相关表示论问题提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We completely and explicitly classify the irreducible representations of finite general linear groups ${\rm{GL}}_{2\frak{n}}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ and finite unitary groups ${\rm{U}}_{2\frak{n}}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ that admit a Shalika model. Using Lusztig's Jordan decomposition of irreducible representations, we establish a multiplicity formula for Shalika multiplicities in terms of the Weyl group of the centralizer of a semisimple element in the dual group. We also derive connections to symplectic models, linear models, and relative Langlands duality.

</details>


### [133] [Quiver representations and idempotent loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15400)
*Liam Riordan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过添加幂等环路研究箭图表示上的C×作用，构造了模范畴mod Ŵ(Q)，并证明其子范畴Ŵ_T(Q)与通过特征T作用的等变模范畴等价，进而用于研究箭图Grassmannian的欧拉示性数，并建立了与预投射代数、Galois覆盖和cluster特征的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究箭图表示上的C×作用，并建立其与幂等环路的联系，以更好地理解箭图Grassmannian的拓扑不变量（如欧拉示性数）。

Method: 通过添加幂等环路构造模范畴mod Ŵ(Q)，定义子范畴Ŵ_T(Q)实现与等变模范畴的等价；利用该范畴研究箭图Grassmannian的欧拉示性数，联系箭图态射与全子范畴，并将结果应用于预投射代数。

Result: 建立了mod Ŵ(Q)与C×作用模之间的联系；通过Ŵ_T(Q)恢复了箭图Grassmannian欧拉示性数的已知组合描述；建立了箭图态射与全子范畴的直接对应；将表示理论应用于预投射代数，构造了Galois覆盖和cluster特征。

Conclusion: 幂等环路的箭图表示论为理解C×作用、箭图Grassmannian的拓扑不变量以及预投射代数中的Galois覆盖和cluster特征提供了统一且有效的框架。

Abstract: We study $\mathbb{C}^{\times}$-actions on quiver representations by adding idempotent loops. We construct a category mod $\widehat{\operatorname{W}}(\mathrm{Q})$ and relate it to $\mathbb{C}Q$ modules with $\mathbb{C}^{\times}$-actions. We further describe a subcategory $\widehat{\operatorname{W}}_{T}(\mathrm{Q})$ equivalent to the category of equivariant modules where $\mathbb{C}^{\times}$ acts on $\mathbb{C}Q$ via a character $T \in \mathbb{Z}^{Q_1}.$ This allows us to study $\mathbb{C}^{\times}$-actions on quiver Grassmannians and we can recover known combinatorial descriptions of their Euler characteristics via the category mod $\widehat{\operatorname{W}}(\mathrm{Q}).$ We directly relate morphisms of quivers to full subcategories of mod $\widehat{\operatorname{W}}(\mathrm{Q}).$ Finally we show that the representation theory of quivers with idempotents can be applied in a useful way to preprojective algebras. We show that this gives constructions of Galois covers and cluster characters used by Geiss, Leclerc, and Schröer.

</details>


### [134] [Cohen Macaulay modules and positroid varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15401)
*Liam Riordan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文描述了子范畴R(v,w) ⊆ CM(C)，这些子范畴在特定条件下（v属于(W^k\W)^max且w≥v）提升了Leclerc的范畴C_{v,w}，具有Frobenius、稳定2-CY等性质，并诱导了开正虚簇提升中的簇结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Jensen-King-Su提出的范畴CM(C)所范畴化的Grassmannian齐次坐标环上的簇结构推广到更一般的子范畴情形，具体是提升Leclerc的范畴C_{v,w}到更高的代数几何框架中。

Method: 通过定义子范畴R(v,w) ⊆ CM(C)，并在v属于(W^k\W)^max且w≥v的条件下建立与Leclerc范畴C_{v,w}的提升关系。

Result: 这些子范畴是Frobenius范畴、稳定2-CY范畴，具有自然的簇特征，并在开正虚簇的提升中诱导出簇结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立的子范畴推广了已知的范畴化构造，为开正虚簇的簇结构提供了新的范畴化框架。

Abstract: Jensen, King, and Su described a category $\operatorname{CM}(C)$ which categorifies the cluster structure on the homogeneous coordinate ring of a Grassmannian. In this paper we describe subcategories $\operatorname{R}(v,w) \subseteq \operatorname{CM}(C)$ which lift Leclerc's categories $\mathcal{C}_{v,w}$ in the case where $v \in \left(W^{k}\backslash W\right)^{\max}$ and $w \geq v.$ As such, these categories are Frobenius, stably 2-CY, have natural cluster characters, and induce a cluster structure in lifts of open positroid varieties.

</details>


### [135] [Mutations of simple-minded collections revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15537)
*Sota Asai*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了有界导出范畴中简单心收集在任意直和项上的显式突变公式，该公式与完美导出范畴中倾斜复形的突变兼容，并利用该公式从2-项简单心收集的突变角度重新审视了τ-倾斜理论，包括与2-项预倾斜复形的极大/极小完备化以及τ-倾斜约化的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单心收集是有限维代数倾斜理论以及完美导出范畴中倾斜复形的必要工具，但缺乏在任意直和项上突变的显式公式及其与倾斜复形突变的兼容性证明，需要系统化的严谨论证。

Method: 给出了简单心收集在任意直和项上的显式突变公式，并提供了完整证明；然后将该公式应用于τ-倾斜理论，通过2-项简单心收集的突变重新解释相关概念。

Result: 得到了与倾斜复形突变兼容的简单心收集的显式突变公式，并建立了2-项简单心收集突变与2-项预倾斜复形极大/极小完备化、τ-倾斜约化之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该突变公式虽可能为专家所知，但本文提供了完整证明以供参考；同时展示了从简单心收集突变视角统一理解τ-倾斜理论的可能性。

Abstract: Simple-minded collections in the bounded derived category $\mathsf{D}^\mathrm{b}(\operatorname{\mathsf{mod}} A)$ are necessary tools in tilting theory of finite dimensional algebras, as well as silting complexes in the perfect derived category $\mathsf{K}^\mathrm{b}(\operatorname{\mathsf{proj}} A)$. In this paper, we give an explicit mutation formula of simple-minded collections at arbitary direct summands, which is compatible with that of silting complexes. Though this mutation formula may be known to experts, we provide a thorough proof for completeness and future reference. Then we use our formula to revisit $τ$-tilting theory in terms of mutations of 2-term simple-minded collections, including their relationship with maximal/minimal completions of 2-term presilting complexes and $τ$-tilting reduction.

</details>


### [136] [The Coxeter transformation as an automorphism of the Tamarkin--Tsygan calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15595)
*Marco Armenta*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Auslander-Reiten双模在导出Picard群中是中心的，并构造了Tamarkin-Tsygan演算上的典范自同构σ_A，该自同构在导出等价下不变；在Hochschild上同调上σ_A为恒等映射，在Hochschild同调上其特征多项式为Coxeter多项式；该构造可推广到光滑真DG代数与光滑射影簇，其在Calabi-Yau范畴上退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Auslander-Reiten双模在导出Picard群中的中心性，并利用导出不变性函子构造Tamarkin-Tsygan演算上的典范自同构，从而为导出等价提供更精细的不变量，揭示Coxeter多项式、Nakayama自同构等经典对象在此框架下的本质。

Method: 利用Auslander-Reiten双模D_A = D_A[-1]与导出Picard群的关系，通过导出不变性函子HB构造自同构σ_A；在Hochschild同调上通过幂等迹基计算矩阵表示为-C_A^{-1}C_A^T，在Hochschild上同调上证明左右作用一致；推广至光滑真DG代数与射影簇。

Result: (1) AR双模在导出Picard群中中心；(2) σ_A在Hochschild上同调上为恒等映射，由此给出Frobenius代数Nakayama自同构平凡作用于Hochschild上同调的简洁证明；(3) σ_A在Hochschild同调上的特征多项式为Coxeter多项式；(4) 丰富演算严格细化演算与Coxeter多项式（A_4, D_4型路代数），但非完全导出不变量（最小共谱树对）；(5) 推广至光滑真DG代数，在Calabi-Yau范畴上退化，在P^n上由Beilinson代数的Coxeter多项式控制。

Conclusion: 本文建立的丰富Tamarkin-Tsygan演算为导出等价提供了比经典Coxeter多项式更精细的不变量，统一了Happel迹公式、de la Peña分圆性等经典结果，并揭示了Nakayama自同构在Hochschild上同调上平凡作用的深层原因。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finite-dimensional algebra over a field $\kk$. We show that the Auslander--Reiten bimodule $\ar_A:=\D A[-1]$ is central in the derived Picard group of $A$ and that, when $\gldim A<\infty$, it induces through the derived-invariance functor $\HB$ of \cite{Armenta19,ArmentaKeller17,ArmentaKeller19} a canonical automorphism $σ_A$ of the Tamarkin--Tsygan calculus of $A$; the pair $(\HB(A),σ_A)$ is invariant under derived equivalence. We then compute both components of $σ_A$. On the Hochschild homology of an elementary algebra, which is concentrated in degree zero, the matrix of $σ_A$ in the basis of idempotent traces is $-C_A^{-1}C_A^{\mathrm{T}}$, so its characteristic polynomial is the Coxeter polynomial; the enriched calculus strictly refines both the calculus and the Coxeter polynomial, as the path algebras of quivers of types $\mathbb{A}_4$ and $\mathbb{D}_4$ show, although it is not a complete derived invariant, as the smallest cospectral pair of trees shows. On Hochschild cohomology we prove that $σ_A$ is the identity: the left and right actions of $\HH^\bullet(A)$ on the bimodule $\D A$ coincide for every finite-dimensional $A$. This yields a short conceptual proof that the Nakayama automorphism of a Frobenius algebra acts trivially on Hochschild cohomology, recovering a recent theorem of Suárez-Álvarez. Finally we extend the construction to smooth and proper differential graded algebras, hence to perfect derived categories of smooth projective varieties; the enrichment degenerates precisely on Calabi--Yau categories, and on $\PP^n$ it is governed by the Coxeter polynomial $(x+(-1)^n)^{n+1}$ of the Beilinson algebra. Happel's trace formula and de la Peña's cyclotomicity theorem for fractionally Calabi--Yau algebras become statements internal to the enriched calculus.

</details>


### [137] [Noncommutative resolutions of affine Schubert varieties in type A and canonical bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15981)
*Ilya Dumanski,Vasily Krylov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为GL_n的仿射舒伯特簇的退化版本构造了一个非交换的代数层，建立了与Steinberg簇的导出等价，并定义了反常- exotic t-结构。通过将K-理论中不可约对象类与Kazhdan-Lusztig典范基等同，建立了与量子仿射群典范基的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Bezrukavnikov-Mirković在Springer resolution情形下的反常-exotic t-结构构造推广到仿射舒伯特簇的退化版本上，并建立与Kazhdan-Lusztig基及量子群典范基的联系。

Method: 定义仿射舒伯特簇退化版本的非交换代数层；通过导出等价建立与Steinberg簇的联系；利用perversely-exotic t-结构研究等变K-理论中的不可约对象基；借助coherent-constructible等价性质完成证明。

Result: 定义了非交换版本的代数层并建立导出等价；将K-理论中不可约对象类显式识别为某个胞腔商中的(抛物型)Kazhdan-Lusztig典范基；建立了与量子仿射群典范基的联系。

Conclusion: 成功将反常-exotic t-结构构造推广到仿射舒伯特簇退化情形，并建立了与组合表示论中经典基对象的联系，同时证明了coherent-constructible等价的一些性质。

Abstract: Given a resolution $\widetilde{\mathrm{Gr}}^{\underlineλ} \rightarrow \overline{\mathrm{Gr}}^λ$ of an affine Schubert variety for $GL_n$, we define its noncommutative version -- a sheaf of algebras on $\overline{\mathrm{Gr}}^λ$, derived equivalent to $\widetilde{\mathrm{Gr}}^{\underlineλ}$ as well as its Steinberg versions in both zero and positive characteristics. This, in particular, allows us to define the perversely-exotic t-structure on the derived category of equivariant coherent sheaves on $\widetilde{\mathrm{Gr}}^{\underlineλ}$, analogously to Bezrukavnikov--Mirković in the case of Springer resolution. We study the basis of classes of irreducible objects in the equivariant K-theory, and explicitly identify it with the (parabolic) Kazhdan--Lusztig canonical basis in a certain cell quotient. This allows us to relate it to the canonical basis for the quantum affine group. In the course of the proof, we establish some properties of coherent-constructible equivalences.

</details>


### [138] [On reduction and gluing technique of simple-minded systems over self-injective algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16081)
*Zhen Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了自内射代数上稳定模范畴中简单心系统的约化问题，提出了通过子集进行粘合的重构方法，并探讨了简单心系统的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和简化自内射代数上稳定模范畴中简单心系统的结构，发展约化与粘合技术。

Method: 利用重构（recollement）方法，通过简单心系统的子集研究其粘合（gluing）技术，推导约化过程。

Result: 建立了稳定模范畴A-stmod中简单心系统的约化框架，获得了粘合构造方法，并揭示了可扩展性性质。

Conclusion: 本文为自内射代数上简单心系统的约化和构造提供了系统性方法，丰富了稳定模范畴的理论工具。

Abstract: Let A be a self-injective algebra over an algebraically closed field. We study reduction of simple-minded systems over stable module category A-stmod. We present a recollement and study gluing technique of simple-minded systems through a subset of a simple-minded system in A-stmod. As a byproduct, we also study the extendible property of simple-minded systems.

</details>


### [139] [A construction of simple-minded systems over domestic Brauer graph algebras II: the 1-domestic case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16085)
*Zhen Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对1- domestic Brauer图代数A，利用覆盖理论和2-domestic Brauer图代数简单心系统的刻画，在A-stmod中构造了一族对象作为简单心系统，且该构造给出了A-stmod中所有简单心系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画1-domestic Brauer图代数稳定模范畴中所有可能的简单心系统。

Method: 结合覆盖理论与2-domestic Brauer图代数简单心系统的已有刻画结果，进行构造和分类。

Result: 构造了一族对象构成简单心系统，并且证明了该构造涵盖了所有可能的简单心系统。

Conclusion: 给出了1-domestic Brauer图代数稳定模范畴中简单心系统的完整分类。

Abstract: Let A be a 1-domestic Brauer graph algebra. By covering theory and the characterization of simple-minded systems of 2-domestic Brauer graph algebras, we construct a family of objects in A-stmod to be a simple-minded system and our construction provides all simple-minded systems in A-stmod.

</details>


### [140] [Right representations of Novikov algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16431)
*A. S. Panasenko*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Novikov代数的右表示理论，定义了右模理想、本原Novikov代数、表示的拟核等概念，证明了无不可约表示的Novikov代数构成遗传根，并建立了Jacobson根与所有不可约表示拟核之交的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Novikov代数建立系统的表示理论，特别是不可约表示的结构刻画，填补该代数结构在表示论方面的理论空白。

Method: 通过引入右模理想概念，用极大模右理想的商来刻画不可约右表示；通过定义拟核、本原代数等概念，利用遗传根理论分析不可约表示的存在性及Jacobson根的结构。

Result: (1) 无不可约表示的Novikov代数构成一个遗传根；(2) Novikov代数是本原的当且仅当其存在几乎忠实的不可约表示；(3) Jacobson根等于所有不可约表示拟核的交集。

Conclusion: 建立了Novikov代数不可约表示与模理想、拟核、Jacobson根之间的深刻联系，为该类代数的结构理论提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We consider the concept of a right representation for Novikov algebras. We introduce the concept of a right modular ideal of a Novikov algebra and obtain a description of irreducible right representations of a Novikov algebra as quotients by maximal modular right ideals.
  We prove that the class of Novikov algebras without irreducible representations is a hereditary radical. We introduce the concept of a primitive Novikov algebra and prove that a Novikov algebra is primitive if and only if it has an almost faithful irreducible representation. We introduce the concept of the quasi-kernel of a representation as the largest ideal contained in the kernel of the representation. We prove that the Jacobson radical of a Novikov algebra is the intersection of the quasi-kernels of all irreducible representations of this algebra.

</details>


### [141] [Homomorphisms to Hook Specht Modules over Quiver Hecke Algebras of Type C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16499)
*Martín Forsberg Conde,Berta Hudak*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射C型第1层循环箭图Hecke代数上Specht模之间的同态。对于具有"短腿"或"短臂"的钩子分拆，给出了完整的箭图Hecke代数作用，并证明了任意非零同态完全由将循环生成元映射到单个标准基元决定。利用这一作用研究到这些Specht模的同态，在短腿情形下给出了完全分类。这是仿射C型中Specht模之间同态的首次研究，并推广了先前在仿射A型中使用的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射C型循环箭图Hecke代数上Specht模之间的同态，这是此前未被探索的领域。该工作将仿射A型中已有的方法推广到仿射C型，为理解更一般仿射李代数类型中的表示论提供基础。

Method: 聚焦于具有"短腿"或"短臂"的钩子分拆对应的Specht模，给出完整的箭图Hecke代数作用描述，并证明同态由单个标准基元的映射唯一确定。利用该作用对到这些Specht模的同态进行分类。

Result: 给出了短腿/短臂钩子分拆Specht模的完整箭图Hecke代数作用；证明了任意非零同态由循环生成元到单个标准基元的映射唯一决定；在短腿情形下给出了到这些Specht模的同态的完全分类。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了仿射C型Specht模之间的同态，成功将仿射A型的方法推广至仿射C型，并在短腿情形下获得完全分类，为后续研究更广泛的仿射李代数类型提供了重要方法和结果。

Abstract: We investigate homomorphisms between Specht modules for level 1 cyclotomic quiver Hecke algebras of affine type C. For hook partitions with a `short leg' or a `short arm', we give the full quiver Hecke algebra action and we show that every non-zero homomorphism from an arbitrary Specht module is determined by sending the cyclic generator to a single standard basis element. We use this action to investigate homomorphisms to these Specht modules, giving a full classification in the short leg case. This provides the first investigation of homomorphisms between Specht modules in affine type C and extends methods previously used in affine type A.

</details>


### [142] [Multisymmetric polynomials on set-theoretic quiver representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16956)
*Radford Green,Cornell Holmes,Mee Seong Im*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限无汇箭图（每个顶点都是足够长路径的终点）上的“最终常值集值表示”的计数问题，将其编码为有向无环图，通过递归源删除方法获得严格上三角矩阵枚举器，并压缩为基于基数向量的矩阵，最终推导出Jordan箭图和循环箭图的计数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Green-Holmes-Im关于等方向循环箭图的最终常值集值表示计数工作，推广到更一般的有限无汇箭图（每个顶点都是足够长路径的终点）。

Method: 1) 将表示编码为有向无环图；2) 引入针对特定有向无环图类的递归源删除方法；3) 在子集格的关联代数中构造严格上三角矩阵枚举器；4) 将枚举器压缩为以基数向量（集值模拟的维数向量）为索引的矩阵。

Result: 得到了有限无汇箭图上的最终常值集值表示的计数公式，并特例化到Jordan箭图（恢复已知结果）和循环箭图（无需使用矩阵-树定理获得多重对称生成多项式）。

Conclusion: 本文的方法不依赖矩阵-树定理，为更广泛的箭图类提供了组合计数工具，且能统一处理Jordan箭图和循环箭图的情形。

Abstract: Eventually constant set-valued representations of a quiver are set-theoretic analogues of nilpotent representations. In recent work by Green-Holmes-Im, the authors enumerated eventually constant set-valued representations for equioriented cyclic quivers using the directed matrix-tree theorem. In this paper, we extend this enumeration to finite quivers without sinks for which every vertex is the target of sufficiently long paths. We encode the representations as directed acyclic graphs and introduce a recursive source-removal method for certain classes of directed acyclic graphs. This yields a strictly upper triangular matrix enumerator in the incidence algebra of the subset lattice. To compute the cardinality of the eventually constant representations, we compress this enumerator to a matrix indexed by cardinality vectors, the set-theoretic analogues of dimension vectors. We conclude by specializing the formulas to the Jordan quiver and recovering the multisymmetric generating polynomial for the cyclic quiver without using the matrix-tree theorem.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Bayesian Optimization for Learning Nonlinear MPC in Autonomous Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14763)
*Lorenzo Ortolani,Gabriel Voss,Gabriele Beltrami,Francesco Dorati,Tommaso Felice Banfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无地图的自主导航框架，将滚动时域规划与非线性MPC相结合，使用LiDAR高斯占据表示、A*搜索和Sigmoid障碍屏障，并通过TPE贝叶斯优化自动调参，在Unitree Go2四足机器人上实现90%导航成功率及38.9%性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态未知环境下的实时自主导航仍然是移动机器人领域的基础性挑战，现有方法对参数敏感且难以迁移到实体机器人。

Method: 1) 基于LiDAR的高斯占据表示构建环境模型；2) A*搜索生成无碰撞轨迹；3) 非线性MPC（CasADi/IPOPT）结合Sigmoid障碍屏障函数跟踪轨迹；4) 离线TPE贝叶斯优化自动搜索近优控制器参数；5) 高斯过程代理模型分析参数敏感性。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上，仿真调参可直接迁移到实体硬件，导航成功率达90.0%，仿真环境下评估指标平均提升38.9%。

Conclusion: 该无地图框架机器人无关性强，仿真到实物的参数迁移有效，为动态未知环境下的四足机器人自主导航提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous navigation in dynamic, unknown environments remains a fundamental challenge for mobile robotics. We propose a map-free framework that tightly integrates reactive rolling-horizon planning with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC). At each control cycle, a LiDAR-based Gaussian occupancy representation is constructed and used to generate collision-free trajectories via A* search, which are then tracked by a CasADi/IPOPT MPC formulation incorporating a smooth sigmoid obstacle barrier. To improve robustness to parameter sensitivity, we adopt an offline Bayesian optimization scheme based on Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE), which identifies near-optimal controller parameters with respect to a composite navigation objective. In addition, a Gaussian Process surrogate is used to analyze parameter sensitivity and provide insight into the optimization landscape.
  The proposed framework is robot-agnostic and is evaluated on the Unitree Go2 quadruped in simulation using Gazebo, followed by deployment on the physical robot. Experimental results show that parameters tuned in simulation transfer effectively to hardware, maintaining comparable performance without additional tuning. The full system achieves up to a 90.0\% navigation success rate when deployed, along with a 38.9\% average improvement in the evaluation metrics across simulated environments.

</details>


### [144] [Synthetic-to-Real Pipeline for Safe Landing Zone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14767)
*Shrikant Banerjee,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向无人机自主着陆的感知与数据生成管线，通过程序化合成数据引擎生成逼真的城市环境并自动标注语义，使用Transformer架构的OneFormer模型在该合成数据上微调实现语义分割，结合欧几里得距离变换（EDT）和动态推理逻辑确定最大内接安全着陆区。实验表明，该方法无需人工标注即可在真实场景中识别无碰撞着陆点，弥合了仿真到现实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机向高自主性发展时，在非合作、非结构化环境中进行无辅助回收至关重要。安全自主着陆需要高保真语义分辨率以区分可降落地形与危险障碍物，但缺乏标注的航拍数据集严重阻碍了相关研发进展。

Method: 1) 构建程序化合成数据引擎，通过域随机化生成逼真城市环境并自动生成语义标注；2) 采用Transformer架构的OneFormer模型，利用多头自注意力机制捕获全局上下文，仅在合成数据上微调；3) 设计确定性着陆模块，基于欧几里得距离变换（EDT）和动态推理逻辑，识别最大内接安全着陆区并保持严格的障碍物安全缓冲距离。

Result: 在UAVid数据集上的定量基准测试证明了鲁棒的语义分割性能；在真实无人机视频上的定性验证表明，系统能够在未见过的环境中识别无碰撞着陆点。

Conclusion: 高保真程序化仿真有潜力消除人工标注的需求，同时为无人机自主回收提供鲁棒的、可在边缘端部署的态势感知能力。

Abstract: As Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) transition toward higher levels of autonomy, the ability to perform unassisted recovery in non-cooperative, unstructured environments becomes critical. Achieving safe autonomous landing requires high-fidelity semantic resolution to distinguish navigable terrain from hazardous obstacles, yet development is often hindered by the scarcity of annotated aerial datasets. This work proposes a comprehensive perception and data generation pipeline designed to bridge the sim-to-real gap for autonomous landing tasks. We introduce a procedural synthetic data engine that generates photorealistic urban environments with automated semantic annotations through domain randomization. A Transformer-based OneFormer architecture is fine-tuned exclusively on this synthetic data, leveraging multi-head self-attention mechanisms for global context resolution. To ensure operational safety, a deterministic landing module utilizes a Euclidean Distance Transform (EDT) and dynamic inference logic to identify the largest inscribed safe landing zones while maintaining strict clearance buffers around obstacles. Quantitative benchmarking against the UAVid dataset demonstrates robust semantic segmentation performance, while qualitative validation on real-world UAV footage confirms the system's ability to identify collision-free landing sites in unseen environments. Our results highlight the potential of high-fidelity procedural simulation to eliminate the need for manual annotation while providing robust, edge-deployable situational awareness for autonomous UAV recovery.

</details>


### [145] [Deep Learning-Based Lunar Crater Terrain Relative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14776)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合深度学习陨石坑检测器与扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的地形相对导航（TRN）算法，用于月球着陆器的精确位置估计。算法通过单目图像检测陨石坑，与全球数据库匹配后去除异常值，再将测量值输入EKF以修正航天器姿态。仿真表明，即使初始位置偏差达5公里，TRN也能将导航误差降至数百米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现月球着陆任务中自主飞行器的精确定位，特别是在地形特征稀疏的危险环境下，克服传统导航方法在此类场景下的局限性。

Method: 1) 基于深度学习的陨石坑检测器（专为NASA陨石坑检测挑战赛设计）；2) 通过匈牙利分配算法匹配检测到的陨石坑与全球数据库；3) 基于共识的异常值移除方法；4) 扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）融合测量值进行位姿估计，并利用高度辅助信息约束径向漂移。

Result: 航天器初始位置偏差达5公里时，TRN算法仍能恢复，将导航误差降至数百米量级。

Conclusion: 所提TRN算法能有效提升月球着陆任务中的定位精度与鲁棒性；但需注意保持图像分辨率与场景尺度与检测器训练集分布一致，以维持陨石坑特征匹配的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate position estimation is crucial for the successful implementation of future lunar landings using autonomous vehicles, especially in dangerous environments with sparse terrain features. In this paper, we propose a terrain relative navigation (TRN) algorithm combining our deep-learning crater detector, which was designed specifically for the NASA Crater Detection Challenge problem, and an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF).
