The key idea is to replace stochastic latent sampling of the trained generative policy with a learned latent actor that predicts a state-dependent latent input for the generative policies. This separates exploration and control: stochastic generative sampling provides diverse action proposals during policy learning, while deterministic latent steering provides stable and adaptive control at deployment. We evaluate SteerGenPO on six Isaac Lab benchmarks and a Unitree G1 locomotion task. The results show SteerGenPO improves over both classical RL and generative RL baselines, while its deterministic latent steering produces more stable inference-time behaviors and more reliable command responses.

</details>


### [219] [WaveSync: Constrained Wavefront Optimization for Synchronized Co-Speech Gestures in Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16600)
*Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Gia Huy Uong,Phuc Van Dinh,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Xiem HoangVan,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WaveSync 是一种混合框架，利用大语言模型解构对话语义生成“语义重要性波”，结合动态运动基元和波前优化，在人形机器人上实现与语音强调同步且满足运动学约束的 expressive 共语手势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人形机器人无法像虚拟化身那样自由执行快速或重叠运动，生成与语音强调对齐且满足严格运动学和动力学约束的共语手势是一个耦合难题。

Method: 1) LLM 将对话响应分解为结构化语义模式并分配词级重要性权重，构建连续的语义重要性波；2) 通过动态运动基元（DMP）塑造手势轨迹以保证运动学可行性并增强表现力；3) 波前优化阶段实现峰-峰手势-语音同步对齐，通过手势持续时间压缩和前向传播解决残留运动学冲突。

Result: 基于五个对话场景的实验表明，该方法实现了高同步精度，在客观和主观评估中均优于三个基线方法。每个组件在产生表现力、语义基础和运动学合规的手势中都发挥了必要作用。

Conclusion: WaveSync 有效解决了人形机器人上语义对齐手势生成中的同步精度与运动学可行性耦合问题，验证了混合框架各组件协同的必要性。

Abstract: Expressive co-speech gestures are crucial for natural human-robot interaction, but generating them on physical humanoid robots is difficult because gesture strokes must align with speech emphasis while satisfying strict kinematic and dynamic constraints. Unlike virtual avatars, humanoid robots cannot freely execute rapid or overlapping motions, making word-level synchronization and hardware-safe motion planning a coupled problem. We present \textbf{WaveSync}, a hybrid framework in which a Large Language Model decomposes dialogue responses into structured semantic schemas and assigns per-word importance weights, constructing a continuous Semantic Importance Wave. Gesture trajectories are shaped through Dynamic Movement Primitives, enforcing kinematic feasibility while enhancing expressiveness. A Wavefront Optimization stage aligns peak-to-peak gesture-speech synchronization and resolves residual kinematic violations through gesture-duration compression and forward propagation. Experimental evaluation based on five dialogue scenarios shows that our method achieves high synchronization accuracy and outperforms three baselines in both objective and subjective evaluations. Each component in WaveSync plays a necessary role in producing gestures that are expressive, semantically grounded, and kinematically compliant. The code, resources, and videos are available at \href{https://github.com/pairs-lab/WaveSync}{WaveSync}

</details>


### [220] [Reinforcement Learning with Inner-loop Dynamics Estimator for Aerial Manipulation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16621)
*Shivansh Pratap Singh,Samaksh Ujjwal,Ishita Chaudhary,V R Vasudevan,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合分层控制框架，通过强化学习（RL）外环实现末端执行器6自由度任务驱动，内环利用动态估计器补偿快速臂运动、载荷变化等不确定性的空中机械臂控制方法。在搭载3自由度机械臂的四旋翼平台上验证，相比RL+PID和RL+INDI+PID基线，该方法在变载荷条件下降低了末端跟踪误差并提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在快速臂运动、载荷变化及相关未知动态不确定性下的全身协同操纵问题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 层次化控制框架：外环使用强化学习将6自由度末端执行器目标映射为全身协调指令，无需精确耦合动力学模型；内环使用动态估计器补偿瞬态惯性偏移和不确定性，不依赖系统模型知识。

Result: 在变载荷硬件实验中，相比RL+PID和RL+INDI+PID基线，所提方法降低了末端跟踪误差并提高了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将学习型全身协调与基于估计器的低层补偿相结合，可提高空中机械臂在变化操作条件下的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators enable physical interaction in hard-to-reach environments; however, the combined problem of direct whole-body aerial manipulation under rapid arm motion, payload changes, and related unknown dynamic uncertainty remains a largely unsolved problem. We present a hierarchical control framework that combines Reinforcement Learning (RL) with an inner-loop dynamics estimator to address this problem. The RL outer loop maps desired 6-degrees-of-freedom (DOF) end-effector targets to coordinated whole-body commands, enabling direct task-driven control without relying on a fully accurate coupled dynamic model in the policy layer. An inner loop then tracks these commands while compensating for transient inertial shifts and uncertainty during execution via a dynamics estimator scheme without requiring system model knowledge. We validate the proposed approach on a custom quadrotor equipped with a 3-DoF manipulator through hardware experiments under varying payload conditions. Compared with RL+PID and RL+INDI+PID baselines, the proposed method reduces end-effector tracking error and improves task success rate across the tested hardware conditions. These results show that combining learned whole-body coordination with estimator-based low-level compensation improves the precision and robustness of aerial manipulation under changing operating conditions.

</details>


### [221] [PATCH: Action-Chunk-Conditioned Latent Patch Innovation Monitoring for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16690)
*Yanan Zhou,Ranpeng Qiu,Yincong Chen,Jiajie Cui,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PATCH是一个动作块条件化的潜在补丁创新监控器，用于机器人操作过程中的部署时干预。它通过预测执行走廊内的潜在补丁演化，累积机器人自身运动无法解释的持续性残差，生成局部化干预信号，实现异常检测、策略暂停、恢复源选择及策略恢复功能。在真实机器人实验上表现优于现有运行时监控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的操作策略在开放工作空间中面对意外局部场景动态（如移动物体、临时遮挡、运动干扰）时表现脆弱，而现有运行时监控器难以区分任务相关的执行风险与良性的视觉变化，缺乏对局部异常的有效感知能力。

Method: 提出PATCH监控器：1) 基于当前动作块定义投影执行走廊；2) 预测走廊内潜在补丁的演化过程；3) 累积机器人自身运动无法解释的持续性残差，形成局部化干预信号；4) 结合PATCH-Router实现执行暂停、恢复源选择、策略恢复的完整干预流程。

Result: 在真实机器人回滚数据上，PATCH相比现有运行时监控器产生更稳定、上下文相关的触发信号。真实机器人部署验证了监控驱动干预和策略恢复在干扰感知操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: PATCH通过动作块条件化的局部潜在补丁残差分析，有效解决了开放空间中局部动态干扰下的机器人操作鲁棒性问题，实现了可靠的运行时监控与干预恢复机制。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies have made substantial progress in real-world robot manipulation, particularly for short-horizon action generation. However, deployment in open workspaces remains fragile under unexpected local scene dynamics, such as moving objects, transient occlusions, or disturbances near the intended motion. Existing runtime monitors often rely on global observation anomalies, policy uncertainty, or frame-level visual changes, and struggle to distinguish task-relevant execution risk from benign visual variation. We introduce PATCH, an action-chunk-conditioned latent patch innovation monitor for deployment-time intervention. Given the active action chunk, PATCH defines a projected execution corridor, predicts latent patch evolution inside it, and accumulates persistent residuals unexplained by the robot's own motion. These residuals form a localized intervention signal that allows PATCH-Router to pause execution, select an available recovery source, and resume the original policy once localized innovation subsides. Experiments on real robot rollout data show that PATCH produces more stable and context-relevant triggers than competing runtime monitors. Real-robot deployment further demonstrates monitor-driven intervention and policy resumption for disturbance-aware manipulation. Project Page: https://yananzhou5555.github.io/PATCH/.

</details>


### [222] [VENOM: Versatile Embodied Network for Omni-bodied Motion tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16696)
*Siddharth Padmanabhan,Kazuki Miyazawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VENOM是一个基于GPT的跨具身全身运动追踪模型，在仿真环境中通过多个人形机器人数据训练，无需将控制问题分解为上、下半身，能够实现与专家级强化学习策略相近的全身运动追踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从示范数据中实现专家级、表达性的全身运动追踪在多个人形机器人上仍然是一个具有挑战性且尚未充分探索的问题。现有跨具身运动追踪策略大多将控制问题分解为上、下半身分别处理，缺乏统一全身追踪的方法。

Method: 提出VENOM，一个基于GPT架构的运动追踪器，在包含状态、动作和奖励的多个人形机器人运动追踪数据集（VENOM数据集）上训练，无需将控制拆分为上、下半身。采用监督学习方式训练，并与MLP（多具身监督学习）及非对称Actor-Critic强化学习专家策略进行对比。

Result: VENOM在不同人形机器人上实现了比MLP（仅用监督学习在多具身数据上训练）更稳定的运动追踪性能；尽管缺乏奖励反馈，VENOM的追踪能力与使用非对称Actor-Critic强化学习训练的专家策略紧密匹配。

Conclusion: VENOM证明了基于GPT的架构可以有效地实现跨具身全身运动追踪，无需分解上下半身控制，且仅通过监督学习即可达到接近专家级强化学习策略的追踪性能，为简化人形机器人全身运动追踪提供了新思路。

Abstract: Achieving expert-level expressive full-body motion tracking across multiple humanoids solely from demonstration data remains a challenging and relatively an underexplored problem in humanoid robot learning. Cross-embodiment motion tracking policies are mostly trained by decoupling the control problem into upper and lower body control. This work proposes VENOM, a cross-embodiment full-body motion tracking model for humanoids in simulation. VENOM is a GPT-based motion tracker trained on multiple humanoid data that can track the entire body without the requirement to split into upper and lower body control. We curate a multi-humanoid motion tracking dataset called the VENOM dataset that contains states, actions, and rewards and train VENOM and the baselines on this dataset. In this letter, we evaluate VENOM's performance against baselines and show that we can achieve a stable motion tracker across different humanoids more capable than an MLP trained on multiple humanoid data with supervised learning alone, and also show that despite lack of reward feedback, VENOM closely matches the tracking capability of experts that were trained using asymmetric-actor critic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [223] [Pride and Prejudice: Toward an Information-Theoretic Framework for Mutually Communicative Driver Behavior Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16735)
*Tingjun Li,Nan Xu,Shuo Feng,Hassan Askari,Bruno Henrique Groenner Barbosa,Konghui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合等级k贝叶斯说服博弈、虚拟特征主动信号、信息论互通信奖励和自适应通信可供性权重的框架，用于建模自动驾驶中变道场景的隐式互通信。通过C-MIRL算法在NGSIM数据集上校准，相比无通信基线，强制变道预测误差降低20%，且主观问卷结果支持模型有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合自动驾驶场景中，自动驾驶车辆（AVs）与人类驾驶车辆（HVs）因误解彼此意图而导致不安全和不高效，现有方法缺乏对隐式互通信和认知不确定性的显式建模。

Method: 1) 构建等级k贝叶斯说服博弈框架，结合虚拟特征实现主动信号传递；2) 引入信息论奖励以量化互通信价值；3) 设计自适应通信可供性权重；4) 提出Pride-Inquiry (P-I)和Pride-Prejudice (P-P)平面分析通信强度与倾向；5) 开发基于通信的多智能体逆强化学习算法（C-MIRL）在NGSIM数据集上校准模型。

Result: 1) 相比无通信基线，强制变道预测误差降低20%，且泛化能力强；2) 驾驶员在环问卷得分与校准后的通信变量正相关；3) 学习到的奖励表明询问和倾听可供性的贡献大于表达和骄傲，且询问偏好的驾驶员间差异更大。

Conclusion: 显式建模互通信和认知不确定性对于交互式驾驶至关重要，所提框架能有效提升混合自动驾驶的安全性和效率。

Abstract: Mixed autonomy driving becomes unsafe and inefficient when autonomous vehicles (AVs) and human-driven vehicles (HVs) misread each other's intentions. We study this problem as implicit mutual communication in lane changes. The proposed framework models how the ego vehicle both expresses its intent and probes the other driver's preference under epistemic uncertainty. It combines a level-k Bayesian persuasion game with virtual features for proactive signaling, information-theoretic rewards for mutual communication, and adaptive weights of communication affordances. We further introduce the Pride-Inquiry (P-I) and Pride-Prejudice (P-P) planes to analyze communication intensity and tendency. The model is calibrated with a Communication-Based Multi-Agent Inverse Reinforcement Learning algorithm (C-MIRL) on the naturalistic NGSIM dataset. Compared with the non-communicative baseline, the proposed model reduces the prediction error of mandatory lane changes by up to 20% while maintaining strong generalization. Driver-In-the-Loop questionnaire scores are positively correlated with the calibrated communication variables, supporting the subjective validity of the model. The learned rewards further show that inquiry and listening affordances contribute more than pride and expression alone, and that inquiry preference varies more strongly across drivers. These results support explicit modeling of mutual communication and epistemic uncertainty in interactive driving.

</details>


### [224] [DataLadder: A Simulation-Enabled Interconversion Toolchain for the Embodied Data Pyramid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16776)
*Peidong Liu,Yongce Liu,Songyan Guo,Fuyuan Ma,Zhihao Yuan,Ao Li,Zengjue Chen,Wenhao Li,Tianle Zhang,Mingyang Li,Jiale Zhang,Junzhe Xiong,Zhiyuan Xiang,Dafeng Chi,Yuzheng Zhuang,Yihang Li,Qingrong He,Jiaming Liang,Chen Cai,Peng Hao,Mingxi Luo,Song Wang,Junwu Xiong,Ruodai Li,Liyi Luo,Wei Tan,Dongjiang Li,Jiawei Li,Hui Shen,Yicheng Gong,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DataLadder 是一个模拟赋能的互转换工具链，通过 Robot ⇌ Simulation ⇌ Human 双通路实现人-机器人对齐的模型评估和数据生成。该工具链利用 JoySim 模拟器作为可扩展评估层和物理一致性过滤器，并通过京东云服务将核心模块封装为可复用基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略需要可信的评估和可用的训练数据，但完全依赖物理机器人难以扩展——真实机器人试验和演示虽然是最可靠的部署信号来源，但速度慢、成本高且难以复现。

Method: 提出 DataLadder 工具链，包含两条通路：(1) Robot→Simulation→Human：将真实机器人桌面整理任务重建为校准数字孪生用于可扩展评估，并通过人类具身反馈检查和优化模拟动作的自然度；(2) Human→Simulation→Robot：将第一人称人类演示提升至模拟环境，在机器人物理约束下检查并转换为以机器人为中心的轨迹、标注和视觉观测。使用 JoySim 模拟器作为可扩展评估层和物理一致性过滤器，核心模块封装为京东云服务。

Result: 实现了模拟与真实之间的双向数据流通，支持可扩展的机器人模型评估和训练数据生成，并将系统转化为可复用的云基础设施。

Conclusion: DataLadder 通过仿真桥接真实机器人和人类演示，解决了机器人数据生成和模型评估的可扩展性问题，提供了可复用的基础设施方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies require trustworthy evaluation and robot-usable training data, but both are difficult to scale with physical robots alone. Real-robot trials and demonstrations remain the most faithful source of deployment signals, yet they are slow, costly, and hard to reproduce. We present DataLadder, a simulation-enabled interconversion toolchain for human-robot aligned model evaluation and data generation, denoted as Robot $\rightleftharpoons$ Simulation $\rightleftharpoons$ Human. On the one hand, the Robot $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Human pathway supports human-robot aligned model evaluation by reconstructing real-robot tabletop organization tasks as calibrated digital twins for scalable evaluation, while using human embodied feedback to inspect and refine the naturalness of simulated motions. On the other hand, the Human $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Robot pathway supports human-robot aligned data generation: it lifts ego-centric human demonstrations into simulation, checks them under robot physical constraints, and converts them into robot-centered trajectories, annotations, and visual observations. Together, these pathways use the JoySim simulator as both a scalable evaluation layer and a physical consistency filter for robot data generation. We further package the core reconstruction, simulation, rendering, and realism-augmentation modules as cloud services on JD Cloud, turning the system into reusable infrastructure for robot data generation and model evaluation.

</details>


### [225] [DIFF-IPPO: Diffusion-Based Informative Path Planning with Open-Vocabulary Belief Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16780)
*Sausar Karaf,Oleg Sautenkov,Mikhail Martynov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFF-IPPO，一种将开放词汇信念图生成器与基于扩散的规划器相结合的流水线，用于信念图上的全局轨迹生成，在多个数据集场景下实现81.49%-86.55%的归一化检测分数，并在模拟搜救场景中由五架无人机组成的团队在3.5分钟内完成首次检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息路径规划方法多依赖高斯过程信念模型，而物体搜索场景常产生复杂的多模态信念图。直接在这些非高斯信念图上进行全局轨迹生成的方法相对不足，尽管基于扩散的规划器具有建模此类分布的强大能力，但尚未被充分应用于信息路径规划。

Method: 提出DIFF-IPPO流水线，包含两个核心组件：(1) 开放词汇信念图生成器，用于感知环境并生成多模态信念图；(2) 基于扩散的规划器，在信念图上生成全局轨迹，使传感器覆盖集中于高信念区域。支持批量信念图条件轨迹生成，适用于多无人机协同场景。

Result: 在多个数据集场景下归一化检测分数达到81.49%-86.55%；在模拟搜救场景中，五架无人机团队使用批量信念图条件轨迹生成，在3.5分钟内首次检测到燃烧建筑。

Conclusion: DIFF-IPPO成功将扩散模型引入信息路径规划，有效处理了非高斯多模态信念图上的全局轨迹生成问题，在物体搜索与探索任务中展现出良好性能与实用性。

Abstract: Exploration and object search require robots to perceive their environment, identify regions of interest, and plan trajectories that improve target-detection likelihood or maximize information gain. Many IPP methods, especially in continuous environmental monitoring, rely on Gaussian-process belief models, while object-search settings often produce complex, multimodal belief maps from semantic or open-vocabulary perception. Global trajectory generation directly conditioned on such non-Gaussian belief maps remains comparatively underexplored. Although diffusion-based planners offer strong capabilities for modeling such distributions, their use in informative path planning remains limited. In this work, we propose DIFF-IPPO, a pipeline that integrates an open-vocabulary belief map generator with a diffusion-based planner for global trajectory generation over belief maps. The method generates trajectories that concentrate sensor coverage over high-belief regions, achieving normalized detection scores between 81.49% and 86.55% across different dataset scenarios. We validate the system in a simulated search-and-rescue scenario where the planner searches candidate building regions to locate a burning building. In this setting, a team of five drones using batched belief-map-conditioned trajectory generation achieves first detections in 3.5 minutes.

</details>


### [226] [SoK: Security and Privacy of Foundation-Model-Powered Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16788)
*Xueluan Gong,Chen Chen,Jinxin Liu,Qian Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个渐进式F-E-S-G结构边界框架，用于系统分析基础模型驱动机器人的安全与隐私风险，涵盖基础模型层、具身系统层、支持生态层和治理影响层，并基于此框架对96篇论文进行了系统化梳理，识别了多种威胁模式、防御不匹配和评估缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献综述通常聚焦于特定的基础模型类型、风险类别、缓解策略或信任边界，缺乏一个统一的结构来分析风险来源、在机器人系统中的传播路径以及应在何处进行干预。

Method: 提出渐进式F-E-S-G结构边界框架（基础模型层、具身系统层、支持生态层、治理影响层），在此基础上构建多层级分类法（信任边界、安全-隐私关注、风险-缓解视角），并对每篇论文使用细粒度编码属性（目标、生命周期阶段、机制、系统访问、效果）进行标注。

Result: 系统化了96篇论文的分析，揭示了多种威胁模式、防御不匹配和评估缺口，这些在单一边界视角下难以识别。

Conclusion: 基于发现指出了开放挑战和未来方向，为开发安全、隐私保护且负责任治理的基础模型驱动机器人系统提供了研究议程。

Abstract: Foundation models are reshaping robotics by enabling robots to interpret open-ended instructions, reason over multimodal contexts, and operate in complex, open-world environments. However, their integration also introduces security and privacy (S&P) risks that extend beyond the FMs themselves to embodied execution pipelines, supporting ecosystems, and broader governance impacts. Existing literature reviews provide valuable insights but often focus on specific FM types, risk categories, mitigation strategies, or trust boundaries. Consequently, the field lacks a unified structure for analyzing where risks originate, how they propagate across robotic systems, and where mitigations should intervene. To address this gap, we propose a progressive F-E-S-G structural boundary framework for analyzing the S&P of FM-powered robots. The framework comprises four layers: the Foundation model layer (F), Embodied system layer (E), Supporting ecosystem layer (S), and Governance impact layer (G). Building on this structure, we develop a multi-level taxonomy that organizes prior studies along three levels: F-E-S-G trust boundary, security-privacy concerns, and risk-mitigation perspectives. We further annotate each study using fine-grained coding attributes, including target, lifecycle stage, mechanism, system access, and effect. Guided by this framework and taxonomy, we systematize 96 papers. Our analysis uncovers multiple threat patterns, defense mismatches, and evaluation gaps that are difficult to identify from a single-boundary perspective. Based on these findings, we identify open challenges and future directions to provide a research agenda for developing secure, privacy-preserving, and responsibly governed FM-powered robotic systems.

</details>


### [227] [ATOM-Bench: A Real-World Benchmark for Atomic Skills and Compositional Generalization in Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16826)
*Zenan Wu,Bingqing Wei,Lu Liu,Zheqi He,Xi Wang,Jiakang Liu,Zehui Li,Guocai Yao,Jing-Shu Zheng,Xi Yang,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATOM-Bench是一个用于评估机器人操作策略中原子技能和组合泛化能力的真实世界基准，包含30个原子任务和24个组合任务，通过2700次物理实验发现现有策略在细粒度运动原子、计数和逻辑过滤方面存在困难，且强原子性能无法可靠迁移到组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略在现实世界中的泛化能力难以诊断，现有方法可能在演示任务上成功，但在执行细粒度原子技能或在新任务结构中重组所学技能时失败，缺乏系统性的评估方法。

Method: 将桌面操作分解为运动原子和指令原子，构建包含30个原子任务和24个保留组合任务的基准；收集3000个人类演示用于原子微调；引入原子分数(AS)和组合失败份额(CFS)两个指标来区分原子技能不足和组合重用受限导致的失败。

Result: 在5种代表性操作策略上进行了2700次物理实验，发现当前策略能获取简单的指令接地技能，但在细粒度运动原子、计数和逻辑过滤上表现不佳；强原子性能无法可靠迁移到保留的组合任务中。

Conclusion: ATOM-Bench为诊断操作策略失败来源（弱运动执行、差指令接地或有限组合重用）提供了可复现的测试平台。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies are increasingly presented as foundation models for robotic control, but their real-world generalization remains difficult to diagnose. A policy may succeed on demonstrated tasks while still failing to execute fine-grained atomic skills or recombine learned skills in new task structures. We introduce \textbf{ATOM-Bench}, a real-world benchmark for evaluating both atomic skills and compositional generalization in manipulation policies. ATOM-Bench factorizes tabletop manipulation into motor atoms and instruction atoms, and contains 30 atomic tasks and 24 held-out compositional tasks across paired single-arm and dual-arm robot tracks. We collect 3,000 human demonstrations for atomic fine-tuning and release both the demonstration data and evaluation rollout data to support reproducible real-world evaluation. Policies are fine-tuned on atomic tasks and evaluated on both atomic skill acquisition and held-out compositional tasks. We further introduce Atomic Score (AS) and Compositional Failure Share (CFS) to distinguish failures caused by weak atomic skills from failures caused by limited compositional reuse. Through 2,700 physical rollouts on five representative manipulation policies, we find that current policies can acquire simple instruction-grounding skills, but still struggle with fine-grained motor atoms, counting, and logical filtering. More importantly, strong atomic performance does not reliably transfer to held-out compositional tasks. ATOM-Bench provides a diagnostic testbed for studying whether failures arise from weak motor execution, poor instruction grounding, or limited compositional reuse.

</details>


### [228] [Video-Based Optimal Transport for Feedback-Efficient Offline Preference-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16856)
*Tung M. Luu,Hwanhee Kim,Younghwan Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOTP是一种基于视频基础模型和最优传输的半监督偏好强化学习框架，仅需少量人工标注即可学习有效奖励函数，在多种基准任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习（PbRL）虽然能从人类反馈中学习奖励函数，但标注成本高昂，限制了其可扩展性。现有方法需要大量人类偏好标签，亟需降低人工监督成本。

Method: 利用视频基础模型（ViFMs）的丰富表征空间，通过最优传输（Optimal Transport）对齐视觉轨迹，为大量未标注数据生成高保真伪标签，实现半监督学习。

Result: 在 locomotion 和 manipulation 基准任务上，VOTP在有限反馈预算下超越了最先进的离线PbRL方法；在存在视觉干扰项时表现鲁棒，并在真实机器人任务中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: VOTP通过结合视频基础模型和最优传输，显著降低了偏好强化学习对人工标注的依赖，实现了高效、鲁棒的奖励函数学习，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Conveying complex objectives to reinforcement learning (RL) agents often requires meticulous reward engineering. Preference-based RL (PbRL) offers a promising alternative by learning reward functions from human feedback, but its scalability is hindered by high labeling costs. Inspired by advances in Video Foundation Models (ViFMs), we present Video-based Optimal Transport Preference (VOTP), a semi-supervised framework that learns effective reward functions from only a handful of labels. By leveraging optimal transport to align visual trajectories within the rich representation space of ViFMs, VOTP effectively generates high-fidelity pseudo-labels for large amounts of unlabeled data, substantially reducing human supervision. Extensive experiments across locomotion and manipulation benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of VOTP, which outperforms state-of-the-art offline PbRL methods under limited feedback budgets. We also showcase the robustness of VOTP in the presence of visual distractors and validate its utility on real robotic tasks, where it learns meaningful rewards with minimal human input.

</details>


### [229] [ExoTraj: A General Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance Policy for Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16876)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Long Sun,Xu Liang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExoTraj框架，结合快速流匹配轨迹预测与模型预测控制扭矩优化，实现外骨骼在复杂户外环境中的自适应助力，无需昂贵运动捕捉系统，显著降低预测误差、代谢率、心率和肌肉激活水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态外骨骼场景中的自适应扭矩预测依赖昂贵的动作捕捉系统，在复杂户外环境中不可行。现有轨迹预测方法多限于单步预测且忽略受试者间变异，限制了轨迹优化空间和泛化能力；同时，人机系统的高动态性和感知-控制强耦合使得基于预测轨迹的高效助力难以实现。

Method: 提出快速流匹配方法实现精确轨迹预测与泛化，利用轨迹生成误差和编码观测引导训练方向；采用模型预测控制设计新型优化目标优化扭矩。将两者整合为统一策略ExoTraj。

Result: 与传统方法相比，ExoTraj在线阶段跨受试者预测误差降低14.0%，对外部噪声具有鲁棒性；相对于零扭矩条件，代谢率降低11.5-24.4%，心率降低1.7-19.5%，峰值肌肉激活水平降低10.9-41.3%。

Conclusion: ExoTraj通过流匹配轨迹预测与MPC扭矩优化的结合，在无需高数据采集成本的情况下实现了复杂户外场景中的自适应助力，性能显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Adaptive torque prediction in dynamic exoskeleton scenarios requires expensive motion capture systems, which are infeasible in complex outdoor environments. Trajectory prediction has emerged as one of the effective approaches to address such an issue. However, the core challenges of exoskeleton trajectory prediction are twofold: establishing the mapping from multi-modal features to trajectory information; constructing the mapping from trajectory to torque. For the former, most existing methods perform only single-step prediction and neglect inter-subject trajectory variability, thereby limiting the trajectory optimization space and prediction generalization. To address this, this paper proposes a fast flow matching method that enables accurate trajectory prediction and better generalization for real-time performance, where trajectory generation errors and encoded observations are used to guide the training direction. For the second challenge, due to the high dynamics of the human-robot system and the strong coupling between perception and control, simple control methods struggle to achieve efficient assistance based on the predicted trajectory. This paper utilizes model predictive control and designs a novel optimization objective to optimize torque, ensuring the exoskeleton achieves comfortable and robust assistance. By integrating the above two components, the unified policy, denoted as ExoTraj, is developed to enable adaptive assistance in complex outdoor scenarios without high data acquisition cost. Experimental results show that compared to traditional methods, ExoTraj reduces cross-subject prediction error by 14.0% during the online phase and maintains robustness against external noise. Relative to the zero torque condition, ExoTraj decreases metabolic rate by 11.5-24.4%, heart rate by 1.7-19.5%, and peak muscle activation levels by 10.9-41.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [230] [SGM-SLAM: Scene Graph Matching for Data-Efficient Distributed SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16881)
*Yewei Huang,Tixiao Shan,Abhinav Rajvanshi,Niluthpol Chowdhury Mithun,Yaxuan Li,Brendan Englot,Han-Pang Chiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的分布式SLAM框架，利用仅包含物体标签和质心的场景图匹配来实现多机器人协同定位与建图，通过融合RGB-LiDAR点云构建场景图，并在仿真和真实场景中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式SLAM方法依赖特征级匹配进行机器人间的测量约束识别，通信开销大且效率低，亟需一种数据高效、仅利用高层语义信息的协同SLAM框架。

Method: 利用融合RGB-LiDAR点云构建场景图，包含语义分割点云层和离散有界物体层；通过仅交换物体标签和质心进行场景图匹配，识别机器人间测量约束；采用多步数据交换与优化流程最大化通信效率。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境（四足机器人室内外数据集）中验证了所提框架的有效性和高效性，实现了数据高效的分布式协同SLAM。

Conclusion: 首次将仅基于物体标签和质心的场景图匹配应用于分布式SLAM，显著降低通信开销的同时保持了协同定位与建图的性能。

Abstract: We introduce a data-efficient distributed Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) framework designed for a team of robots equipped with LiDAR, cameras, and inertial sensors. Our framework uses scene graph matching to identify inter-robot measurement constraints. Unlike prior approaches that rely on feature-level matching, our framework is the first to perform scene graph matching using only object labels and centroids. Our approach constructs a scene graph by using fused RGB-LiDAR point clouds to generate both a semantically segmented point cloud layer, and a layer of discrete bounded objects, to accompany estimated robot trajectories. Scene graph matching is performed collaboratively through exchanging and matching object data with neighboring robots. To maximize communication efficiency, we utilize a multi-step data exchange and optimization process. We demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our approach using both simulation and real-world datasets collected by legged robots in indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [231] [LOPAL: Local Performance-Aware Active Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16888)
*Johannes Heidersberger,Shail Jadav,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOPAL，一种利用局部演示质量信息的主动学习方法，通过GMM编码轨迹与质量评估，结合共享自治主动数据采集，在不完美演示中提升机器人任务性能（真实管道检测任务提升27.31%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法未能处理演示质量在局部范围内的差异性，即一次演示中某些片段质量高、某些片段质量低，导致难以从不完美且不一致的人类演示中有效学习。

Method: 两个协同组件：1）局部性能驱动的LfD方法，使用GMM同时编码演示轨迹及其局部质量评估，通过组合互补的高质量局部数据生成优于原始演示的轨迹；2）主动数据采集，在缺失优质数据的区域通过共享自治机制主动请求用户提供纠正，同时机器人自主执行已学行为。

Result: 在真实管道检查任务中，任务性能提升27.31%，同时降低了采集演示所需的人力成本。

Conclusion: 利用局部演示质量信息进行主动学习可以有效克服人类演示中的不一致性和次优性，提升学习效果并减少演示负担。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables intuitive robot skill acquisition by allowing robots to learn directly from human task demonstrations. However, current methods often fail to address the fact that due to suboptimal and inconsistent human behavior, the quality of the demonstration can vary within each demonstration. Therefore, we introduce LOPAL (LOcal Performance-aware Active Learning), an active learning approach that leverages this local demonstration quality information. Our approach consists of two synergistic components. First, a local performance-driven LfD method uses a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) to encode both the demonstrated trajectories and their associated local quality assessments. This enables the generation of trajectories that outperform the imperfect demonstrations by utilizing complementary local data of high performance. Second, active data acquisition allows to improve beyond the imperfect demonstrations by collecting additional informative samples. In areas missing good data, the user is actively requested to provide corrections through a shared autonomy (SA) mechanism, while the robot autonomously executes the learned behavior. The efficacy of LOPAL was validated in both a simulation and a real-world experiment. The results from a real-world pipe inspection task showed that the proposed approach can achieve up to 27.31 % improvement in task performance while also reducing the effort required to collect the demonstrations.

</details>


### [232] [Binary Tracking for Spatial QA and Navigation with Open Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16902)
*Dongbin Na,Chanwoo Kim,Soonbin Rho,Giyun Choi,Gangbok Lee,Dooyoung Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BinTrack，一个完全开源的空间定位智能体，通过对机器人轨迹段进行二分搜索来回答空间查询，在SpaceLocQA基准上提升准确率高达22.8%，并实现1.5倍以上的推理加速。同时发布了多行程户外基准数据集GangnamLoop。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间问答方法依赖GPT-4o等闭源模型进行检索增强，但机器人在真实环境中因网络不稳定、通信延迟和部署成本难以可靠依赖在线闭源模型，因此需要能在机器人本地运行的开源空间问答方法。

Method: BinTrack利用机器人轨迹的时间顺序，通过查询中识别出的两个锚定地标对轨迹段进行二分搜索，从而定位目标位置的空间坐标。

Result: 在SpaceLocQA基准上，BinTrack相比其他开源方法准确率提升高达22.8%，在最具挑战性的全局类别上甚至匹配了GPT-4o等闭源模型的结果；推理速度提升1.5倍以上。

Conclusion: BinTrack证明了纯开源方案可以在空间问答任务上达到与闭源模型相当的性能，且具有更优的推理效率。GangnamLoop数据集为多行程户外场景下的机器人空间理解提供了新的评测基准。

Abstract: This work addresses spatial question answering for service robots traversing long egocentric routes. Given a query such as "where can I find a dry cleaner on the way back home?", the system returns a metric coordinate that downstream navigation components can act on. Prior Spatial Question Answering approaches leverage retrieval-augmented agents built on closed-source models such as GPT-4o for path exploration. However, robots operating in the real world often cannot reliably depend on online closed-source models due to network instability, communication latency, and deployment cost. It creates a need for open-source based Spatial Question Answering approaches that can run onboard the robot, yet prior research in this direction remains limited. This work proposes BinTrack, a simple yet effective, fully open-source spatial-localization agent that leverages the temporal ordering of a robot's trajectory. BinTrack performs a binary search over the trajectory segments between two anchor landmarks identified from a query. It improves overall accuracy by up to 22.8% over other open-source implementations and even matches the reported closed-source model result on the global category of the SpaceLocQA benchmark, the most challenging setting that has so far required strong reasoning agents such as GPT-4o. Furthermore, its optimized inference strategy consistently yields more than a 1.5x inference speedup over previous approaches. Finally, this work releases GangnamLoop, a novel and practical multi-trip outdoor benchmark collected by deploying a real quadruped robot on public streets with the anonymization policy. It revisits the same locations under different outdoor conditions and pairs the robot's low viewpoint with the human owner's. The source codes and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/ndb796/BinaryTracking

</details>


### [233] [Unified Motion-Action Modeling for Heterogeneous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16917)
*Yunhao Cao,Shitong Liu,Chao Feng,Meryl Zhang,Xuanchen Lu,Andrew Owens,Kuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一运动-动作（UMA）模型，利用3D物体运动轨迹作为共享接口，通过掩码生成目标统一视觉运动控制与动力学建模，支持多任务预训练并在多种推理模式下超越专门化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要手动标注任务指令且难以跨异构数据源实现多任务预训练，缺乏统一的框架来桥接视觉运动控制和动力学建模。

Method: 以3D物体运动轨迹作为共享接口，采用掩码生成目标将物体运动与机器人动作视为共同演化变量；利用事后重新标记的运动上下文和对比目标解耦任务意图与场景几何，实现无需手动标注的多任务预训练。

Result: 在机器人演示、人类视频和模拟数据混合预训练后，UMA在运动条件视觉运动控制、基于运动的动力学建模和少样本任务适应三种推理模式下均一致优于专门化的SOTA基线。

Conclusion: UMA验证了物体运动轨迹作为统一接口的有效性，同一预训练参数可直接部署于多种推理任务，无需针对每种模式重新训练。

Abstract: We present Unified Motion-Action (UMA) Model, an approach that uses 3D object motion trajectories as a shared interface to bridge visuomotor control and dynamics modeling. UMA treats object motion and robot actions as co-evolving variables under a masked generative objective, in which the mask pattern determines both the supervision regime during pretraining and the inference mode at deployment. Using hindsight-relabeled motion contexts and a contrastive objective that disentangles task intent from scene geometry, UMA enables multi-task pretraining across heterogeneous data sources without requiring manually annotated task instructions. At deployment, the same pretrained parameters support motion-conditioned visuomotor control, motion-based dynamics modeling, and task adaptation from few-shot demonstrations. Pretrained on a mixture of robot demonstrations, human videos, and simulated data, UMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines specialized for each inference mode.

</details>


### [234] [CrossMaps: Confidence-Aware Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping for Rover Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16935)
*Jan-Niklas Klein,Sona Ghahremani,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrossMaps是一个实时、置信度感知的开放词汇语义建图管线，通过双存储器架构（STM+LTM）融合多尺度CLIP嵌入与置信度线索，在Jetson Orin驱动的UGV上实现语言可查询的语义地图，用于引导漫游车导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫游车需要感知来维护编码对象和传感器质量（如测距可靠性、光照伪影、数据密度）的空间地图，以指导数据融合、嵌入更新和部分可观测下的导航。现有方法缺乏融合置信度感知的实时开放词汇语义建图能力。

Method: CrossMaps基于VLMaps风格方法，集成多尺度CLIP嵌入与置信度感知融合，采用双存储器架构：短期记忆（STM）利用几何、语义和时间置信度线索聚合噪声视觉观测，长期记忆（LTM）存储置信且一致的单元格作为持久语义地标。

Result: 在Jetson Orin驱动的UGV上结合SLAM实现实时运行，产生可通过自然语言查询的语义热力图，用于引导漫游车导航。

Conclusion: CrossMaps通过置信度感知的双存储器架构实现了实时、开放词汇的语义建图，有效耦合了感知与导航过程，支持自然语言查询以指导部分可观测环境下的漫游车导航。

Abstract: Rovers rely on perception to maintain spatial maps that encode both objects and sensor quality (e.g., range reliability, lighting artifacts, data density), guiding data fusion, embedding updates, and navigation under partial observability. To study these coupled perception-navigation processes, we present CrossMaps, a real-time confidence-aware open-vocabulary semantic mapping pipeline that constructs language-queryable maps from RGB-D data. Building on VLMaps-style approaches, CrossMaps integrates multi-scale CLIP embeddings with confidence-aware fusion and a dual-memory architecture consisting of Short-Term Memory (STM) and Long-Term Memory (LTM). The STM aggregates noisy visual observations using geometric, semantic, and temporal confidence cues, while confident and coherent cells are promoted to the LTM as persistent semantic landmarks. Designed for deployment with a Jetson Orin-powered UGV alongside SLAM, CrossMaps runs in real time and produces semantic heatmaps that can be queried with natural language to guide rover navigation.

</details>


### [235] [SidewalkBench: Benchmarking Visual Navigation on Urban Sidewalks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16953)
*Zhizheng Liu,Honglin He,Vivek Alumootil,Akshat Pandya,Brad Squicciarini,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SidewalkBench，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的城市人行道视觉导航统一基准，包含多样化高保真场景、反应式行人行为和标准化评估协议，对9种导航模型在1235个场景中进行评估，发现行人交互和长距离鲁棒性是现有模型的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市人行道导航面临复杂结构布局、动态行人行为和长距离等挑战，而现有视觉导航模型缺乏统一的基准进行定量和可重复评估。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建GPU加速的高保真人行道仿真环境，包含程序生成和真实扫描场景，并引入基于事件的反应式行人行为与灵活动画系统，设计330个单元测试、800个行人反应式和105个长距离场景的标准化评估协议。

Result: 对9种视觉导航模型在1235个场景上的评估表明，行人交互和长距离鲁棒性是现有模型的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 行人交互和长距离鲁棒性是现有模型的关键瓶颈，而利用合成数据扩展人行道训练是一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban sidewalk navigation presents significant challenges due to complex structural layouts, dynamic pedestrian behaviors, and long distances. While recent visual navigation models offer a promising solution, the lack of a unified benchmark hinders quantitative and reproducible evaluation. To bridge this gap, we propose SidewalkBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed for visual navigation on urban sidewalks. Built upon NVIDIA Isaac Sim, SidewalkBench brings GPU-accelerated simulation of diverse, high-fidelity sidewalk environments, including both procedurally generated and real-world scanned scenes. We further populate the scenes with rich, reactive event-based pedestrian behaviors and flexible, efficient animation, enabling standardized model evaluation under realistic real-world settings. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 9 visual navigation models on 330 unit-test scenarios, 800 pedestrian-reactive scenarios, and 105 long-horizon scenarios. Our findings highlight that pedestrian interaction and long-horizon robustness remain critical bottlenecks for existing models, and scaling up sidewalk training with synthetic data emerges as a promising solution.

</details>


### [236] [When Should a Robot Replan? Regret-Guided Update Scheduling in Time-Varying MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16972)
*Negin Musavi,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Ruben Hernandez,William Schafer,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人在非平稳环境中的预算受限自适应控制问题，提出了一种基于动态遗憾的跳过更新策略，并在仿真和真实机器人平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非平稳环境中运行需要不断适应变化的动态，但机载能量和计算预算限制了状态估计和重新规划的频率，因此需要确定在时间轴上何时使用有限的预算进行更新。

Method: 将问题建模为具有转移漂移速率已知上界的时变马尔可夫决策过程（TVMDP），提出跳过更新（skip-update）方案：在选定的更新时刻，通过最大似然估计转移核并计算有限时域策略；在更新间隔内重用该策略并传播状态估计。通过动态遗憾分析导出遗憾增长与TVMDP性质及跳过长度的关系，进而提出基于遗憾的自适应预算分配规则。

Result: 在模拟火星车导航任务（时变滑移动态）和Crazyflie四旋翼飞行器（室内障碍场）上，自适应分配规则优于其他预算有限的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应遗憾引导更新规则能够有效分配有限预算，实现比固定预算基线更好的控制性能。

Abstract: Robots operating in non-stationary environments must continually adapt their policies as the dynamics drift, but onboard energy and compute budgets cap how often a full state estimation and re-planning step can be performed. This raises a question: \emph{when}, along a horizon, should a robot spend its limited budget? We formulate this problem in time-varying Markov decision processes (TVMDPs) with a known bound on the rate of transition drift. We model execution as a \emph{skip-update} scheme in which, at chosen update times, the agent estimates the transition kernel by maximum likelihood and computes a finite-horizon policy, and between updates reuses this policy under a propagated state estimate. We analyze the dynamic regret of this scheme and show how it grows during skip intervals in terms of the properties of the TVMDP and the skip lengths; the resulting bound answers the opening question via an online, regret-guided update rule that allocates the budget adaptively. We evaluate the rule in a simulated Mars-rover navigation task with time-varying slip dynamics and on a Crazyflie quadrotor in indoor obstacle fields. Adaptive allocation outperforms other budgeted baselines.

</details>


### [237] [Task-Error Residual Learning for Real-Robot Five-Ball Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16978)
*Kai Ploeger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差学习的方法，使用方向性任务误差监督和任务误差模型驱动样本选择，实现了在Barrett WAM机械臂上稳定三到五球杂耍。系统从第二次尝试开始收敛，误差单调递减且无后续失败，而人类练习五球杂耍通常需要数年。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中标准标量奖励携带的信息远少于定义任务的方向性任务误差，且随机探索进一步丢弃了每次rollout返回的信息，导致样本效率低下。残差学习需要提高样本效率，关键在于两个因素：每次rollout返回的信息量以及学习器使用这些信息的效率。

Method: 采用残差学习框架，使用方向性任务误差作为监督信号，并结合任务误差模型驱动样本选择。沿两个三元轴比较残差学习器：学习反馈中的方向信息（directional information）和分析先验的承诺程度（commitment of analytic prior），涵盖Newton式雅可比更新、复合贝叶斯优化和随机搜索方法。

Result: 系统从第二次尝试开始收敛，首次尝试失败后任务误差单调递减且不再发生失败。实现了三球、四球和五球稳定杂耍。方向性反馈和 informative prior 单独使用均不足，最可靠的方法是最简单的固定雅可比Newton更新。学习到的残差能容忍显著的先验失配和关节跟踪退化，主要影响收敛速度。

Conclusion: 残差学习在真实机器人上的瓶颈在于监督信号的信息内容以及学习器如何使用这些信息，而非周围堆栈（stack）的精度。两个轴（方向性信息和先验承诺程度）都是必要的。

Abstract: For residual learning that refines existing behavior, sample efficiency depends on two things: how much information each rollout returns, and how efficiently the learner uses that information. Reinforcement learning's standard scalar reward carries far less information than the directional task error that defines the task. Random exploration further discards whatever information each rollout returns. Through residual learning with directional task-error supervision and a task error model that drives sample selection, we achieve stable three-, four-, and five-ball juggling on anthropomorphic Barrett WAM arms. Despite planning and controlling through a simple, idealized stack, the system converges from the second attempt. The first attempt drops, after which task error decreases monotonically without further failures. In comparison, five-ball juggling typically takes humans years of practice. We compare residual learners across two ternary axes, the directional information in the learning feedback and the commitment of the analytic prior, spanning Newton-style Jacobian updates, Composite Bayesian Optimization, and stochastic search methods. Both axes prove necessary: neither directional feedback nor an informative prior suffices alone, and the simplest method that combines them, a fixed-Jacobian Newton update, is the most reliable. The learned residual tolerates substantial prior misalignment and degraded joint tracking, affecting mainly convergence speed. The bottleneck for residual learning on real robots is therefore the information content of the supervision signal and how the learner uses it, not the accuracy of the surrounding stack. Video documentation of all experiments is available at https://kai-ploeger.com/residual-juggling.

</details>


### [238] [ROVE: Unlocking Human Interventions for Humanoid Manipulation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17011)
*Wei Xiao,Weiliang Tang,Yuying Ge,Hui Zhou,Yao Mu,Li Zhang,Yixiao Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROVE是一个针对人形机器人VLA模型后训练的强化学习框架，通过乐观价值估计和跨本体人类经验视频，从不完美的干预轨迹中提取高价值行为，在真实世界接触丰富和精细操作任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类干预为VLA模型后训练提供关键纠偏信号，但人形机器人的全身运动学和灵巧手控制使干预轨迹常次优，现有方法会吸收犹豫、低效甚至错误的行为。

Method: 提出ROVE框架：(1) 人机协同管线收集部署与干预数据；(2) 乐观价值估计(OVE)从混合质量轨迹中优先选择高价值行为；(3) 引入跨本体人类经验视频增强价值估计的鲁棒性，处理长尾失败与恢复模式。

Result: 在真实世界接触丰富和精细人形操作任务上，ROVE超越经验学习基线，并在多次滚动-干预迭代中持续改进。

Conclusion: ROVE通过OVE和跨本体视频监督有效解决了次优人类干预下的学习问题，为人形机器人VLA后训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human interventions provide crucial corrective signals for post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, enabling seamless humanoid interventions is a formidable systems challenge due to complex whole-body kinematics and dexterous-hand control. Consequently, the collected intervention trajectories are often suboptimal, and methods that rely on human interventions as expert supervision can absorb hesitant, inefficient, or even erroneous behaviors. To address both the system and algorithmic challenges, we propose ROVE, a reinforcement learning framework for humanoid VLA post-training with imperfect human interventions. First, ROVE introduces a human-in-the-loop pipeline capable of collecting deployment and intervention data for humanoid manipulation. Second, it utilizes Optimistic Value Estimation (OVE) to prioritize high-value behaviors from mixed-quality trajectories. To further robustify value estimation, we incorporate cross-embodiment human experience videos to provide rich supervision for long-tailed failure and recovery modes. The resulting critic yields informative advantage signals, steering the VLA actor to focus on high-value behaviors rather than indiscriminately imitating all actions. On challenging real-world contact-rich and fine-grained humanoid manipulation tasks, ROVE outperforms experience-learning baselines and consistently improves across multiple rollout-intervention iterations.

</details>


### [239] [R2RDreamer: 3D-aware Data Augmentation for Spatially-generalized 2D Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17040)
*Xiuwei Xu,Haowen Sun,Angyuan Ma,Yiwei Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Bingyao Yu,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2RDreamer提出了一种真实到真实的演示增强框架，通过轻量级3D编辑（点云与轨迹）结合2D视频补全（遮挡感知投影+密集控制图像到视频模型），在保持几何一致性的同时生成时域连贯的RGB观测，有效提升了模仿学习策略在空间泛化任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的空间泛化通常需要大规模多样化演示数据，而现有数据增强方法面临sim-to-real差距或依赖强3D场景解析，且多为3D点云策略设计。作者希望提出一种既能保持3D几何一致性又适用于RGB 2D策略的增强方法。

Method: R2RDreamer首先在共享3D坐标系下对不完整物体点云和机械臂末端轨迹进行轻量级3D几何编辑；然后将编辑后的场景通过遮挡感知投影映射到掩码图像空间的控制视频中；最后利用密集控制图像到视频模型完成时域一致的RGB观测补全。

Result: 在空间偏移的操控任务中，使用2D扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作策略进行实验，结果表明R2RDreamer能够从有限的源演示中显著提升空间泛化能力，消融实验验证了3D编辑、遮挡感知投影和视频补全各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: R2RDreamer通过结合3D几何编辑与2D视频补全，有效弥合了3D增强与2D RGB策略之间的鸿沟，为模仿学习的空间泛化提供了一条无需复杂3D场景解析和sim-to-real转换的实用路径。

Abstract: Spatial generalization is critical for imitation-learned manipulation policies, but achieving it typically requires scaling demonstrations across diverse object poses, robot configurations, and camera viewpoints. Data augmentation from a few source demonstrations offers a practical alternative to costly real-world collection. Simulation-based augmentation can create controllable variation, but requires complex environment and object setup and may introduce a sim-to-real gap. Recent real-to-real methods avoid these issues by jointly editing 3D observations and action trajectories from real demonstrations, yet they still rely on strong 3D scene parsing and geometry completion, and often produce observations tailored to 3D pointcloud policies rather than RGB-based 2D policies. We propose R2RDreamer, a real-to-real demonstration augmentation framework that preserves the geometric consistency of 3D action-observation editing while moving visual completion to 2D video space. Specifically, R2RDreamer first performs lightweight 3D augmentation by editing incomplete object pointclouds and end-effector trajectories in a shared 3D frame; it then projects the edited scene into masked image-space control videos with occlusion-aware reasoning and uses a dense-control image-to-video model to complete temporally coherent RGB observations. Experiments on spatially shifted manipulation tasks with both 2D diffusion-style policies and vision-language-action policies show that R2RDreamer improves spatial generalization from limited source demonstrations, with analyses validating the contributions of 3D editing, occlusion-aware projection, and video completion.

</details>


### [240] [Hierarchical Advantage Weighting for Online RL Fine-Tuning of VLAs from Sparse Episode Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17043)
*Tongyan Fang,Siyuan Huang,Naiyu Fang,Ganlong Zhao,Zhongjin Luo,Jianbo Liu,Xiaogang Wang,Ying Dong,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HABC通过分层优势加权行为克隆，利用独立评论头分别优化可行性和效率，并结合状态自适应门控与干预感知的信用分配，显著提升预训练VLA策略在稀疏奖励下的在线强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预训练VLA策略进行在线RL微调时，每个回合仅产生单一二元结果（成功/失败），但演员更新需要每个时间步的监督。将稀疏结果简化为单一标量奖励或优势信号会混淆不同形式的逐跳反馈，一旦基础任务成功实现后，二元标签无法区分高效与缓慢的完成；同时真实世界滚动混合自主与干预片段，将回合结果跨越这些边界分配会导致错误信用分配。

Method: 提出HABC，训练两个独立的评论头分别针对可行性和效率两个目标，在不同数据子集上训练；通过状态自适应门控g_t融合两者的单步优势，优先考虑可行性直到成功确定后再转向效率；将融合结果转换为逐跳权重作用于演员损失；采用干预感知的信用分配，将结果标签限制在当前策略执行的片段内。

Result: 在三个接触式双臂操作的机器人实验中，HABC将监督微调基线成功率从36%、44%、12%分别提升至92%、88%、38%。

Conclusion: HABC通过解耦可行性与效率两个目标并采用干预感知信用分配，有效解决了稀疏二元奖励下VLA策略在线RL微调中的信用分配歧义问题，显著提升性能。

Abstract: When pretrained VLA policies are fine-tuned through online RL, each rollout episode produces only a single binary outcome (success or failure), yet the actor update requires per-transition supervision. Existing approaches commonly reduce this sparse outcome to a single scalar reward or advantage signal, which conflates distinct forms of transition-level feedback and provides limited guidance once basic task success becomes achievable. First, a single scalar signal conflates the two objectives of viability and efficiency; once basic success is achieved, the binary label provides no gradient to distinguish efficient completions from slow ones. Second, real-world rollouts mix autonomous and intervention segments; naively assigning episode outcomes across these boundaries introduces incorrect credit assignment. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC), which trains separate critic heads for these two objectives on different data subsets and combines their outputs with a state-adaptive balance. A state-adaptive gate $g_t$ merges their one-step advantages, prioritizing viability when success is uncertain and shifting to efficiency only when viability is high, and converts the result into per-transition weights on the actor loss. Intervention-aware credit assignment further restricts outcome labels to segments executed by the current policy, preventing supervision from leaking across intervention boundaries. In real-robot experiments on three contact-rich bimanual tasks, HABC raises success from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines of 36%, 44%, and 12% to 92%, 88%, and 38%.

</details>


### [241] [Geometric Action Model for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17046)
*Jisang Han,Seonghu Jeon,Jaewoo Jung,René Zurbrügg,Honggyu An,Tifanny Portela,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Seungryong Kim,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAM是一种语言条件操控策略，通过复用预训练几何基础模型(GFM)作为共享骨干，在中间层拆分GFM，浅层作为观察编码器，插入因果未来预测器预测未来潜在token，再通过剩余GFM模块解码，实现语言条件的时间世界建模，在仿真和真实机器人操控任务中优于当前基础模型规模基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)和视频世界-动作模型(WAM)虽然继承了强大的语义或时间先验，但主要在2D图像或2D衍生潜在空间上操作，缺乏接触式操控所需的3D几何信息。

Method: 在预训练几何基础模型(GFM)的中间层进行拆分：浅层作为观察编码器，在拆分点插入因果未来预测器，基于语言、本体感知和动作历史预测未来潜在token，然后将预测token通过剩余GFM块进行特征传播和解码，从而用单一骨干同时生成未来几何和动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操控基准测试中，GAM比当前基础模型规模基线更准确、更鲁棒、更快、更轻量。

Conclusion: GAM通过最小化架构修改，使GFM具备语言条件的时间世界建模能力，同时保留其丰富的几何先验，实现了更优的操控性能。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies must follow user instructions while reasoning about how objects, cameras, and robot actions interact in the 3D physical world. Recent vision-language-action models (VLAs) and video world-action models (WAMs) inherit strong semantic or temporal priors from large-scale foundation models, but they still operate primarily on 2D image frames or 2D-derived latent spaces, leaving implicit the 3D geometry required for contact-rich manipulation. We propose the Geometric Action Model (GAM), a language-conditioned manipulation policy that directly repurposes a pretrained geometric foundation model (GFM) as a shared substrate for perception, temporal prediction, and action decoding. GAM splits the GFM at an intermediate layer: the shallow layers serve as an observation encoder, and a causal future predictor inserted at the split layer forecasts future latent tokens conditioned on language, proprioception, and action history. The predicted future tokens are then routed through the remaining GFM blocks for feature propagation and decoding, allowing a single backbone to produce both future geometry and actions. This design equips the GFM with language-conditioned temporal world modeling through minimal architectural modification while preserving its rich geometric priors. Across a broad suite of simulation and real-robot manipulation benchmarks, GAM is more accurate, more robust, faster, and lighter than current foundation-model-scale baselines.

</details>


### [242] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HUG，一种基于流匹配模型的人手抓取生成方法，利用人类日常抓取数据（1M-HUGs数据集，包含100万帧、6707个物体实例）实现从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化人手抓取姿态，并可重定向至机器人手，在零样本场景中实现抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人的抓取能力远不及人类，而人类每天抓取数千物体。作者认为最自然的机器人抓取数据来源是人类本身，因此希望利用人类抓取数据来指导机器人抓取。

Method: 1) 使用智能眼镜收集1M-HUGs数据集（第一人称视角，100万帧，6707物体，41栋建筑）；2) 提出流匹配模型，融合RGB和深度观测，输出参数化抓取（腕部平移、腕部旋转、MANO手部姿态）；3) 预测的抓取可重定向至各种机器人手。

Result: 在HUG-Bench基准（90个未见物体）上，HUG在真实世界30物体测试集上分别比SOTA抓取基线高出+23%和+34%。

Conclusion: HUG通过大规模人类抓取数据学习，实现了从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化、可迁移到机器人的抓取姿态，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


### [243] [T-Rex: Tactile-Reactive Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17055)
*Dantong Niu,Zhuoyang Liu,Zekai Wang,Boning Shao,Zhao-Heng Yin,Anirudh Pai,Yuvan Sharma,Stefano Saravalle,Ruijie Zheng,Jing Wang,Ryan Punamiya,Mengda Xu,Yuqi Xie,Yunfan Jiang,Letian Fu,Konstantinos Kallidromitis,Matteo Gioia,Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Haiwen Feng,Fabio Galasso,Wei Zhan,David M. Chan,Yutong Bai,Roei Herzig,Jiahui Lei,Fei-Fei Li,Ken Goldberg,Jitendra Malik,Pieter Abbeel,Yuke Zhu,Danfei Xu,Jim,Fan,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人操作中缺乏触觉反应能力的问题，提出了大规模触觉数据集、可变速率混合Transformer架构（MoT）和时间触觉VQ-VAE编码器，在12个精细操作任务上实现了超过30%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人操作中普遍忽略触觉模态或仅限于静态触觉编码器，主要受限于训练数据稀缺、标准化评估缺失、VLA架构约束以及静态触觉编码器的局限性。作者旨在突破这些限制，推动触觉反应式操作的前沿发展。

Method: 1) 构建了100小时大规模触觉丰富数据集，采用优先基础运动基元的数据高效收集策略；2) 提出可变速率混合Transformer（MoT）架构，配备新型时间触觉VQ-VAE编码器，以有效利用高频触觉信号同时保持VLA现有能力。

Result: 在12个需要精细力控制与可变形物体操作的任务上，触觉反应式策略比最强基线平均成功率提升超过30%。

Conclusion: 触觉模态的引入能够显著提升机器人精细操作能力，本文提出的数据收集方法与架构设计为触觉-语言-动作模型的有效整合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ability to react dynamically to tactile signals has long been considered crucial to agile human-level dexterity. Yet contemporary learning-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation generally either overlook the tactile modality or are limited to encoders with static cues, due in part to the scarcity of diverse training data and standardized evaluation, architectural constraints in current VLA models, and limitations of static tactile encoders. In this paper, we push the frontier of tactile-reactive manipulation by addressing all of these limitations. We propose a large-scale, 100-hour tactile-rich dataset collected via a novel, data-efficient recipe that prioritizes elementary motor primitives. To effectively exploit naturally high-frequency touch signals without sacrificing the existing capabilities of existing VLAs, we introduce a variable-rate Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture equipped with a novel temporal tactile VQ-VAE encoder. We demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-reactive policies on 12 manipulation tasks requiring delicate force control and deformable object manipulation, achieving over 30% higher average success rate than the strongest baseline.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [On the Equivalence of Instantaneous and Mechanistic Reproduction Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15397)
*Jeremy Goldwasser,Ryan J. Tibshirani,Alyssa Bilinski*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文证明了在均匀混合假设下，瞬时Rt（基于更新方程）与机制Rt（基于SEIR等仓室模型）是等价的，并推导了SEIR动态隐含的世代间隔分布。研究表明，世代间隔实际上编码了特定的仓室结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病学中存在两种看似不同的Rt定义（瞬时Rt与机制Rt），且在世代间隔的假设上存在模糊性，作者旨在厘清两者关系并揭示世代间隔与仓室模型的深层联系。

Method: 通过数学推导，在均匀混合假设下证明瞬时Rt与SEIR模型中的机制Rt等价，并推导出SEIR动态所隐含的世代间隔分布表达式。

Result: 证明了两种Rt定义在均匀混合假设下的等价性，并给出了SEIR动态对应的世代间隔分布。

Conclusion: 世代间隔并非假设较少的输入量，而是隐含了特定的仓室模型结构，这对实际流行病建模中参数的选择具有重要启示。

Abstract: The effective reproduction number ($R_t$) is widely used to track epidemic dynamics in real time. The standard estimation framework uses "instantaneous $R_t$," defined via the renewal equation, which relates new infections to past infections through a generation interval distribution. Compartmental models like SEIR yield a seemingly distinct quantity, "mechanistic $R_t$," based on the effective contact rate and duration of infectiousness. We prove these two definitions are equivalent under homogeneous mixing, the standard assumption in compartmental modeling. We also derive the generation interval distribution implied by SEIR dynamics. A practical consequence is that generation intervals, often treated as assumption-light inputs to renewal equation estimators, in fact encode specific compartmental structure.

</details>


### [245] [How recombination rates affect escape from low-fitness states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15496)
*Elisa Heinrich-Mora,Chase Van Amburg,Marcus W. Feldman*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了三基因座模型（两个选择位点+一个重组修饰位点）中，重组率如何进化并影响群体越过适应度低谷的能力。结果表明，修饰子的多态性可以稳定或 destabilize 低适应度状态，且具有相同选择单倍型频率的群体可能因修饰子组成不同而具有不同的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解群体如何在存在不利突变组合的情况下逃离低适应度遗传状态，以及重组率本身如何进化并影响这一过程。

Method: 构建三基因座模型（两个选择位点+重组修饰子位点），分析系统的平衡状态，推导低适应度固定集的局部稳定性条件。

Result: 低适应度状态的稳定性取决于选择强度、选择位点间的重组率、修饰子与选择位点间的重组率以及修饰子组成。与经典二基因座模型不同，修饰子模型产生一个连续统的固定状态，其稳定性随修饰子频率变化。

Conclusion: 影响重组的遗传变异不仅通过改变有利多基因座组合的形成来改变进化结果，还通过改变替代进化状态的稳定性来起作用。

Abstract: Adaptation often requires the assembly of favorable combinations of mutations that are individually deleterious. As a result, populations may remain trapped in low-fitness genetic states even when higher-fitness genotypes exist. Recombination plays a dual role in this process because it can both generate and disrupt advantageous multilocus combinations. Previous work showed that the balance between selection and recombination determines whether populations cross fitness valleys or persist in low-fitness states associated with demographic decline. We study this problem in a three-locus model consisting of two selected loci and a recombination modifier locus. The modifier has no direct effect on fitness but alters the recombination rate between the selected loci, allowing recombination itself to evolve. We characterize the fixation states of the system and derive explicit conditions for the local stability of the low-fitness fixation set. Stability depends on selection strength, recombination among selected loci, recombination between the modifier and selected loci, and modifier composition. In the classical two-locus model, stability depends on a single recombination parameter. By contrast, the modifier model generates a continuum of fixation states whose stability varies with modifier frequency. Populations with identical selected-haplotype frequencies can therefore differ in stability solely because they differ in modifier composition. We further show that modifier polymorphism can either stabilize or destabilize the low-fitness state, depending on the relative magnitudes of modifier-dependent recombination rates. These results demonstrate that genetic variation affecting recombination alters evolutionary outcomes not only by changing the formation of favorable multilocus combinations but also by changing the stability of alternative evolutionary states.

</details>


### [246] [Self-propelled evolution on regenerating landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15530)
*Alexander Heyde,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小生态进化模型，通过单一环境敏感性参数耦合群体复制动力学与可再生资源驱动的景观，揭示了群体如何在初始平坦的适应度景观上产生并利用自我诱导的选择梯度，实现定向演化，并展现出持续振荡、混沌动力学和进化分支等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统适应度景观理论将景观视为静态的，但实际进化中的群体既响应环境又重塑环境。本文旨在建立一个最小模型来捕捉这种双向耦合，解释群体如何在动态景观中实现自我驱动的定向演化。

Method: 通过单一环境敏感性参数将群体密度的复制动力学与可再生资源驱动的景观相耦合，构建最小生态进化模型。推导出约化动力学方程，将费舍尔基本定理推广至可变形的适应度景观，引入曲率驱动的方差动力学和环境反馈项。

Result: 模型展现出持续振荡、混沌动力学和进化分支等复杂动态行为；群体能够在初始平坦的景观上产生并沿自我诱导的选择梯度定向移动；推导的约化方程成功解释了这些现象。

Conclusion: 群体能够主动重塑环境并在再生性景观上进行自我推进，这表明适应度景观本质上是由生态进化反馈动态生成的，而非预先给定的静态结构。这扩展了我们对进化动力学的理解。

Abstract: Evolving populations both respond to and reshape their environments, making fitness landscapes dynamic rather than static. We present a minimal eco-evolutionary model that couples replicator dynamics for a population density with a regenerating resource-driven landscape through a single environmental sensitivity parameter. This allows evolving populations to generate and ride self-induced selection gradients, enabling directed motion in trait space even on initially flat landscapes. Our analysis reveals sustained oscillations, chaotic dynamics, and evolutionary branching. To explain these, we derive reduced dynamical equation that extend Fisher's fundamental theorem to deformable landscapes by incorporating curvature-driven variance dynamics and environmental feedback. Together, these results show how populations actively reshape and self-propel themselves on regenerating landscapes.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Polarization Signatures of Rotating Black Holes in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14736)
*Yu-Xiang Huang,Sen Guo,En-Wei Liang,Kuan Liu,Kai Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过相对论性射线追踪模拟，研究了沉浸于完美流体暗物质（PFDM）背景中的旋转黑洞的偏振辐射特征，发现暗物质参数k会系统性改变偏振角分布、净线偏振分数及大尺度偏振相位，表明暗物质可在黑洞偏振信号中留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受M87*视界尺度偏振观测启发，探究PFDM背景对旋转黑洞偏振结构的影响，检验暗物质是否能在黑洞偏振观测中留下可探测信号。

Method: 采用完全相对论性射线追踪方法，模拟不同磁場几何、高阶成像及PFDM强度参数k下的偏振辐射传输过程，并对偏振图像进行分解分析。

Result: 1) 含极向分量的磁场构型产生连续螺旋EVPA模式，大尺度EVPA形态由磁场拓扑主导；2) k值变化影响近邻光子环区域的EVPA偏转和偏振强度；3) 高阶像在环附近提供局域但不可忽略的修正；4) PFDM相较于纯Kerr黑洞，降低净线偏振分数|m|_net并改变大尺度偏振相位argβ₂。

Conclusion: PFDM作为额外的强引力成分，系统性重组视界尺度偏振结构，暗示周围暗物质可在黑洞偏振信号中留下可观测的特征。

Abstract: Motivated by horizon-scale polarization observations of M87^{*}, we investigate polarized emission from rotating black holes (BHs) immersed in a perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) background. Fully relativistic ray tracing is utilized to examine how the polarization structure jointly depends on magnetic-field geometry, higher-order imaging, and the PFDM intensity parameter k. We show that, for a given k value, magnetic configurations with a polar component naturally produce a continuous spiral pattern in the electric vector position angle (EVPA), while the large-scale EVPA morphology remains primarily controlled by the magnetic-field topology. Variations in k affect light propagation and polarization transport near the horizon, leading to corresponding changes in EVPA deflection and polarized intensity around the photon ring. A decomposition of the total polarized image further reveals that higher-order images provide localized yet non-negligible corrections near the ring. Compared with the polarization characteristics of M87^{*} inferred by the EHT, image-domain quantities such as |m|_{\rm net} and \argβ_2 indicate that PFDM induces systematic shifts relative to the pure Kerr BH case, lowering the net linear polarization fraction and modifying the large-scale polarization phase. Overall, PFDM acts as an additional strong-gravity ingredient that systematically reorganizes the horizon-scale polarization structure, suggesting that surrounding dark matter can leave observable imprints on black hole polarization signatures.

</details>


### [248] [Centaurus A Inner Lobes -- I. Hydrodynamic modeling of a Precessing Jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14807)
*Rita C. Anjos,Brian Reville*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维相对论流体动力学模拟，研究了半人马座A内瓣进动射流的动力学演化及其与周围介质的相互作用。模拟采用物理驱动参数，成功再现了观测到的S形形态、明亮发射区分布及南北瓣不对称性，并确定进动周期为1.8 Myr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究射流进动在半人马座A内瓣形态塑造中的作用，理解大尺度射流和瓣结构的动力学演化机制。

Method: 采用三维相对论流体动力学（RHD）模拟，构建由进动驱动的大尺度射流和瓣结构演化模型，使用物理驱动参数并对比射电观测图像。

Result: 确定了1.8 Myr的进动周期能够再现观测特征，包括S形形态、明亮发射区分布以及南北瓣的不对称性。

Conclusion: 射流进动是塑造半人马座A内瓣结构的关键机制，模拟结果与观测高度吻合。

Abstract: We present a numerical investigation into the precessing jets of the inner lobes of Centaurus A, focusing on their dynamical evolution and interaction with the surrounding medium. Using three-dimensional relativistic hydrodynamic simulations, we model the development of large-scale jet and lobe structures driven by precession. Our setup incorporates physically motivated parameters to reproduce observed morphological features. We compare the resulting structures from our simulations with observed radio images of Centaurus A, particularly focusing on the S-shaped morphology, the distribution of bright emission regions, and the observed asymmetry between the northern and southern lobes. Our findings indicate a precession period of 1.8 Myr, which reproduces observational characteristics. This study explores the role of jet precession in shaping the inner lobes of Centaurus A.

</details>


### [249] [Red Giant Destruction by Stellar and Black Hole Collisions in Galactic Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14843)
*Barak Rom,Eliot Quataert*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究了星系核致密环境中红巨星与主序星或恒星级黑洞碰撞的影响，发现高速碰撞会剥离红巨星包层，产生低质量氦白矮星，并影响银心红巨星的投影密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密星系核环境中红巨星与不同类型天体的碰撞过程及其天体物理后果。

Method: 通过解析估计，分析红巨星与主序星及恒星级黑洞碰撞时，由冲压压力、吸积驱动激波导致的包层剥离，以及引力反冲导致的氦核抛射等机制。

Result: 高速碰撞（~10³ km/s）能有效耗竭红巨星种群；低速时恒星级黑洞碰撞主导但整体破坏率低。碰撞产生约500 Gyr⁻¹的低质量氦白矮星，这些天体可在~10⁵-⁶ M☉大质量黑洞周围产生有趣的白矮星潮汐瓦解事件。在银心，红巨星投影密度在~1角秒内变平，较暗红巨星主要受碰撞影响，较亮红巨星主要受短寿命影响。

Conclusion: 红巨星碰撞是星系核动力学演化的重要过程，能产生特殊类型的致密天体和潮汐瓦解事件，对理解银心恒星种群分布有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the impact of collisions involving red giants (RGs) in the dense stellar environments of galactic nuclei. We analytically estimate when collisions with main-sequence stars or stellar-mass black holes can strip a RG's envelope via ram pressure or accretion-driven shocks, or eject its helium core through gravitational recoil. At high velocities, $v\gtrsim10^3~{\rm km/s}$, collisions with main-sequence stars efficiently deplete the RG population. At lower velocities, collisions with stellar-mass BHs typically dominate over stellar encounters, but the overall RG destruction rate is low and does not significantly affect the RG population. Nonetheless, these collisions produce low-mass helium white dwarfs, which are the stripped cores of the disrupted RGs, at a rate of $\sim 500~~{\rm Gyr}^{-1}$. Helium white dwarfs can produce an interesting class of white dwarf tidal disruption events around $\sim 10^{5-6} M_\odot$ massive black holes where Carbon-Oxygen white dwarfs cannot be tidally disrupted outside the horizon. Applied to our own Galactic Center, we quantify the impact of collisions on the observed population of RGs, as well as the effects of their intrinsic scarcity due to short RG lifetimes. We find that the RG projected density flattens within $\sim1$'', primarily due to collisions for fainter RGs and their short lifetimes for more luminous RGs.

</details>


### [250] [A Multimessenger Analysis of the High-Energy Milky Way: Source Populations Contribute Significantly to IceCube's Galactic Neutrino Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14848)
*Alisha Roberts,Ilias Cholis,Dan Hooper,Samyak Jain*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对IceCube观测到的银河系平面高能中微子辐射与LHAASO测量的弥散超高能伽马射线进行联合分析，发现伽马射线可由星际介质中的宇宙线相互作用和未分辨的TeV晕共同解释，而中微子辐射则无法仅由宇宙线相互作用产生，必须来自未分辨的源群体，且银河系中微子源很可能贡献了大部分辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解银河系平面高能中微子和伽马射线的起源，区分弥散过程与未分辨点源（如TeV晕、中微子源）的贡献，揭示中微子辐射的主要来源。

Method: 联合分析IceCube的银河系平面中微子数据和LHAASO的弥散超高能伽马射线数据，构建包含三种成分（星际介质宇宙线相互作用、未分辨TeV晕、未分辨银河系中微子源）的模型进行比较。

Result: 伽马射线可由弥散过程与TeV晕共同解释；中微子辐射不能单独由宇宙线相互作用产生，需要未分辨源贡献；银河系中微子源在多种宇宙线输运假设下均贡献显著，很可能是主要成分。

Conclusion: 银河系中微子源对IceCube观测到的银河系平面中微子通量贡献显著，很可能是该辐射的主导来源。

Abstract: We perform a joint analysis of the high-energy neutrino emission observed from the Galactic Plane by IceCube and the diffuse ultra-high-energy gamma-ray emission measured by LHAASO. We compare this data to models that include diffuse emission from cosmic-ray interactions in the interstellar medium, unresolved TeV halos, and unresolved Galactic neutrino sources. We find that the gamma-ray emission can be explained by a combination of diffuse processes and unresolved TeV halos. The observed neutrino emission cannot be generated by cosmic-ray interactions in the interstellar medium alone, but requires contributions from one or more unresolved source populations. Across a wide range of assumptions about Galactic cosmic-ray transport, we find that Galactic neutrino sources contribute significantly to the neutrino flux observed from the Galactic Plane and are likely responsible for most of this emission.

</details>


### [251] [Short Spike, Long Story: Episode-Dependent Shifts of Long-Duration Type-I GRBs on $E_{\rm p,z}$--$E_{\rm iso}$ Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15098)
*Ya-Hui Jiang,Run-Chao Chen,Zhen-Yu Yan,Kamil Nadaf,Xiao-Hong Zhao,Bin-Bin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过分离长时I型GRB的初始尖峰和延展发射，分析其在Amati关系图上的位置，发现初始尖峰符合I型（并合起源），而延展发射偏向II型（塌缩星起源），提示仅依赖Amati关系可能导致分类误判，需结合多波段观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于T₉₀的GRB分类无法唯一对应前身星类型，特别是一些长时I型GRB（并合起源）的持续时间远超2秒阈值。需要超越持续时间的诊断工具来识别这类暴的物理起源。

Method: 选取8个长时I型GRB及并合候选体样本，将初始尖峰与延展发射分离，分别分析它们在E_{p,z}-E_{iso}平面（Amati关系）上的位置，并结合时间分辨光谱分析。

Result: 初始尖峰位于或接近经验I型区域，符合致密并合起源；延展发射系统性地偏向II型区域，若单独分析可能被误判为塌缩星起源。这种I型到II型的转变在时间分辨光谱分析中得到进一步支持，但不同暴的转变幅度和轨迹存在差异。

Conclusion: 仅依赖Amati关系进行经验分类可能产生误导，尤其在初始硬尖峰微弱、位于仪器带宽外或完全缺失时。广泛的时域和能谱覆盖以及独立的多波段诊断对识别这些事件的物理起源至关重要。

Abstract: Observations of peculiar GRBs have challenged the traditional $T_{90}$-based classification, demonstrating that duration does not map uniquely onto progenitor type. A striking class is long-duration Type~I GRBs -- merger-origin events whose prompt emission lasts far longer than the canonical 2 s boundary, typically comprising an initial short hard spike followed by softer extended emission. Identifying the physical origin of such bursts requires diagnostics beyond duration alone, among which the Amati relation, linking rest-frame spectral peak energy $E_{\rm p,z}$ and isotropic-equivalent energy $E_{\rm iso}$, is widely used as a complementary classification tool. We analyze a sample of eight long-duration Type~I GRBs and merger candidates by separating the initial spike from the extended emission and examining their episode-dependent locations on the $E_{\rm p,z}$--$E_{\rm iso}$ plane. We find that the initial spike generally lies within, or close to, the empirical Type~I region, consistent with a compact-merger-like prompt-emission component. In contrast, the extended-emission episode systematically occupies a region closer to Type~II GRBs, and could be misidentified as collapsar-like if analyzed in isolation. This episode-dependent Type~I-to-Type~II transition is further supported by time-resolved spectral analysis, although its magnitude and trajectory vary among bursts, suggesting diversity in central-engine evolution or outflow properties between the two phases. Our results caution that the Amati relation alone can lead to misleading empirical classification when the initial hard spike is weak, outside the instrumental bandpass, or missed entirely, leaving only the extended emission to be analyzed. Broad temporal and spectral coverage, and independent multi-wavelength diagnostics, is therefore essential for identifying the physical origin of these events.

</details>


### [252] [Relativistic Accretion Flow in a Generic Class of Spherically Symmetric Static Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15143)
*Pradeepkumar Yadav,Sayan Chakrabarti,Santabrata Das*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有高阶1/r修正的静态球对称时空中，低角动量、无粘、对流吸积流的性质。通过求解相对论流体动力学方程，获得了全局跨音速吸积解（O、A、W、I型），分析了时空参数（δ, η, β）和流参数（比能ℰ、角动量λ）对临界点结构的影响，并计算了热轫致辐射的谱能量分布（SED），发现δ增强会提升SED亮度，且含内临界点的I型解比仅含外临界点的O/A型解辐射更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正引力理论下（特别是含高阶1/r修正的静态球对称时空）低角动量、无粘吸积流的动力学特性，并将时空几何与可观测信号（谱能量分布）联系起来。

Method: 采用包含高达1/r四次方阶修正的静态球对称度规，自洽求解相对论流体动力学方程，得到全局跨音速吸积解；在δ-λ和λ-ℰ参数空间中标定多临界点解存在区域；计算热轫致辐射的谱能量分布。

Result: 1) 吸积流根据参数可呈现单临界点或多临界点结构；2) 阐明了多临界点解存在的参数空间区域及其随δ的演化；3) 增大δ使SED相对于Schwarzschild情形增强；4) 含内临界点的I型全局跨音速解比仅含外临界点的O/A型解具有更高的辐射功率。

Conclusion: 高阶修正参数δ显著影响吸积流的临界点结构和辐射效率，I型内临界点解在辐射特征上优于O/A型解，为通过吸积观测检验修正引力理论提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of low angular momentum, inviscid, advective accretion flows in a generic static and spherically symmetric spacetime that incorporates higher-order corrections up to the fourth order in $1/r$. Employing this metric, we self-consistently solve the relativistic hydrodynamical equations and obtain the family of global transonic accretion solutions ($O$, $A$, $W$ and $I$-types) by means of the spacetime parameters ($δ$, $η$, $β$) and the flow parameters (specific energy $\mathscr{E}$ and angular momentum $λ$). Our analysis reveals that the accretion flow possesses either single or multiple critical points depending on these input parameters. We delineate the regions of the $δ-λ$ and $λ-\mathscr{E}$ parameter spaces that admits solutions with multiple critical points and demonstrate how these regions evolve with increasing spacetime parameter $δ$. Furthermore, while connecting the spacetime geometry with observable signatures, we compute the spectral energy distribution (SED) from thermal bremsstrahlung emission and observe that increasing $δ$ enhances the SED relative to the Schwarzschild case. Finally, we find that global transonic solutions harbouring inner critical points ($I$-types) yields more luminous power than those with only outer critical points ($O$ and $A$ types).

</details>


### [253] [Mapping Domain-Wall Bayesian Reconstruction with LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15387)
*Satyabrata Datta,Rome Samanta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究LISA探测器对尖峰型畴壁引力波信号的贝叶斯重建，通过构建信号参数平面的重建图，发现当湮灭温度在10^3–10^6 GeV范围内时重建效果最佳，信噪比≥50的信号可实现双参数精确重建，信噪比~10的信号在有限参数空间内仍可有效重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了定量评估LISA探测器对尖峰型畴壁引力波信号的重建能力，确定在哪些参数空间内信号可以被有效恢复，以及参数简并程度和不确定性大小，从而为粒子物理中预言毫赫兹频段尖峰引力波谱的畴壁模型提供观测指导。

Method: 采用双参数畴壁谱模板，包含银河系双白矮星致密双星和河外致密双星的各向同性未调制天体物理前景以及LISA仪器噪声。在等距网格上设置64个注入点进行嵌套采样推断，利用Clough–Tocher方法插值生成平滑的参数平面重建图。

Result: LISA在湮灭温度约10^3–10^6 GeV范围内重建畴壁信号最有效，此区域后验分布更紧、简并度更低，可实现真正的双参数重建。最有利区域对应SNR≥50；SNR~10的信号仅在T_*≲10^5 GeV的较窄参数空间内可有效重建，对应峰值振幅Ω_GW^peak h^2 ≳ 4×10^{-11}，峰值频率f_p ≲ 10–20 mHz。

Conclusion: 本文提供了LISA频段内尖峰型畴壁信号的定量重建预测，为预测毫赫兹频段尖峰引力波谱的畴壁粒子物理实现提供了有用指导。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian reconstruction of peaked domain-wall gravitational-wave signals at LISA and construct reconstruction maps over the signal-parameter plane. These maps identify the regions in which the signal can be probed with minimal posterior uncertainty and parameter degeneracy. Our analysis employs a two-parameter domain-wall spectral template and includes isotropic, unmodulated astrophysical foregrounds from Galactic double white-dwarf binaries and extra-galactic compact binaries, together with LISA instrumental noise. The inference is performed for 64 injection points distributed on an equidistant grid using nested sampling, and the resulting posterior quantities are interpolated with the Clough--Tocher method to generate smooth maps over the full parameter plane. We find that LISA reconstructs domain-wall signals most effectively when the annihilation temperature lies approximately in the range $10^3\text{--}10^6\,\mathrm{GeV}$. In this regime, the posterior becomes both tighter and less degenerate, enabling genuine two-parameter reconstruction. The most favorable region corresponds to signals with ${\rm SNR}\gtrsim 50$, while signals with ${\rm SNR}\sim 10$ can still be reconstructed effectively only in a narrower part of parameter space concentrated near $T_*\lesssim 10^5\,\mathrm{GeV}$. In terms of the observable spectrum, this weaker-signal region corresponds approximately to peak amplitudes $Ω_{\rm GW}^{\rm peak}h^2 \gtrsim 4\times10^{-11}$ and peak frequencies typically satisfying $f_p\lesssim 10\text{--}20\,{\rm mHz}$. Our results provide a quantitative reconstruction forecast for peaked domain-wall signals in the LISA band and a useful guide for particle-physics realizations of domain walls that predict peaked gravitational-wave spectra in the milli-Hz range.

</details>


### [254] [Comparison of X-ray Emission Properties of TDEs and Soft Flares from AGNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15388)
*Samaresh Mondal,K. Decker French,Jason T. Hinkle*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过交叉匹配XMM-Newton和Swift-XRT的X射线源目录与类星体及光学TDE样本，发现活动星系核（AGN）在耀发事件中会变得较软，其硬度比（HR）与TDE相似，这种软X射线性质源于~2 keV以下的类黑体成分（可能与软X射线过剩有关）以及吸积率增加导致的连续谱变陡。统计显示，XMM-Newton中2.5%（23/920）和Swift-XRT中4.4%（179/4089）的AGN表现出符合特定标准的耀发，估算的耀发率在(0.9-2.5)×10⁻³ galaxy⁻¹ yr⁻¹量级。更极端的耀发（峰值流量变化>20倍，HR<-0.75）发生率约为(3.1-11.0)×10⁻⁵ galaxy⁻¹ yr⁻¹。通过ZTF光变和WISE颜色确认了61%-79%的耀发源为AGN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN的X射线耀发活动可能模拟潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）的预期辐射，从而污染TDE搜索。为区分两者，需系统比较TDE与AGN的X射线发射特性。

Method: 将XMM-Newton和Swift-XRT的点源目录与百万类星体目录及光学选择的TDE样本进行交叉匹配，分析硬度比（HR）、光谱成分和耀发率，并通过ZTF光变曲线和WISE颜色确认AGN身份。

Result: AGN在耀发期间HR变软，与TDE相似，源于~2 keV以下的类黑体成分和连续谱变陡。XMM-Newton和Swift-XRT中分别有2.5%和4.4%的AGN表现出耀发，耀发率为(0.9-2.5)×10⁻³ galaxy⁻¹ yr⁻¹。极端耀发率约为(3.1-11.0)×10⁻⁵ galaxy⁻¹ yr⁻¹。61%-79%的耀发源通过光学/红外确认。

Conclusion: AGN耀发在X射线硬度比上与TDE相似，可能污染TDE搜索。通过光学或红外对应体（如ZTF光变或WISE颜色）有助于区分两者。

Abstract: X-ray flaring activity from active galactic nuclei (AGNs) may mimic the expected emission from tidal disruption events (TDEs), thus contaminating TDE searches. To compare X-ray emission properties between TDEs and AGNs, we cross-match publicly available XMM-Newton and Swift-XRT point source catalogs with the Million Quasars Catalog and optically selected TDEs. We find that AGNs tend to become softer with a similar hardness ratio (HR) value as TDEs during their flaring events. The soft HRs during flaring events in these AGNs are driven by the emergence of a blackbody-like component below $\sim$2 keV, likely associated with the soft X-ray excess, as well as the continuum emission becoming steeper with the increase of accretion rate. We find 2.5% in XMM-Newton (23 out of 920) and 4.4% in Swift-XRT (179 out of 4089) AGNs display flares with peak count rate $>$$2σ$ from the median count rate and peak HR$<$$-0.75$.
