The rate of such flares in XMM-Newton and Swift-XRT is (1.1-2.5)$\times10^{-3}\rm\ galaxy^{-1}\ yr^{-1}$ and (0.9-2.0)$\times10^{-3}\rm\ galaxy^{-1}\ yr^{-1}$, respectively. We also estimate the rate of flares with a maximum flux change (peak/min) of $>20\times$ over on a rest-frame time scale of two years or more with a HR$<$$-0.75$ at peak to be (4.7-11.0)$\times10^{-5}\rm\ galaxy^{-1}\ yr^{-1}$ and (3.1-7.1)$\times10^{-5}\rm\ galaxy^{-1}\ yr^{-1}$ in XMM-Newton and Swift-XRT, respectively. Finding an optical or infrared counterpart may help to identify these flaring AGNs. We confirm 61% and 79% of flaring sources from XMM-Newton and Swift-XRT, respectively, as AGNs through variability in ZTF light curves or a WISE $W1$$-$$W2$$>$0.8 mag color cut.

</details>


### [255] [Constraining hydrodynamic model of nearby type IIP SN 2023ixf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15395)
*V. P. Utrobin,N. N. Chugai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用更全面的观测数据重新对SN 2023ixf进行流体动力学建模，得到爆炸能量2.8×10^51 erg、抛射质量13.2 M☉、前身星半径1540 R☉、Ni-56质量0.07 M☉，并首次从爆发到硬X射线辐射全过程模拟了该超新星现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有流体动力学模型给出的爆炸能量和抛射质量范围过宽，需要利用更广泛的观测数据来消除参数简并，从而更精确地约束SN 2023ixf的物理参数。

Method: 基于更全面的观测数据集（优先考虑早期最大抛射速度以消除参数简并），采用辐射流体动力学方法，从爆发到硬X射线辐射出现进行全过程模拟。

Result: 得到SN 2023ixf的爆炸能量为2.8×10^51 erg、抛射质量为13.2 M☉、前身星半径为1540 R☉、Ni-56质量为0.07 M☉；周介质由致密壳层（质量0.01 M☉，半径5×10^14 cm）和外部稀薄风组成。

Conclusion: 两个周介质成分均与早期H-α宽线翼（由汤姆孙散射导致）及X射线数据提供的柱密度一致；首次成功实现了从爆发到硬X射线辐射的完整模拟。

Abstract: Despite proximity of SN 2023ixf and a wealth of observational data, the released hydrodynamic models leave too broad range of the derived explosion energy and the ejected mass. We revisit the hydrodynamic modeling based on a broader set of observables than have been previously used. Among those of top priority is the early maximum ejecta velocity that is crucial in removing parameter degeneracy. The inferred parameters of SN 2023ixf are the explosion energy of 2.8x10^{51} erg, ejecta mass of 13.2 Msun, presupernova radius of 1540 Rsun, and Ni-56 mass of 0.07 Msun. The circumstellar matter is composed by the dense circumstellar shell with the mass of 0.01 Msun and radius of 5x10^{14} cm, as well as the external rarefied wind. Both circumstellar components are consistent with the early H-alpha broad wings caused by the Thomson scattering and the intrinsic column density provided by X-ray data. Based on the radiation hydrodynamics we, for the first time, simulate the SN 2023ixf phenomenon from the explosion to the emergence of the hard X-ray radiation.

</details>


### [256] [From One to Two: A Second Binary Millisecond Pulsar in the Globular Cluster M92 (NGC 6341)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15639)
*Dejiang Yin,Li-yun Zhang,Baoda Li,Yinfeng Dai,Lin Wang,Qiuyu Yu,Yujie Lian*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用500米口径球面射电望远镜（FAST）在球状星团M92中发现了第二颗毫秒脉冲双星PSR J1717+4308B（M92B），周期3.51 ms，并给出了相位连接的计时解。通过分析其负周期导数确认了星团成员身份，并基于两颗脉冲星的贝叶斯分析约束了M92的核心结构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球状星团M92中发现和认证新的毫秒脉冲星，以扩充该星团的脉冲星样本，并利用脉冲星计时技术约束星团的结构和动力学参数。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜进行频率域加速搜索发现脉冲星；通过相位连接得到计时解；利用负自旋周期导数确认星团成员身份；采用贝叶斯马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）分析方法结合两颗脉冲星的数据约束星团核心结构参数。

Result: 发现PSR J1717+4308B，自旋周期3.51 ms，DM=35.29 pc cm⁻³，轨道周期2.3天，偏心率约4.8×10⁻⁴，最小伴星质量0.2 M☉，自旋周期导数为负值；DM与M92A一致（差异<0.2 pc cm⁻³），确认双星均为M92成员；贝叶斯MCMC给出与N体动力学模型一致的星团核心参数约束。

Conclusion: 成功在M92中发现并认证了第二颗毫秒脉冲双星，证明了即使在脉冲星样本稀疏的情况下，脉冲星计时也能为球状星团核心结构提供有价值的动力学约束信息。

Abstract: We report the discovery and phase connected-timing solution of a second millisecond binary pulsar, PSR J1717+4308B (M92B), in the globular cluster M92 (NGC 6341) using the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope. This new pulsar, with a spin period of 3.51 ms and a dispersion measure (DM) of 35.29 pc cm$^{-3}$, was discovered through frequency-domain acceleration searches. The timing solution shows that M92B is in a binary system with an orbital period of 2.3 days, an eccentricity of $\simeq 4.8 \times 10^{-4}$, and a minimum companion mass of 0.2 $\,M_\odot$. M92B lies within the cluster core radius in projection, and its negative spin period derivative ($\dot{P}$) is consistent with acceleration in the cluster potential. The measured negative $\dot{P}$ of M92B, together with a DM consistent with that of M92A ($< 0.2\, \rm pc\,cm^{-3}$), confirms that both pulsars are members of the cluster. A Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis based on these two pulsars yields broad constraints on the core structural parameters of M92 that are consistent with $N$-body dynamical modeling. This demonstrates that pulsar timing can provide useful dynamical information in sparse pulsar samples.

</details>


### [257] [Mrk 382: A Narrow-line Seyfert 1 Galaxy with Recurrent X-ray State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15674)
*Yanli Ai,Wenfeng Wen,Liming Dou,Jiahua Wu,Chen Hu,Tinggui Wang,Xiaohui Yang,Jing Wang,Xue-Bing Wu,Qiusheng Gu,Xinwen Shu,Pudu,Jian-Min Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了近邻窄线Seyfert~1星系Mrk~382在约15年时间尺度上发生的重复性X射线状态转变。基于Swift、Chandra、XMM-Newton和eROSITA等多时期观测数据，发现其0.3–2 keV流量变化近一个数量级，X射线谱从陡峭的高态（Γ=2.32）转变为极硬的低态（Γ=1.39），且低态呈现强反射主导特征（反射分数从~4增至~34），紫外波段跟随X射线变化但幅度较小，光学及红外变化更温和。Mrk~382未进入极端X射线弱态，也未检测到明显的光学谱型变化，是研究活动星系核日冕几何变化和多波段变异的珍贵实验室。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Mrk~382的长期X射线状态转变及其多波段辐射关联，揭示活动星系核（AGN）中吸积流与日冕几何的动态演化机制。

Method: 利用Swift、Chandra、XMM-Newton、eROSITA的多历元X射线观测，结合紫外、光学、红外波段数据，通过能谱拟合和反射模型分析状态依赖的谱演化，并量化反射分数、光子指数等参数。

Result: 1) 0.3–2 keV流量变化近一个数量级，多次在亮态与暗态间转换；2) 高态X射线谱陡峭（Γ=2.32），低态谱极硬（Γ=1.39）且出现窄Fe Kα线（等值宽度~330 eV）；3) 低态反射分数从~4增至~34，表明反射主导与强光弯曲效应；4) 紫外辐射跟随X射线但幅度更小，光学/红外变化更温和；5) 未进入极端X射线弱态，未观测到光学谱型变化。

Conclusion: Mrk~382是罕见的经历重复性X射线状态转变的近邻Seyfert星系，为研究AGN日冕几何变化、反射主导状态以及多波段变异机制提供了重要的观测案例。

Abstract: We report recurrent X-ray state transitions in the nearby narrow-line Seyfert~1 galaxy Mrk~382 using multi-epoch observations from \textit{Swift}, \textit{Chandra}, \textit{XMM-Newton}, and eROSITA, together with archival ultraviolet, optical, and infrared data. The 0.3--2 keV flux varies by nearly an order of magnitude over the past $\sim15$ yr, with multiple transitions between bright and faint states. The source brightened by a factor of $\sim10$ between the 2010 \textit{Chandra} observation and the 2011 \textit{XMM-Newton} high state, then declined by $\sim6$--7 to a low state in 2019, followed by renewed brightening in recent \textit{Swift} monitoring. The X-ray spectrum shows strong state-dependent evolution, changing from a steep high-state continuum ($Γ=2.32\pm0.04$) to a much harder low-state spectrum ($Γ=1.39\pm0.06$). The low-state spectrum also exhibits a narrow Fe K$α$ line with an equivalent width of $\sim330$ eV. Reflection modeling indicates that the low-flux state is strongly reflection dominated, with the reflection fraction increasing from $R_{\rm refl}\sim4$ to $\sim34$, consistent with a compact corona subject to strong light-bending effects. The ultraviolet emission broadly follows the long-term X-ray variability but with smaller amplitude, while the optical and mid-infrared bands vary more mildly. Despite the dramatic X-ray variability, Mrk~382 does not enter an extreme X-ray-weak state, and we did not detect clear optical spectral-type changes based on the currently available observations. Mrk~382 is therefore a rare nearby Seyfert galaxy undergoing recurrent X-ray state transitions, providing a valuable laboratory for studying changing coronal geometry and multiwavelength AGN variability.

</details>


### [258] [Gamma-Ray Periodicity in Jetted AGN: Revisiting Periodicity Candidates with >17 years of Fermi-LAT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15676)
*P. Peñil,A. Rico,A. Domínguez,M. Ajello,S. Buson,S. Adhikari*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用超过17年的Fermi-LAT观测数据，重新分析了喷流活动星系核中先前报告的伽马射线周期候选体。结果表明，大多数候选周期因有限的时间覆盖和红噪声变异性而未被确认，仅8个源保持>2σ局部显著性，其中PG 1553+113和S5 1044+71最为显著（>3σ局部显著性，全局显著性约0σ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对先前文献中报告的伽马射线周期候选体进行稳健检验，区分其究竟是持续性周期行为、瞬态准周期振荡（QPOs）还是随机变异性产生的波动。

Method: 使用超过17年的Fermi-LAT数据，结合广义Lomb-Scargle周期图、相位离散最小化和奇异谱分析等多种计时方法，并利用简单/弯曲幂律功率谱密度及ARIMA/ARFIMA自回归模型生成的人工光变曲线来估计检验统计量。

Result: 大多数先前报告的周期候选体未得到确认；仅8个源保持>2σ局部显著性；PG 1553+113和S5 1044+71为最显著案例（>3σ局部显著性，全局显著性约0σ）；部分先前研究的预测与新观测一致。

Conclusion: 许多先前报告的周期可能源于有限的时间覆盖和红噪声变异性或瞬态QPO特征，而非持续性周期行为；PG 1553+113和S5 1044+71仍是最有希望的候选源，但需谨慎看待全局显著性。

Abstract: We reanalyze previously reported gamma-ray periodicity candidates in jetted active galactic nuclei using more than 17 years of Fermi-LAT observations. The updated data provide a robust test of whether earlier results correspond to persistent periodic behavior, to transient quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs), or to fluctuations produced by stochastic variability. We apply complementary timing methods, including the Generalized Lomb-Scargle periodogram, Phase Dispersion Minimization, and Singular Spectrum Analysis, and estimate the test statistics using artificial light curves generated from different variability models, including simple and bending power-law power spectral densities, as well as ARIMA and ARFIMA autoregressive models. We find that most previously proposed candidates in literature are not confirmed when the longer baseline is considered, indicating that many reported periods were likely driven by limited temporal coverage and red-noise variability or transient QPO-like features rather than persistent periodic behavior. Only eight sources retain hints of periodic behavior at the >2sigma local test statistics. Among them, PG 1553+113 and S5 1044+71 remain the most significant cases, with local test statistics above 3sigma, and with a global significance consistent with ~0sigma (because of the large trial factor). In addition, we assess predictions from previous studies as an independent test of the proposed periods and find that some are consistent with the new observations.

</details>


### [259] [A Universal Physics Defining the Radiation Spectra of Blazars and Gamma-Ray Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15759)
*Z. Lucas Uhm,Sang-Sung Lee,Bing Zhang,Judith Racusin,Bindu Rani,Myungshin Im,Aeree Chung,Hyung Mok Lee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文发现，尽管耀变体和伽马射线暴（GRBs）起源截然不同（前者由超大质量黑洞驱动，后者是大质量恒星死亡或致密天体合并的信号），但两者的辐射谱都遵循统一的物理机制——相对论性电子在快速冷却状态下、在递减磁场中的冷却物理学。这种冷却物理（而非加速机制）决定了光谱形状，可能解释了十多年前观测到的两类天体喷流能量学的统一标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耀变体和伽马射线暴这两类截然不同的高能天体物理现象是否共享统一的辐射物理机制，并解释它们之间观测到的喷流能量学标度关系。

Method: 采用简单玩具模型，假设一个光学薄的单区域且磁场递减，产生耀变体的对数抛物线谱；将该模型与之前对GRBs低能谱指数的研究对比，识别共同物理机制。

Result: 耀变体谱可用对数抛物线函数描述；递减磁场中快速冷却的电子物理能自然产生该谱形；该冷却机制同样解释了GRBs的低能谱指数；光谱形状由冷却物理而非加速机制决定。

Conclusion: 快速冷却物理是耀变体和GRBs辐射谱的统一物理起源，颠覆了传统认知，可能为广泛的天体物理学领域开辟新方向。

Abstract: Blazars and gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are both cosmic beacons of extreme energy release powered by relativistic jets. However, they originate from tremendously different environments. Blazars are the sustained powerhouses driven by supermassive black holes at galactic centers, whereas GRBs are the transient death signals of massive stars or merging compact objects. Here we show that, despite the enormous differences, a universal physics defines the radiation spectra of blazars and GRBs. The blazar spectrum is well described by a "log-parabola" function. Employing a simple toy model with a single optically-thin region of a decreasing magnetic field, we produce the log-parabola spectrum very naturally for blazars. We find that the blazar spectrum is shaped by the "cooling physics" of relativistic electrons in the fast-cooling regime, which we identify as the universal physics since we previously showed that the fast-cooling physics of electrons with a decreasing magnetic field also explains the mysterious low-energy spectral index of the gamma-ray spectrum for a majority of GRBs. This fast-cooling physics of electrons likely nails down the physical origin underlying the universal scaling of the jet energetics between blazars and GRBs, which was observationally suggested more than a decade ago. We highlight that the spectrum shaper in both blazars and GRBs is the cooling physics, not the acceleration mechanism. This finding is conventional-belief-defying and may open up new avenues in a wide range of astrophysics.

</details>


### [260] [Discovery of Unusual Jet Orientation Variations in the Microquasar GRS 1915+105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16228)
*Xi Yan,Lang Cui,Wu Jiang,Zhen Yan,Sándor Frey,Sergei Trushkin,Timur Mufakharov,Ruchika Dhaka,Shuangjing Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对微类星体GRS 1915+105在2025年巨射电耀发期间的三期东亚VLBI网（EAVN）6.7 GHz观测，发现其东南-西北方向喷流在日时间尺度上存在显著朝向变化，最小位置角为118°±7°，37天后增至152°±2°。自2023年以来喷流朝向在118°–188°范围内大幅变化，可能与当前X射线遮蔽状态下内吸积盘的翘曲进动有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示微类星体GRS 1915+105在X射线遮蔽状态下喷流朝向的短时尺度变化，验证是否由内吸积盘翘曲进动驱动。

Method: 利用东亚VLBI网（EAVN）在6.7 GHz频段进行三期高分辨率成像观测，结合RATAN-600监测程序筛选的2025年巨射电耀发事件，测量喷流位置角（PA）并分析其时间演化。

Result: 观测到喷流最小PA为118°±7°，37天后增至152°±2°；结合文献，自2023年以来PA变化范围达118°–188°；发现一奇异形态可能指示东南向喷流发生侧向展宽。

Conclusion: GRS 1915+105在X射线遮蔽状态下的喷流朝向在日尺度上发生显著变化，最可能的解释是内吸积盘翘曲进动，但需后续观测进一步验证。

Abstract: We report large day-timescale variations in the orientation of the southeast--northwest jet in the prototype microquasar GRS 1915+105. These results are based on three-epoch East Asia VLBI Network (EAVN) observations at 6.7 GHz, obtained during giant radio flares in 2025 detected by the RATAN-600 monitoring program. Our observations reveal the smallest position angle (PA) of $118^\circ \pm 7^\circ$ ever measured for the jet in GRS 1915+105, which increases to $152^\circ \pm 2^\circ$ within 37 days. Based on the literature results, we further suggest that the jet orientation has exhibited significant variations over a PA range of $118^\circ$--$188^\circ$ since 2023. This unusual jet orientation behavior in GRS 1915+105 during its current X-ray-obscured state may arise from a warped, precessing inner accretion disk, as implied by recent X-ray spectroscopy. Notably, one image reveals a peculiar morphology in GRS 1915+105, which likely indicates lateral spreading of the approaching southeast jet. Future observations are essential to clarify the issues raised in this work.

</details>


### [261] [Detection and luminosity-dependent evolution of the high-energy hump in the Be/X-ray pulsar 1A 1118-61](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16805)
*Alexander Salganik,Sergey S. Tsygankov,Sergey V. Molkov,Hua Xiao,QingChang Zhao,Long Ji,Alexander A. Mushtukov,Igor Yu. Lapshov,Alexander A. Lutovinov,Alexey Yu. Tkachenko,Hua Feng,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Xiao-Bo Li,Shu Zhang,Juri Poutanen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用SRG/ART-XC、Insight-HXMT和NuSTAR观测数据，追踪了Be/X射线脉冲星1A 1118-61在2026年 outburst 中的光度依赖谱演化。研究发现，随着源亮度下降，连续谱发展出明显的双峰结构（~10 keV和~30-40 keV），高能峰在L<sub>4-35</sub>≃(0.8-1.8)×10<sup>36</sup> erg s<sup>-1</sup>时变得清晰可辨，并在~55 keV处探测到回旋吸收线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在追踪1A 1118-61的光度依赖谱演化，并确定高能峰变得清晰可辨的光度范围。

Method: 使用SRG/ART-XC和Insight-HXMT密集监测，结合三次宽带NuSTAR观测，覆盖从峰值光度~7×10<sup>37</sup> erg s<sup>-1</sup>到低光度平台(3-8)×10<sup>35</sup> erg s<sup>-1</sup>的衰减过程，采用唯象双分量康普顿化模型描述连续谱。

Result: 源衰减时连续谱发展出双峰形态，峰值分别位于~10 keV和~30-40 keV；ART-XC监测将转变光度约束在L<sub>4-35</sup>≃(0.8-1.8)×10<sup>36</sup> erg s<sup>-1</sup>；两个连续谱峰的流量比在L<sub>4-35</sub>~10<sup>37</sup> erg s<sup>-1</sup>处存在间断；在高能峰中探测到~55 keV的回旋吸收线，其中心能量无显著光度依赖。

Conclusion: 在磁化吸积流中共振相互作用的框架下讨论了观测到的行为。

Abstract: Context. Accreting X-ray pulsars exhibit strong luminosity-dependent changes in their broad-band spectra. At high luminosities, their spectra are usually described by a power-law continuum with a high-energy cutoff, whereas low-luminosity observations have revealed a two-hump spectral morphology.
  Aims. We aim to trace the luminosity-dependent spectral evolution of the Be/X-ray pulsar 1A 1118-61 and to constrain the luminosity range over which the high-energy hump becomes clearly distinguishable.
  Methods. We use dense SRG/ART-XC and Insight-HXMT monitoring, together with three broad-band NuSTAR observations of 1A 1118-61 obtained during its 2026 outburst, to trace the luminosity-dependent evolution of the spectral shape. The ART-XC data follow the decay from a peak luminosity of $\simeq7\times10^{37}$ erg s$^{-1}$ to a low-luminosity plateau at $\simeq(3$-$8)\times10^{35}$ erg s$^{-1}$ in the 4-35 keV band, while the NuSTAR observations provide broad-band spectra during the bright phase, the decline, and the plateau. We describe the continuum with a phenomenological two-component Comptonization model.
  Results. As the source faded, the broad-band continuum developed a distinct high-energy hump, giving rise to a two-hump morphology with broad maxima near $\sim$10 keV and $\sim$30-40 keV. The ART-XC monitoring constrains the transition to this morphology to $L_{4-35}\simeq(0.8$-$1.8)\times10^{36}$ erg s$^{-1}$. We also find a break in the luminosity dependence of the flux ratio between the two continuum humps around $L_{4-35}\sim10^{37}$ erg s$^{-1}$. A cyclotron line at $\simeq55$ keV is detected in the high-energy hump, with no significant luminosity dependence of its centroid energy. We discuss this behavior in the context of resonant interactions in the magnetized accretion flow.

</details>


### [262] [From Morphology to Variability: Radiative Cooling Effects on Horizon-Scale Polarization in Two-Temperature GRMHD Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16818)
*Jane SiNan Long,Akhil Uniyal,Mingyuan Zhang,Yosuke Mizuno,Ziri Younsi,Christian M. Fromm,Kinwah Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过两温度GRMHD模拟，研究了辐射冷却对黑洞吸积流极化信号的影响，发现冷却会增强法拉第深度、增大环结构与极化模式的非轴对称性，并引起极化角的频繁反转。这些极化不对称性、时变性和径向结构可作为辐射冷却的可靠诊断特征，有望通过EHT等高分辨率观测加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用极化信号作为新的探针，研究辐射冷却对地平线尺度吸积流的影响，以量化诊断冷却如何改变极化辐射特性。

Method: 基于两温度GRMHD模拟，对比有/无辐射冷却的模型；分析极化形态、变率、法拉第深度、非轴对称模功率（β_m）、极化角（∠β_2）的径向分布等；额外测试非热电子分布对结果的影响。

Result: 冷却增强有效法拉第深度，导致大尺度法拉第加扰增强；冷却与非冷却模型的高阶光子退极化效应相当；冷却增大了环结构和极化模式的内在不对称性（非轴对称m≠2模相对β_2增强）；冷却引起极化角∠β_2更强的时变性及频繁符号反转；∠β_2径向剖面可区分法拉第旋转主导区和光子环贡献区，清晰分离冷却与非冷却情形；230 GHz极化结构对电子分布函数细节不敏感。

Conclusion: 地平线尺度的极化不对称性、时变性和径向结构编码了辐射冷却的稳健特征，时间分辨偏振测量和高分辨率成像（如EHT）对于约束黑洞吸积流中的辐射过程具有重要诊断价值。

Abstract: Polarization signatures provide a new window to investigate the effects of radiative cooling in the horizon-scale accretion flows. Morphology and variability of polarization offer quantifiable diagnostics of how cooling modifies the polarised emission from two-temperature GRMHD simulations. We find that cooling enhances the effective Faraday depth, leading to stronger large-scale Faraday scrambling, particularly at higher accretion rates. In contrast, depolarization associated with higher-order photons is comparable between cooling and non-cooling models. Radiative cooling also increases the intrinsic asymmetry in both the ring structure and the polarization pattern. This effect is quantified by enhanced power in non-axisymmetric azimuthal modes ($β_m$, $m \neq 2$) relative to the dominant quadrupolar component $β_2$. The increased asymmetry is directly linked to stronger temporal variability of the polarization angle $\angleβ_2$, including frequent sign reversals that are absent in non-cooling models. The radial profile of $\angle β_2$ further localizes the physical origin of these effects, distinguishing regions dominated by Faraday rotation from those influenced by photon ring contributions, and providing a clear separation between cooling and non-cooling cases. Additional tests including a non-thermal electron population indicate that the polarization structure at 230 GHz is largely insensitive to the detailed form of the electron distribution functions. Our results demonstrate that horizon-scale polarization asymmetry, variability, and radial structure encode robust signatures of radiative cooling. These findings highlight the diagnostic power of time-resolved polarimetry and high-resolution imaging for constraining radiative processes in black hole accretion flows with EHT-like observations.

</details>


### [263] [Curvature--Radiation Geometries Across the Second CHIME/FRB Fast Radio Burst Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17039)
*Thonimar V. Alencar,Jéferson A. S. Fortunato,Wiliam S. Hipólito-Ricaldi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用三种曲率辐射模板对CHIME/FRB第二次目录中的快速射电暴进行了群体级频谱分析。虽然三种模型的中位约化卡方值接近1，但仅15%-21%的源同时满足拟合优度和残差独立性标准，表明当前模板虽能捕捉主要频谱包络，但无法描述精细频谱结构。一维束模型和腔模型的相干尺度分别约为16-18 cm和25-28 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FRB频谱模型尚缺乏对大规模目录数据的系统性群体分析，需要评估不同曲率辐射模型在描述FRB频谱特性方面的适用性，并检验其是否能充分解释观测数据的精细结构。

Method: 使用点源、一维束和配对束腔三种曲率辐射模板，对CHIME/FRB第二次目录中的重复爆发和非重复爆发进行群体级频谱拟合。采用约化卡方、AIC/BIC和Ljung-Box残差自相关检验评估拟合质量。

Result: 三种模板的中位约化卡方值均接近1；重复爆发的卡方分布比非重复爆发更窄且存在统计显著但幅度较小的群体差异。AIC偏好一维束模型最多，BIC则更倾向于点源模型。但仅15%-21%的源同时满足拟合优度和残差独立性标准，残差自相关问题普遍存在。

Conclusion: 曲率辐射启发的几何模型能捕捉多数FRB的主要频谱包络，但需要额外的物理机制或频谱成分来描述数据的精细频谱结构。一维模型和腔模型推断的相干尺度分别为16-18 cm和25-28 cm。

Abstract: We present a population-level spectral analysis of fast radio bursts from the second CHIME/FRB catalog using three curvature-radiation-motivated templates: point-source, one-dimensional bunch, and paired-bunch cavity models. Fits are evaluated with reduced chi-squared $χ^2_r$, AIC/BIC, and the Ljung-Box residual autocorrelation test. All three templates yield median $χ^2_r$ values close to unity for both repeating and non-repeating bursts. Repeaters show narrower $χ^2_r$ distributions than non-repeaters, with statistically significant but modest population-level differences. AIC favours the one-dimensional bunch model for the largest fraction of sources, whereas BIC increases the relative preference for the simpler point-source model. However, residual autocorrelation remains widespread across all models: only 15%-21% of sources simultaneously satisfy goodness-of-fit and residual-independence criteria, indicating persistent structured residuals beyond the tested templates. These results suggest that while curvature-radiation-motivated geometries capture the dominant spectral envelope of many FRBs, additional physical ingredients or spectral components are required to describe the fine-scale spectral structure of the data. The inferred coherence scales are $\sim$16-18 cm for the one-dimensional model and $\sim$25-28 cm for the cavity model.

</details>


### [264] [Galaxy-cluster-stacked Fermi-LAT, part IV: $\sim70$ GeV WIMP annihilation lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17044)
*Uri Keshet*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对费米-LAT γ射线数据与eROSITA等星系团目录的交叉相关和叠加分析，发现了约70、40和13 GeV的三条发射线，最佳解释为一对质量分别为67.3 GeV和71.4 GeV的WIMP暗物质粒子通过双光子、γZ和γh等湮灭通道产生，显著性达5.3σ，且与银河系中心GeV excess在b\bar{b}连续谱上一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质间接探测主要依赖星系团的深度势阱约束速度依赖(p-波)湮灭，但信号较弱。本文旨在通过叠加大量星系团目录和与大规模结构示踪物交叉相关，从费米-LAT数据中提取更微弱的暗物质湮灭信号。

Method: 使用三种独立分析方法：1) 与eROSITA巡天图的交叉相关分析；2) 对MCXC、eROSITA和DESI目录中星系团的叠加分析，并将光子能量提升至星系团静止系；3) 复合匹配滤波器和轮廓似然估计。对高分辨率谱线进行多通道拟合。

Result: 在费米-LAT数据中发现三条发射线(~70、40、13 GeV)，对应χχ→γγ、γZ、γh湮灭通道。交叉相关分析全局显著性5.6σ，叠加分析2.3σ。进一步高分辨率谱分析发现六条线和一个宽三线特征，与两个交叉湮灭WIMP（质量67.3和71.4 GeV）的九个预期通道吻合，显著性5.3σ，截面约10^{-20}~10^{-19} cm^3/s。银河系中心GeV excess与b\bar{b}连续谱一致。

Conclusion: 这些谱线特征与一对质量约67-71 GeV的WIMP暗物质粒子的交叉湮灭预言高度吻合，可能是暗物质间接探测的重要证据。银河系中心GeV excess的连续谱成分也支持这一暗物质解释。

Abstract: The strongest constraints on the velocity-dependent ($p$-wave) annihilation of weakly interacting massive particle (WIMP) dark matter were derived from the deep potential wells of galaxy clusters. Even weaker signals can be extracted from sufficient aggregated clusters, by cross-correlating $γ$-rays with large-scale structure tracers or stacking over extensive cluster catalogs. Three independent such analyses show a similar triad of emission lines in Fermi-LAT data, around 70, 40, and 13 GeV, emerging from featureless spectra in wide-sky regions upon cross-correlation with eROSITA maps, and in stacked MCXC, eROSITA, and DESI catalog clusters once boosted to the cluster frame. These lines fit the anticipated $χχ\toγγ$, $γZ$, and $γh$ annihilation channels of a $\sim70$ GeV WIMP $χ$, detected by composite matched filters at trial-corrected global $Z$-scores reaching $5.6σ$ (cross-correlations) and $2.3σ$ (stacking), with intrinsic $\sim10^{[-20,-19]}$ cm$^3$ s$^{-1}$ channel cross-sections. High-resolution spectra establish six lines and a broad (three-line) feature in total, naturally aligned with the anticipated nine channels of two cross-annihilating WIMPs of masses $67.3_{-0.1}^{+0.1}$ and $71.4_{-0.1}^{+0.2}$ GeV (profile-likelihood bounds; $_{-5\%}^{+3\%}$ systematic; $5.3σ$). The Galactic-center GeV excess is broadly consistent with the corresponding $χχ\to b\bar{b}$ continuum.

</details>

### [265] [Coresets for Continuous $k$-Center in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16061)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对固定维度的双曲空间 $\mathbb H^D$ 中的连续 $k$-中心问题，构建了大小为 $(1/\varepsilon)^{O(kD)}$ 的核心集，该核心集大小与输入半径和点数 $n$ 无关，且能在 $O(nk(1/\varepsilon)^{O(kD)})$ 时间内构建，从而在固定 $D, k, \varepsilon$ 时实现线性时间常数规模核心集构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间中球的体积呈指数增长，这使得传统的基于网格的核心集构造方法无法获得与输入半径无关的核心集大小。本文旨在克服这一困难，构建大小独立于输入半径和 $n$ 的核心集。

Method: 根据最远优先尺度划分构造过程：在有限尺度下，使用庞加莱球模型中的局部欧几里得网格；在中间尺度，使用锚点中心的壳-锥分解结合双曲余弦定理的精确距离剖面；在大尺度，通过由每个壳-锥桶上诱导的 $k$-中心距离函数的粗略剖面索引的输入见证来避免离散化环境球。

Result: 核心集大小为 $(1/\varepsilon)^{O(kD)}$，构造时间为 $O(nk(1/\varepsilon)^{O(kD)})$，两者均与输入半径无关，核心集大小也与 $n$ 无关。

Conclusion: 对于固定维度 $D$、中心数 $k$ 和精度 $\varepsilon$，该方法提供了双曲空间中连续 $k$-中心问题的线性时间常数规模核心集构造算法。

Abstract: We construct coresets for the continuous $k$-center problem in fixed-dimensional hyperbolic space $\mathbb H^D$. The input is a set $P$ of $n$ points in $\mathbb H^D$, where $D=O(1)$, and the centers may be placed anywhere in the ambient hyperbolic space. Given $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, we construct a subset $P_\varepsilon\subseteq P$ such that every optimal continuous $k$-center solution for $P_\varepsilon$ is a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation for $P$.
  The main difficulty is the exponential volume growth of hyperbolic balls, which prevents a direct grid-based coreset from having size independent of the input radius. We overcome this by dividing the construction according to the farthest-first scale. At bounded scales, we use local Euclidean grids in the Poincaré ball model. At intermediate scales, we use an anchor-centered shell--cone decomposition together with exact distance profiles obtained from the hyperbolic law of cosines. At large scales, we avoid discretizing the ambient ball and instead keep input witnesses indexed by coarse profiles of the induced $k$-center distance functions on each shell--cone bucket.
  The resulting coreset has size $\left(1/\varepsilon\right)^{O(kD)}$ and can be constructed in time $O(nk\left(1/\varepsilon\right)^{O(kD)}).$ Both bounds are independent of the input radius, and the coreset size is also independent of $n$. Consequently, for fixed $D$, $k$, and $\varepsilon$, this gives a linear-time construction of a constant-size coreset for the continuous $k$-center problem in hyperbolic space.

</details>


### [266] [Polynomial-Time Riesz-Energy Subset Selection for Ordered Point Sets on Lines and $\ell_1$-Staircases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16946)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究一维固定基数最小Riesz s-能量子集问题，证明了Monge性质与子模性，将其转化为多项式时间可解的子模最小化问题，并给出了显式的最小S-T割算法，复杂度为O(k³(n-k)³)至O(k⁴(n-k)⁴)，附带Python开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一维固定基数下最小Riesz s-能量子集选择问题的计算复杂性，利用组合优化结构实现多项式时间求解。

Method: 证明一维Riesz交互的Monge不等式，从而在有限分配格上导出子模性；将问题归约为分配格上的子模最小化，进一步构造显式的最小S-T割网络，包含k(n-k)个阈值节点和O(k²(n-k)²)条边。

Result: 对于任意实数s>0，问题具有Monge性质和子模性；最小割算法的时间复杂度为O(k³(n-k)³)（基于O(NM)流界）或O(k⁴(n-k)⁴)（基于O(N²M)界）；算法可直接应用于ℓ₁阶梯上的子集选择，如二维Pareto前沿或天空线近似。

Conclusion: 一维Riesz s-能量最小子集问题可通过子模最小化或最小割在多项式时间内求解，并且附带开源Python实现，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional fixed-cardinality minimum Riesz $s$-energy subset problem with fixed exponent $s > 0$: given ordered real points $x_1 < x_2 < \cdots < x_n$, a positive parameter $s>0$, and a cardinality $k$, choose indices $1 \leq i_1 < \cdots < i_k \leq n$ minimizing $E_s(i_1,\ldots,i_k)=\sum_{1\leq p<q\leq k}(x_{i_q}-x_{i_p})^{-s}$. The paper proves a Monge property for the one-dimensional Riesz interaction. Encoding feasible subsets by increasing index vectors, this Monge inequality implies submodularity on a finite distributive lattice and gives polynomial-time solvability via submodular minimization over distributive lattices. The structural construction is valid for every real $s>0$; bit-complexity claims require the arithmetic assumptions stated in the complexity section. The same structure also yields an explicit minimum $S$--$T$ cut algorithm with $k(n-k)$ threshold variables and $O(k^2(n-k)^2)$ finite pairwise edges. The resulting graph has $N=k(n-k)$ nodes and $M=O(k^2(n-k)^2)$ arcs after an $O(k^2(n-k)^2)$ coefficient-construction step; an $O(NM)$ max-flow bound gives an $O(k^3(n-k)^3)$ min-cut step, while the conservative $O(N^2M)$ bound gives $O(k^4(n-k)^4)$. Due to isometry, the results apply directly to subset selection on $\ell_1$ staircases, such as choosing diverse and representative Pareto front or skyline approximations in two dimensions. An open-source Python implementation of the min-cut algorithm accompanies the reproducibility material.

</details>


### [267] [A constant-factor approximation of the Gromov-Hausdorff distance in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17051)
*Sushovan Majhi*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了欧几里得平面中有限点集之间Gromov-Hausdorff距离的多项式时间常数因子近似算法，通过双射瓶颈距离、脂肪-近共线二分法及多重性核等技术，解决了平面情形下长期悬而未决的近似问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Gromov-Hausdorff距离的近似计算中，此前仅在直线情形下存在固定欧几里得维度的多项式时间常数因子近似。Hall和Papadimitriou (2005) 在直线上给出了2-近似，并观察到三维中3-近似是NP难的，而平面情形一直悬而未决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 利用双射Gromov-Hausdorff距离 $d_{GH}^{bij}$ 作为引擎；(2) 提出"脂肪-近共线二分法"（fat-or-collinear dichotomy）：脂肪点集可通过单一刚体运动对齐，近共线点集则通过树状图进行聚类匹配，在平坦无尺度的过程中恢复相对取向和每节点的反射符号；(3) 将双射放松为对应关系以得到 $d_{GH}$ 本身，转化为簇内多重性核（within-cluster-multiplicity kernel）问题。

Result: 首次给出了欧几里得平面中有限点集之间 $d_{GH}$ 和 $d_{GH}^{bij}$ 的多项式时间常数因子近似算法。匹配的下界（维度下降、多重性间隙、每尺度作用的反射障碍）证明了每个成分的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文解决了平面情形下Gromov-Hausdorff距离近似计算这一开放问题，所提出的脂肪-近共线二分法框架及其下界分析为更广泛度量空间中的类似问题提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We give the first polynomial-time constant-factor approximation of the Gromov--Hausdorff distance $d_{GH}$ between finite point sets in the Euclidean plane; in fixed Euclidean dimension such an approximation was previously known only on the line (Majhi, Vitter, and Wenk, 2024). Its engine is the bijective (bottleneck) Gromov--Hausdorff distance $d_{GH}^{bij}$: for two equal-size sets the least additive distortion $\max_{i,j}|d_X(i,j) - d_Y(σi, σj)|$ of a bijection $σ$ equals $2\,d_{GH}^{bij}$, which we likewise approximate within an absolute constant. Approximating additive distortion goes back to Hall and Papadimitriou (2005), who gave a $2$-approximation on the line and observed approximation within $3$ to be NP-hard in dimension three; the planar case they left open is the one we settle. A fat-or-collinear dichotomy drives both bounds: a fat set is aligned by a single rigid motion, while a near-collinear set is split into clusters matched along their dendrogram in one flat, scale-free pass, with relative orientations and per-node reflection signs -- at every scale of the dendrogram -- recovered by global cuts. Relaxing bijections to correspondences yields $d_{GH}$ itself, which reduces to a lone within-cluster-multiplicity kernel -- the pairs an optimal correspondence collapses -- that the same theory closes. Matching lower bounds -- a dimension drop, a multiplicity gap, and a reflection barrier acting at every scale -- show each ingredient is necessary.

</details>
